# AUDIO-004 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Every audio field can be authored in the visual editor (music panel, per-scene sound effect and voice, `Auto (from voice)`), the user can see waveforms on My audio cards and listen to library sounds, a read-only audio timeline under the Player shows the mix the plan computed, and the plan's warnings appear next to the controls they are about. Bundled sounds reach the Player, the timeline and (for AUDIO-005) render readiness through one page-session bundled-audio store.

**Architecture:** All audio edits are pure `(draft) -> draft` functions over the existing visual draft (`src/web/audio/audioFields.ts`), so every edit stays one validated candidate Story. One page-session store (`src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts`) checks each bundled id once (fetch, `inspectAudio`, `decodeAudioPeaks`, the D-9 truncation rule), retries network failures, memoizes terminal failures and feeds the Player's source map (`bundledAudioPolicy: "explicit"`), the timeline and the field warnings. The editor never mixes anything: the timeline and the warnings read `compileAudioPlan`'s `tracks`, `issues`, `maxSimultaneousGain` and `musicEnvelopeAt` (AINV-4), compiled once more from the very inputs `StoryAudio` uses. Everything image-shaped and every Story without audio stays as it is on `main` (AINV-6).

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript (Node `--experimental-strip-types`), React 19, `@remotion/player` 4.0.529, `mediabunny` 1.56.1 (already installed), IndexedDB library from AUDIO-003, `node:test`, Playwright (bundled Chromium: it plays WAV but has no AAC/H.264; system Chrome for the browser-render config).

**Spec:** `docs/audio/AUDIO-004-editor-ui.md`, `docs/audio/README.md` (AINV-n, constants, D-n, S-n) and the implementation notes of `AUDIO-001`, `AUDIO-002`, `AUDIO-003` (executors read the spec and README first; where this plan and the spec differ, the **Rulings** section below is what the controller decided, and the code on `main` is authoritative for everything else).

**Prerequisite (already merged before this branch starts):** AUDIO-001, AUDIO-002 and AUDIO-003 (`main` at the merge of AUDIO-003, `72250b5`). AUDIO-005 is planned on top of this plan and consumes `src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts` exactly as written here. This plan never installs a package.

## Global Constraints

- TypeScript imports use explicit `.ts`/`.tsx` extensions; Node tests are `tests/*.test.mjs` with `node:test` + `node:assert/strict`, importing `.ts` directly (`node --experimental-strip-types --test <file>`). Node cannot import `.tsx`: logic lives in pure `.ts` modules (tested in Node, with injected fakes for browser APIs), components stay thin and are covered by source-contract tests and Chromium specs.
- Write the failing test first. After **every** task run: `npm test`, `npm run lint` (eslint + `tsc`; eslint reports 6 warnings that exist on `main` and 0 errors), `npm run web:build`, `npx playwright test --project=chromium` (Playwright serves the BUILT bundle: run `npm run web:build` before browser tests). Tasks that change `src/web/components/Preview.tsx` or the Player's inputs also run `npx playwright test --config=playwright.browser-render.config.mjs` (system Chrome). `npm run test:harness` and `npm run test:cli-e2e` are run once in the last task (nothing here touches `tests/harness/**` or the CLI). If, under the 12 parallel Chromium workers, exactly `yaml-persistence.spec.mjs › secondary tab suppresses persistence…` fails, re-run that spec alone: it is timing-sensitive and unrelated.
- AINV-6: a Story without audio behaves exactly as on `main`: no timeline element, no volume controls, no new request, the same Player inputs, the same draft keys. The only existing assertions this plan edits are the ones that name something it deliberately changes, each listed in the task that changes it: the `auto (from voice)` text in `tests/browser/visual-editor.spec.mjs` (Task 2) and the AUDIO-002 source contract that pins `"static-fallback"` in `tests/web-preview.test.mjs` (Task 5).
- The AUDIO-001 interim MP4 render block (`AUDIO_RENDER_BLOCK_MESSAGE`, the `renderStoryMediaOnWeb` guard with `bypassAudioBlockForTest`, App's `audioRenderBlock` and `data-audio-block`, the CLI refusal in `scripts/renderStory.ts`) and its tests stay untouched: AUDIO-005 removes them. No task here may weaken them.
- Constants already on `main` (never redefine): `DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME = 1`, `DEFAULT_MUSIC_DUCK = 0.35`, `MAX_FADE_SECONDS = 10`, `WAVEFORM_PEAKS_PER_SECOND = 50`, `MAX_WAVEFORM_PEAKS = 15_000` (`src/audio/constants.ts`); `AUDIO_TRUNCATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS = 0.25` (`src/localAssets/limits.ts`); `LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE = 50` (`src/web/assetLibrary/constants.ts`). New constant of this plan: `BUNDLED_AUDIO_RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [1000, 2000, 4000]` (`src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts`). The selects' library read has **no cap**.
- Copy, verbatim: `Music`, `Sound effect`, `Voice`, `None`, `Bundled`, `My audio`, `Volume`, `Ducking`, `Fade in`, `Fade out`, `Auto (from voice)`, `auto · 4.3 s` (a middle dot), `auto · voice not available`, `Play <label>` / `Stop <label>`, `Used by the music and 2 scenes.` (already implemented by AUDIO-003), `Voice is cut by 1.2 s.`, `Fades shortened to fit the Story.`, `Audio not available: render blocked.`, `The mix may clip (peak gain 1.4).`, `This music is too short to loop (under 1 s); it plays once.`, `Audio could not be loaded.`, `Retry loading <label>` (the button's accessible name; its text is `Retry`).
- Accessible names used by the specs (so executors do not rename them): selects are `combobox`es named exactly `Music`, `Sound effect`, `Voice`; sliders are named `Music volume`, `Music ducking`, `Sound effect volume`, `Voice volume`; number inputs `Fade in (seconds)` and `Fade out (seconds)`; the checkbox `Auto (from voice)`; the timeline is one `role="slider"` element with `data-audio-timeline`.
- All new controls live inside the existing authoring fieldset, so render/import locks disable them like every other authoring control. The audio timeline is outside it (it only seeks the Player; it changes nothing).
- Visual-draft rules from v0.2 apply: every edit produces a candidate Story validated by the schema and the browser policy; an invalid candidate stays a pending draft; the YAML editor needs no change.
- Dependencies: nothing is added. Never run anything against production or `*.netlify.app`; never kill processes by image name; no downloads or installs; commits end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Windows dev machine (PowerShell 5.1 for the user; Git Bash for agents); CI on ubuntu-latest, Node 22. On the Windows checkout the existing source files have CRLF line endings: edit them with the Edit tool (which keeps them) and never reformat or convert a whole file. New files may use LF. Snippets labelled `diff` below are the exact changes to an existing file: each `-` block is the file's current text (quoted from `main` or from an earlier task of this plan), each `+` block the new text; apply them with the Edit tool, without the `-`/`+` markers and without carriage returns.
- Never run `git` inside a scratch copy of the repository that was made by copying a worktree: its `.git` is a pointer file to the real repository. Use the repository you were given.

## Review Focus

1. **The Player sounding a bundled file nobody checked** (the static-file fallback, the first render before the store has answered, an undecodable or truncated file, a file that fails once and then recovers). Expected: a bundled sound is mounted only from the store's checked `blob:` URL; until then it is `pending` and plays nothing, after a terminal failure it is `unavailable`, the other sounds keep playing, and no second request for the file is made. Pinned in Task 4 (store transitions), Task 5 (source map, mounts, Chromium with routed bytes) and Task 7 (field text).
2. **An audio edit that leaves a Story invalid or loses a number**: `Voice -> None` on an `auto` scene (the schema rejects `auto` without a voice), unchecking `Auto (from voice)`, a value equal to its default written into the YAML, an out-of-range fade, a field the Story holds that the library no longer offers (a deleted local file, a bundled id without a file). Expected: one candidate that validates (or a pending draft with the field's error), minimal canonical YAML, and the select never showing a different sound than the Story has (AINV-5). Pinned in Tasks 1 and 2.
3. **Stale asynchronous results overwriting newer state**: an id dropped while its fetch or decode runs, a pending retry timer, a library read that finishes after the Story changed, a preview URL that arrives after Stop or after another Play, a waveform read after a library mutation. Expected: the late result is discarded and releases whatever it acquired. Pinned in Tasks 3, 4, 5 and 6 with deferred promises and fake timers.
4. **The timeline doing work per Player frame, or not following the page**: canvases redrawn on every `frameupdate`, lanes not resized with the page, the playhead not synced, keyboard seeking past the ends or skipping scenes. Expected: only the playhead moves while playing; lanes redraw on Story/plan/waveform/width change; arrows move between scene starts, `Home`/`End` to the ends. Pinned in Task 6.
5. **Warnings about a Story the user is not looking at, or in the wrong place**: a pending draft whose scenes differ from the Active Story's plan, `Voice is cut` on the wrong scene, a loading sound shown as an error, a failed bundled fetch with no way out. Expected: warnings come from the Active Story's plan only while no draft is pending, sit on the field they are about, a loading sound has no text, and `Audio could not be loaded.` has a working `Retry`. Pinned in Task 7.

## Rulings assumed by this plan (the controller's, 2026-10-14; each is repeated where it matters)

- **Execution order and the AUDIO-005 contract.** AUDIO-004 is implemented and merged BEFORE AUDIO-005. `src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts` exports exactly the spec's API: `requestBundledAudio(ids: Iterable<BundledAudio>)`, `retryBundledAudio(id)`, `subscribeBundledAudio(listener): () => void`, `getBundledAudioStates(): ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState>` (a new map object on every transition, the same object otherwise), `useBundledAudioStates()` (`useSyncExternalStore`), and the states `loading` / `retrying{attempt: 1|2|3}` / `ready{peaks, blob, url}` / `fetch-failed{message}` / `unusable{message}`. `peaks` is typed `{peaksPerSecond: number; peaks: Uint8Array}` (the shape of AUDIO-003's `WaveformRecord`). The factory `createBundledAudioStore(deps)` is what Node tests drive; the page uses the module-level functions. Extras AUDIO-005 may use: `audioSourceForBundledState`, `bundledAudioKey`, `parseBundledAudioKey`, `storyBundledAudio`, the message constants. App already holds `const bundledStates = useBundledAudioStates()` after Task 5.
- **Where the render-readiness line is drawn.** AUDIO-004 owns the store, the Player's explicit policy and the field warnings. Today **every Story with audio is already render-blocked** by the interim block, so the spec's sentences "Render MP4 becomes disabled with the readiness message as soon as the decode fails" and "Render becomes enabled without a reload after the retry" cannot be true or tested until AUDIO-005 removes that block; they belong to AUDIO-005 (its readiness messages read `useBundledAudioStates()` in App). This plan does NOT touch `audioRenderBlock`, the Render button's `disabled` expression, the render banner or `data-audio-block`, and adds no render-readiness text. One Chromium test asserts that `Render MP4` is still disabled for an audio Story (the interim block holds). The spec's "render lock disables every new control" is exercised with an asset import in flight, which sets the very same `authoringLocked` fieldset lock (a real render of an audio Story is impossible while the block exists).
- **The Player.** Task 5 switches the Player to `bundledAudioPolicy: "explicit"`, fed by the store; moves `audioSources` assembly to App (`buildAudioSourceMap(activeStory, localAssets.sources, (id) => audioSourceForBundledState(bundledStates.get(id)))`, passed to `Preview` as a required `audioSources` prop) and updates the AUDIO-002 source-contract test that pinned `"static-fallback"`. Remotion Studio keeps its `"static-fallback"` default (`ToraVideo`/`AudioSourcesProvider` are not touched).
- **No replacement dialog.** "Import matching file" with a different audio file stays refused with the mismatch text (AUDIO-003). The spec's UI section does not list an audio replacement dialog, so none is planned; the scene-field selects are how a user replaces a sound.
- **Usage counts.** The delete warning of a regular My audio card counts the **visual draft** (a pending draft is never under-counted, as for images). A **missing card** is derived from the Active Story, so its `Used by …` text is counted from that same Active Story usage (`activeStoryAudioUsage`, from the collector's `LocalAudioUsage`), never from the draft. Both are tested (Task 3, Node and Chromium).
- **"An open sound select adds the id the user previews" is not implemented.** The spec's UI has no bundled-sound preview control (previews exist on My audio cards only), and `requestBundledAudio` takes the whole wanted set (ids not in it are cancelled), so App is the single requester. A future bundled preview must add its id to App's set.
- **Warnings use the Active Story's plan and are hidden while a draft is pending.** The plan belongs to the Active Story; a pending draft may have moved or dropped scenes, so no field warning is shown until the draft is eligible again (the pending banner already explains the state).
- **Store details the spec leaves open.** An HTTP error status or a failing body read is as transient as a network error. A catalog entry with `file: null` goes straight to `unusable` (no `loading`, no fetch). An id that stops being requested loses its in-flight fetch, pending retry **and every non-terminal state** (`loading`, `retrying`, `fetch-failed`); `ready` and `unusable` are memoized for the page session. `retryBundledAudio` only acts on a `fetch-failed` id that is still requested. `ready.url` is created once per id and never revoked (six ids at most, page session).
- **My audio selects.** Bundled music ids have `file: null`: they are absent from the selects (the spec); the group lists music before effects when a music file is supplied. The My audio group lists **every** present row (no cap: the selects' read pages through the whole library, metadata only, in pages of `LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE`, so no valid sound is unselectable and the result does not depend on storage order), sorted by label (case-insensitive, accents distinct: one shared `Intl.Collator("en", {sensitivity: "accent"})`) then ref; rows that share a label are told apart by `(m:ss)` and then a short id; a read that is superseded or aborted is discarded. The sorted groups are memoized per select (not recomputed on every render). A value the Story holds that no group offers stays as one extra option (`Calm loop (not available)`, `Missing audio abcd…1234`).
- **`Auto (from voice)` memory.** The numeric duration a scene had before the box was checked is remembered in the draft scene (`lastDuration`, never part of the candidate Story). Opening the YAML editor rebuilds the draft from the Story, so after a round trip through YAML the memory is gone and unchecking uses "the resolved seconds rounded to 0.1 s" (the spec's fallback). `auto · X s` and that fallback both come from `sceneFrames`, the single rule `compileTimeline` uses (exported in Task 1).
- **Preview priming (WebKit/Safari).** `start` primes the shared `HTMLAudioElement` synchronously inside the click (a data-URI silent WAV, `play()` with its rejection caught) before awaiting the verified URL, then swaps the source and plays; a Stop or another Play during the acquire still wins (Node-tested with a fake element). The gates are Chromium-only (the repository's WebKit project runs one unrelated spec), so **WebKit/Safari playback is not exercised by any gate**; Task 8 records this.
- **The authoring lock stops a running preview.** App stops any preview when `authoringLocked` becomes true (an import in flight, a render, an open asset dialog), because the Stop button sits inside the disabled fieldset. Chromium-tested.
- **`Loading audio…`.** While the library read for the selects has not settled (a ready library whose first read is in flight), a held local ref's extra option reads `Loading audio…`, not `Missing audio …`; once settled and still absent it reads `Missing audio abcd…1234` (AINV-5 wording). A library that is still opening counts as not settled too, so a persisted local voice never flashes `Missing audio …` on page load.
- **Warnings are announced with their field.** Each warning block (music, sound effect, voice) is a `role="status"` region that stays mounted and is named by its select's `aria-describedby`; after `Retry` focus returns to the field's select. `Voice is cut by X s.` rounds the cut up, so it never reads `0.0 s`.
- **Clip message.** The peak gain is rounded **up** to one decimal, so a mix that clips never reads `1.0`: `1.4` stays `1.4`, a seam at `sqrt(2)` reads `1.5`.
- **Carry from AUDIO-003's review.** `useStoryAudioDurations` keeps its behaviour; its effect body becomes the pure, exported `startAudioDurationRead(store, refs, settle) => cancel` and is tested behaviourally (a refresh re-reads, a stale result is discarded, a rejection settles the empty map) in Task 5. The waveform read of the timeline follows the same contract (`startWaveformRead`, Task 6).
- **Test placement.** The spec lists `tests/web-editor.test.mjs (extend)` for the pure editor helpers; they live in `tests/audio-editor-fields.test.mjs` (as AUDIO-003 relocated its unit tests), and the bundled store, timeline and notices have their own files as the spec names them (`tests/bundled-audio-store.test.mjs`, `tests/audio-timeline.test.mjs`). The Chromium spec is one file, `tests/browser/audio-editor.spec.mjs`, that every UI task appends to.
- **The timeline sits inside `.preview-frame`**, under the Player, only for Stories with audio. It is one `role="slider"` element (`aria-label` = scene and time, `aria-valuenow` = frame) with `data-audio-tracks="<plan track keys>"` as a test hook.
- **Chromium limits.** Bundled Chromium plays WAV but not AAC or H.264: every spec here uses WAV fixtures and the bundled WAV effects. The specs run against the real Player (muted start, arming). A spec that needs "the Player sounded something" counts Web Audio starts and media-element plays after the user's unmute (`installStartCounter`, Task 5).
- **When the owner supplies the music files** (AUDIO-001 task 7) the Music select gains the Bundled music entries: the one assertion that lists the select's options (`None, Whoosh, Boom, Ding, Pop`, Task 2) must then be updated in that change.

### Task 1: Pure editor helpers (draft edits, select options, `auto` text, library rows)

Every audio edit of the visual editor is a pure function here, so each one is tested as "one candidate Story" without a DOM.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/story/timeline.ts` (export `sceneFrames`; its parameter type widens to `Pick<StoryScene, "duration" | "voice">`)
- Modify: `src/web/visualDraft.ts` (`lastDuration`, `replaceVisualScene`, `setVisualMusic`)
- Create: `src/web/audio/audioFields.ts`, `src/web/audio/libraryAudioOptions.ts`
- Test: `tests/audio-editor-fields.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (on `main`): `bundledAudioCatalog`, `isBundledAudio`, `AudioDurationMap`, `AudioRef`, `BundledAudio`, `BundledAudioEntry` (`src/audio/catalog.ts`); `DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME`, `DEFAULT_MUSIC_DUCK` (`src/audio/constants.ts`); `shortAssetId(ref)` (`src/localAssets/sources.ts`); `formatAudioDuration(seconds)` (`src/audio/formatDuration.ts`, `m:ss`); `isAudioPayload` (`src/localAssets/readiness.ts`); `parseLocalAssetRef` (`src/localAssets/refs.ts`); `store.getPayloadMeta(digest)`; `AudioClip`, `Music` (`src/story/schema.ts`); `listLocalAssetPage(store, "audio", cursor)` (`src/web/assetLibrary/library.ts`); `createMemoryAssetStore()` (`tests/helpers/memoryAssetStore.mjs`).
- Produces:
  - `src/story/timeline.ts`: `sceneFrames(scene: Pick<StoryScene, "duration" | "voice">, fps: number, audioDurations: AudioDurationMap): {durationInFrames: number; durationSource: "fixed" | "voice" | "fallback"}`.
  - `src/web/visualDraft.ts`: `VisualSceneDraft.lastDuration?: string`; `replaceVisualScene(draft, index, scene): VisualStoryDraft`; `setVisualMusic(draft, music: Music | undefined): VisualStoryDraft`.
  - `src/web/audio/audioFields.ts`: `NO_AUDIO_VALUE = ""`; `SceneAudioRole = "sfx" | "voice"`; `MUSIC_DEFAULTS`; `MusicNumberField`; `LibraryAudioOption = {ref: LocalAudioRef; label: string; durationSeconds?: number}` (the duration is read only for rows whose label another row shares; the library group is sorted with ONE shared `Intl.Collator("en", {sensitivity: "accent"})`, then by ref, and rows that share a label are told apart: `label (m:ss)`, and `label (m:ss) · abcd…1234` when that is still equal or no duration is known); `AudioSelectOption = {value: string; label: string}`; `AudioSelectGroups = {bundled; library; current: AudioSelectOption | null}`; `audioSelectOptions({current, library, catalog?}): AudioSelectGroups`; `setDraftMusicSource(draft, src: AudioRef | null)`; `setDraftMusicNumber(draft, field, value)`; `setSceneSfxSource(scene, src | null)`; `setSceneAudioVolume(scene, role, volume)`; `setSceneAuto(scene, auto, resolvedSeconds)`; `setSceneVoiceSource(scene, src | null, resolvedSeconds)`; `roundedSeconds(seconds): string`; `AutoSceneView = {seconds: number; source: "voice" | "fallback"}`; `autoSceneView(scene, audioDurations, fps): AutoSceneView`; `autoDurationText(view): string`.
  - `src/web/audio/libraryAudioOptions.ts`: `readLibraryAudioOptions(store, signal?): Promise<LibraryAudioOption[]>` (every present audio row, paged with the library's cursor until exhausted; no limit; rows whose label another row shares also get `durationSeconds`, one metadata read each).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/audio-editor-fields.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {bundledAudioCatalog} from "../src/audio/catalog.ts";
import {buildLocalAssetRef} from "../src/localAssets/refs.ts";
import {
  MUSIC_DEFAULTS,
  NO_AUDIO_VALUE,
  audioSelectOptions,
  autoDurationText,
  autoSceneView,
  setDraftMusicNumber,
  setDraftMusicSource,
  setSceneAudioVolume,
  setSceneAuto,
  setSceneSfxSource,
  setSceneVoiceSource,
} from "../src/web/audio/audioFields.ts";
import {readLibraryAudioOptions} from "../src/web/audio/libraryAudioOptions.ts";
import {LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/constants.ts";
import {
  evaluateVisualDraft,
  replaceVisualScene,
  storyToVisualDraft,
} from "../src/web/visualDraft.ts";
import {createMemoryAssetStore} from "./helpers/memoryAssetStore.mjs";

const digestOf = (n) => n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0");
const audioRef = (n) => buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digestOf(n));
const VOICE = audioRef(1);
const FPS = 30;
const baseScene = (extra = {}) => ({
  type: "dialogue",
  pose: "formal",
  background: "office",
  text: "Hello",
  duration: 3,
  ...extra,
});
const draftOf = (story) => storyToVisualDraft({title: "T", ...story});
const candidateOf = (draft) => {
  const result = evaluateVisualDraft(draft);

  assert.equal(result.kind, "eligible", JSON.stringify(result.errors));

  return result.story;
};

describe("audioSelectOptions", () => {
  const library = [
    {ref: audioRef(5), label: "Echo"},
    {ref: audioRef(3), label: "banana"},
    {ref: audioRef(2), label: "Apple"},
    {ref: audioRef(4), label: "Echo"},
  ];

  it("offers the four bundled effects and no music while the music files are not supplied", () => {
    const groups = audioSelectOptions({current: undefined, library: []});

    assert.deepEqual(
      groups.bundled.map((option) => option.value),
      ["whoosh", "boom", "ding", "pop"],
    );
    assert.equal(groups.bundled.some((option) => option.value.endsWith("-loop")), false);
    assert.equal(groups.current, null);
  });

  it("lists music before effects and leaves out every entry whose file is null", () => {
    const catalog = {
      ...bundledAudioCatalog,
      "calm-loop": {...bundledAudioCatalog["calm-loop"], file: "audio/music/calm-loop.mp3", durationSeconds: 60},
    };
    const groups = audioSelectOptions({current: undefined, library: [], catalog});

    assert.deepEqual(
      groups.bundled.map((option) => option.value),
      ["calm-loop", "whoosh", "boom", "ding", "pop"],
    );
    assert.equal(groups.bundled[0].label, "Calm loop");
  });

  it("sorts My audio by label (ignoring case), then by ref, whatever order the library returned", () => {
    const groups = audioSelectOptions({current: undefined, library});

    assert.deepEqual(
      groups.library.map((option) => option.value),
      [audioRef(2), audioRef(3), audioRef(4), audioRef(5)],
    );
  });

  it("labels that differ only in case tie, so the ref decides (accents stay distinct)", () => {
    const tied = [
      {ref: audioRef(9), label: "echo"},
      {ref: audioRef(8), label: "ECHO"},
      {ref: audioRef(7), label: "Echo"},
      {ref: audioRef(6), label: "Écho"},
      {ref: audioRef(5), label: "Zulu"},
    ];
    const values = audioSelectOptions({current: undefined, library: tied}).library.map((option) => option.value);

    // The three spellings of echo come out by ref (7, 8, 9), then the accented one, then Zulu.
    assert.deepEqual(values, [audioRef(7), audioRef(8), audioRef(9), audioRef(6), audioRef(5)]);
  });

  it("tells two library rows with the same label apart: the duration, then a short id when that is equal too", () => {
    const rows = [
      {ref: audioRef(1), label: "voice", durationSeconds: 2.2},
      {ref: audioRef(2), label: "voice", durationSeconds: 10},
      {ref: audioRef(3), label: "take", durationSeconds: 4},
      {ref: audioRef(4), label: "take", durationSeconds: 4.3},
      {ref: audioRef(5), label: "solo", durationSeconds: 7},
      {ref: audioRef(6), label: "mute"},
      {ref: audioRef(7), label: "mute"},
      {ref: audioRef(8), label: "Voice", durationSeconds: 3},
    ];
    const labels = audioSelectOptions({current: undefined, library: rows}).library.map((option) => option.label);

    assert.deepEqual(labels, [
      "mute · 0000…0006",
      "mute · 0000…0007",
      "solo",
      "take (0:04) · 0000…0003",
      "take (0:04) · 0000…0004",
      "voice (0:02)",
      "voice (0:10)",
      "Voice",
    ]);
  });

  it("keeps a value the Story holds selectable when no group offers it", () => {
    const missing = audioSelectOptions({current: audioRef(9), library});
    const noFile = audioSelectOptions({current: "calm-loop", library});

    assert.deepEqual(missing.current, {value: audioRef(9), label: "Missing audio 0000…0009"});
    assert.deepEqual(noFile.current, {value: "calm-loop", label: "Calm loop (not available)"});
  });

  it("does not repeat a value a group already offers", () => {
    assert.equal(audioSelectOptions({current: "ding", library}).current, null);
    assert.equal(audioSelectOptions({current: audioRef(3), library}).current, null);
  });

  it("the None option has the empty value", () => {
    assert.equal(NO_AUDIO_VALUE, "");
  });
});

describe("music fields", () => {
  const bare = draftOf({scenes: [baseScene()]});

  it("selecting a track creates music with only its src, and None removes it", () => {
    const withMusic = setDraftMusicSource(bare, "calm-loop");

    assert.deepEqual(withMusic.music, {src: "calm-loop"});
    assert.deepEqual(candidateOf(withMusic).music, {src: "calm-loop"});
    assert.equal("music" in setDraftMusicSource(withMusic, null), false);
    assert.equal("music" in candidateOf(setDraftMusicSource(withMusic, null)), false);
  });

  it("changing the track keeps the volume, fades and ducking", () => {
    const tuned = setDraftMusicNumber(setDraftMusicSource(bare, "calm-loop"), "volume", 0.6);

    assert.deepEqual(setDraftMusicSource(tuned, VOICE).music, {src: VOICE, volume: 0.6});
  });

  it("a value equal to its default is omitted, any other value is written", () => {
    let draft = setDraftMusicSource(bare, "calm-loop");

    draft = setDraftMusicNumber(draft, "volume", 0.6);
    draft = setDraftMusicNumber(draft, "duck", 0.5);
    draft = setDraftMusicNumber(draft, "fadeIn", 0.5);
    draft = setDraftMusicNumber(draft, "fadeOut", 1.5);
    assert.deepEqual(draft.music, {src: "calm-loop", volume: 0.6, duck: 0.5, fadeIn: 0.5, fadeOut: 1.5});

    for (const field of Object.keys(MUSIC_DEFAULTS)) {
      draft = setDraftMusicNumber(draft, field, MUSIC_DEFAULTS[field]);
    }

    assert.deepEqual(draft.music, {src: "calm-loop"});
    assert.deepEqual(MUSIC_DEFAULTS, {volume: 1, duck: 0.35, fadeIn: 0, fadeOut: 0});
  });

  it("changing a number without music changes nothing", () => {
    assert.equal(setDraftMusicNumber(bare, "volume", 0.2), bare);
  });

  it("an out-of-range fade is a schema-invalid pending candidate with the field's error", () => {
    const draft = setDraftMusicNumber(setDraftMusicSource(bare, "calm-loop"), "fadeIn", 11);
    const result = evaluateVisualDraft(draft);

    assert.equal(result.kind, "schema-invalid");
    assert.ok(result.errors["music.fadeIn"]);
  });

  it("a Story without audio keeps exactly the keys it had", () => {
    const untouched = setDraftMusicSource(bare, null);

    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(untouched), ["title", "scenes"]);
    assert.deepEqual(untouched.scenes, bare.scenes);
  });
});

describe("scene sound fields", () => {
  const scene = draftOf({scenes: [baseScene()]}).scenes[0];

  it("sets and removes the sound effect and keeps its volume across a change of sound", () => {
    const withSfx = setSceneSfxSource(scene, "ding");

    assert.deepEqual(withSfx.sfx, {src: "ding"});

    const louder = setSceneAudioVolume(withSfx, "sfx", 0.5);

    assert.deepEqual(setSceneSfxSource(louder, "boom").sfx, {src: "boom", volume: 0.5});
    assert.equal("sfx" in setSceneSfxSource(louder, null), false);
  });

  it("a volume equal to the default is omitted; without a clip nothing changes", () => {
    const withSfx = setSceneAudioVolume(setSceneSfxSource(scene, "ding"), "sfx", 0.5);

    assert.deepEqual(setSceneAudioVolume(withSfx, "sfx", 1).sfx, {src: "ding"});
    assert.equal(setSceneAudioVolume(scene, "voice", 0.5), scene);
  });

  it("the voice keeps its own volume when the sound changes", () => {
    const withVoice = setSceneAudioVolume(setSceneVoiceSource(scene, VOICE, 2.7), "voice", 0.8);

    assert.deepEqual(setSceneVoiceSource(withVoice, audioRef(2), 2.7).voice, {src: audioRef(2), volume: 0.8});
  });
});

describe("Auto (from voice)", () => {
  const withVoice = draftOf({scenes: [baseScene({voice: {src: VOICE}, duration: 4})]});
  const scene = withVoice.scenes[0];

  it("cannot be checked without a voice", () => {
    const silent = draftOf({scenes: [baseScene()]}).scenes[0];

    assert.equal(setSceneAuto(silent, true, 2.7), silent);
  });

  it("checking remembers the number and unchecking restores it", () => {
    const auto = setSceneAuto(scene, true, 2.7);

    assert.equal(auto.duration, "auto");
    assert.equal(auto.lastDuration, "4");
    assert.equal(candidateOf(replaceVisualScene(withVoice, 0, auto)).scenes[0].duration, "auto");

    const restored = setSceneAuto(auto, false, 2.7);

    assert.equal(restored.duration, "4");
    assert.equal("lastDuration" in restored, false);
    assert.equal(setSceneAuto(scene, false, 2.7), scene);
    assert.equal(setSceneAuto(auto, true, 2.7), auto);
  });

  it("unchecking a scene that came from YAML as auto restores the resolved seconds rounded to 0.1 s", () => {
    const fromYaml = draftOf({scenes: [baseScene({voice: {src: VOICE}, duration: "auto"})]}).scenes[0];

    assert.equal(setSceneAuto(fromYaml, false, 81 / FPS).duration, "2.7");
    assert.equal(setSceneAuto(fromYaml, false, 3).duration, "3");
    assert.equal(setSceneAuto(fromYaml, false, 4.33333).duration, "4.3");
  });

  it("an unusable remembered duration is ignored", () => {
    for (const lastDuration of ["", "  ", "abc", "0", "-1"]) {
      const auto = {...setSceneAuto(scene, true, 2.7), lastDuration};

      assert.equal(setSceneAuto(auto, false, 2.7).duration, "2.7", JSON.stringify(lastDuration));
    }
  });

  it("choosing Voice None on an auto scene is ONE valid edit that also restores the number", () => {
    const auto = setSceneAuto(scene, true, 2.7);
    const draft = replaceVisualScene(withVoice, 0, auto);
    const cleared = replaceVisualScene(draft, 0, setSceneVoiceSource(auto, null, 2.7));
    const story = candidateOf(cleared);

    assert.equal("voice" in story.scenes[0], false);
    assert.equal(story.scenes[0].duration, 4);
    assert.equal("lastDuration" in cleared.scenes[0], false);
  });

  it("Voice None on an auto scene read from YAML uses the resolved seconds rounded to 0.1 s", () => {
    const fromYaml = draftOf({scenes: [baseScene({voice: {src: VOICE}, duration: "auto"})]});
    const cleared = replaceVisualScene(fromYaml, 0, setSceneVoiceSource(fromYaml.scenes[0], null, 81 / FPS));
    const story = candidateOf(cleared);

    assert.equal(story.scenes[0].duration, 2.7);
    assert.equal("voice" in story.scenes[0], false);
  });

  it("Voice None on a numeric scene only removes the voice", () => {
    const cleared = setSceneVoiceSource(scene, null, 2.7);

    assert.equal(cleared.duration, "4");
    assert.equal("voice" in cleared, false);
  });

  it("the lastDuration memory never reaches the candidate Story", () => {
    const auto = setSceneAuto(scene, true, 2.7);
    const story = candidateOf(replaceVisualScene(withVoice, 0, auto));

    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(story.scenes[0]).sort(), ["background", "duration", "pose", "text", "type", "voice"]);
  });
});

describe("the text of an auto scene", () => {
  it("shows the timeline's seconds for a known voice and a hint for the fallback", () => {
    const known = autoSceneView({voice: {src: VOICE}}, {[VOICE]: 2.2}, FPS);
    const long = autoSceneView({voice: {src: VOICE}}, {[VOICE]: 3.8}, FPS);
    const unknown = autoSceneView({voice: {src: VOICE}}, {}, FPS);

    assert.deepEqual(known, {seconds: 81 / FPS, source: "voice"});
    assert.equal(autoDurationText(known), "auto · 2.7 s");
    assert.equal(autoDurationText(long), "auto · 4.3 s");
    assert.equal(autoDurationText(unknown), "auto · voice not available");
    assert.equal(unknown.seconds, 3);
  });
});

describe("readLibraryAudioOptions", () => {
  const seed = (store, n, row = {}) =>
    store.setRaw("assets", audioRef(n), {
      label: `Sound ${n}`,
      originalFilename: `sound-${n}.mp3`,
      createdAt: "2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z",
      ...row,
    });

  it("reads every present row across library pages and skips damaged rows", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const total = LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE * 2 + 3;

    for (let n = 1; n <= total; n += 1) {
      seed(store, n);
    }

    store.setRaw("assets", audioRef(total + 1), {label: 42});

    const options = await readLibraryAudioOptions(store);

    assert.equal(options.length, total);
    assert.deepEqual(options[0], {ref: audioRef(1), label: "Sound 1"});
    assert.equal(options.at(-1).ref, audioRef(total));
    assert.equal(options.some((option) => option.ref === audioRef(total + 1)), false);
  });

  it("reads the duration only for rows whose label another row shares", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const meta = (durationSeconds) => ({
      kind: "audio",
      mimeType: "audio/wav",
      byteSize: 10,
      container: "wave",
      codec: "pcm",
      durationSeconds,
      sampleRate: 48_000,
      channels: 2,
    });

    seed(store, 1, {label: "voice"});
    seed(store, 2, {label: "voice"});
    seed(store, 3, {label: "solo"});
    seed(store, 4, {label: "voice"});
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(1), meta(2.2));
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(2), meta(10));
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digestOf(3), meta(7));
    // Row 4 has no metadata: it simply has no duration.

    const rows = await readLibraryAudioOptions(store);

    assert.deepEqual(rows.map((row) => [row.label, row.durationSeconds]), [
      ["voice", 2.2],
      ["voice", 10],
      ["solo", undefined],
      ["voice", undefined],
    ]);
  });

  it("is empty for an empty library", async () => {
    assert.deepEqual(await readLibraryAudioOptions(createMemoryAssetStore()), []);
  });

  it("has no cap: 120 rows over three pages are all listed, and the select offers all of them sorted by label", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const total = 120;

    assert.ok(total > LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE * 2);

    // Labels are in a different order from the refs (the library's storage order).
    for (let n = 1; n <= total; n += 1) {
      seed(store, n, {label: `Sound ${String(total + 1 - n).padStart(3, "0")}`});
    }

    const rows = await readLibraryAudioOptions(store);

    assert.equal(rows.length, total);
    assert.equal(new Set(rows.map((row) => row.ref)).size, total);

    const groups = audioSelectOptions({current: audioRef(1), library: rows});

    assert.equal(groups.library.length, total);
    assert.equal(groups.current, null, "the last-stored ref is offered, not replaced by a Missing option");
    assert.deepEqual(
      groups.library.map((option) => option.label),
      Array.from({length: total}, (_, index) => `Sound ${String(index + 1).padStart(3, "0")}`),
    );
  });

  it("stops reading when aborted", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const controller = new AbortController();

    seed(store, 1);
    controller.abort();
    await assert.rejects(readLibraryAudioOptions(store, controller.signal), {name: "AbortError"});
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create the helper modules**

Create `src/web/audio/audioFields.ts`:

```ts
import {
  bundledAudioCatalog,
  isBundledAudio,
  type AudioDurationMap,
  type AudioRef,
  type BundledAudio,
  type BundledAudioEntry,
} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import {
  DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME,
  DEFAULT_MUSIC_DUCK,
} from "../../audio/constants.ts";
import type {LocalAudioRef} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";
import {formatAudioDuration} from "../../audio/formatDuration.ts";
import {shortAssetId} from "../../localAssets/sources.ts";
import type {AudioClip, Music} from "../../story/schema.ts";
import {sceneFrames} from "../../story/timeline.ts";
import {
  setVisualMusic,
  type VisualSceneDraft,
  type VisualStoryDraft,
} from "../visualDraft.ts";

/** The value of the `None` option of every sound select. */
export const NO_AUDIO_VALUE = "";

export type SceneAudioRole = "sfx" | "voice";

/** The defaults the Story omits (canonical YAML stays minimal). */
export const MUSIC_DEFAULTS = {
  volume: DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME,
  duck: DEFAULT_MUSIC_DUCK,
  fadeIn: 0,
  fadeOut: 0,
} as const;

export type MusicNumberField = keyof typeof MUSIC_DEFAULTS;

/**
 * A My audio row as a select option: the label the user gave it. The duration
 * is only read for rows whose label another row shares (to tell them apart).
 */
export type LibraryAudioOption = {
  ref: LocalAudioRef;
  label: string;
  durationSeconds?: number;
};

export type AudioSelectOption = {value: string; label: string};

export type AudioSelectGroups = {
  /** Catalog entries with a file, music first. */
  bundled: AudioSelectOption[];
  /** Library rows by label. */
  library: AudioSelectOption[];
  /** The Story's own value when neither group offers it (a missing local file, a bundled id without a file). */
  current: AudioSelectOption | null;
};

/** One collator for every sort: case-insensitive, accents distinct (building one per comparison is slow). */
const labelCollator = new Intl.Collator("en", {sensitivity: "accent"});

const compareLibraryAudio = (a: LibraryAudioOption, b: LibraryAudioOption): number =>
  labelCollator.compare(a.label, b.label) || (a.ref < b.ref ? -1 : a.ref > b.ref ? 1 : 0);

/**
 * The text of every library row. Labels are not unique (a different file with
 * the same name imports under the same default label), so rows that share a
 * label get ` (m:ss)` appended, and rows still equal after that a short id
 * ` · abcd…1234`. Rows with a unique label are shown as they are.
 */
const libraryOptionTexts = (rows: readonly LibraryAudioOption[]): string[] => {
  const count = (values: readonly string[]) => {
    const counts = new Map<string, number>();

    for (const value of values) {
      counts.set(value, (counts.get(value) ?? 0) + 1);
    }

    return counts;
  };
  const labelCounts = count(rows.map((row) => row.label));
  const withDuration = rows.map((row) =>
    (labelCounts.get(row.label) ?? 0) > 1 && row.durationSeconds !== undefined
      ? `${row.label} (${formatAudioDuration(row.durationSeconds)})`
      : row.label,
  );
  const textCounts = count(withDuration);

  return rows.map((row, index) =>
    (labelCounts.get(row.label) ?? 0) > 1 && (textCounts.get(withDuration[index]) ?? 0) > 1
      ? `${withDuration[index]} · ${shortAssetId(row.ref)}`
      : withDuration[index],
  );
};

const unavailableLabel = (value: string): string =>
  isBundledAudio(value)
    ? `${bundledAudioCatalog[value].label} (not available)`
    : `Missing audio ${shortAssetId(value as LocalAudioRef)}`;

/**
 * The options of one sound select. Bundled entries with `file: null` are
 * absent (they cannot play); the group lists music before effects, otherwise in
 * catalog order. Library rows are sorted by label (case-insensitive, accents
 * distinct), then by ref, and rows that share a label are told apart (see
 * `libraryOptionTexts`). A value the
 * Story already holds that is in neither group stays selectable, so the select
 * never shows a different sound than the Story has (AINV-5).
 */
export const audioSelectOptions = (input: {
  current: string | undefined;
  library: readonly LibraryAudioOption[];
  catalog?: Readonly<Record<BundledAudio, BundledAudioEntry>>;
}): AudioSelectGroups => {
  const entries = Object.values(input.catalog ?? bundledAudioCatalog).filter(
    (entry) => entry.file !== null,
  );
  const bundled = [
    ...entries.filter((entry) => entry.kind === "music"),
    ...entries.filter((entry) => entry.kind !== "music"),
  ].map((entry) => ({value: entry.id as string, label: entry.label}));
  const sorted = [...input.library].sort(compareLibraryAudio);
  const texts = libraryOptionTexts(sorted);
  const library = sorted.map((row, index) => ({
    value: row.ref as string,
    label: texts[index],
  }));
  const offered = new Set([...bundled, ...library].map((option) => option.value));
  const current =
    input.current === undefined || offered.has(input.current)
      ? null
      : {value: input.current, label: unavailableLabel(input.current)};

  return {bundled, library, current};
};

const withoutKey = <T extends object, K extends keyof T>(
  value: T,
  key: K,
): Omit<T, K> => {
  const copy = {...value};

  delete copy[key];

  return copy;
};

/** Selects (or, with `null`, removes) the Story's music; the other music settings are kept. */
export const setDraftMusicSource = (
  draft: VisualStoryDraft,
  src: AudioRef | null,
): VisualStoryDraft =>
  setVisualMusic(
    draft,
    src === null ? undefined : ({...(draft.music ?? {}), src} as Music),
  );

/** Writes one music number; a value equal to its default is omitted. No music: unchanged. */
export const setDraftMusicNumber = (
  draft: VisualStoryDraft,
  field: MusicNumberField,
  value: number,
): VisualStoryDraft => {
  if (draft.music === undefined) {
    return draft;
  }

  return setVisualMusic(
    draft,
    value === MUSIC_DEFAULTS[field]
      ? (withoutKey(draft.music, field) as Music)
      : {...draft.music, [field]: value},
  );
};

/** Selects (or removes) a scene's sound effect; its volume is kept. */
export const setSceneSfxSource = (
  scene: VisualSceneDraft,
  src: AudioRef | null,
): VisualSceneDraft =>
  src === null
    ? (withoutKey(scene, "sfx") as VisualSceneDraft)
    : {...scene, sfx: {...(scene.sfx ?? {}), src} as AudioClip};

/** Writes the volume of a scene's sfx or voice; the default is omitted. No clip: unchanged. */
export const setSceneAudioVolume = (
  scene: VisualSceneDraft,
  role: SceneAudioRole,
  volume: number,
): VisualSceneDraft => {
  const clip = scene[role];

  if (clip === undefined) {
    return scene;
  }

  return {
    ...scene,
    [role]:
      volume === DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME
        ? withoutKey(clip, "volume")
        : {...clip, volume},
  };
};

const isAuto = (scene: VisualSceneDraft): boolean =>
  scene.duration.trim() === "auto";

/** The remembered numeric duration, when it is still a usable one. */
const rememberedDuration = (scene: VisualSceneDraft): string | null => {
  const seconds = Number(scene.lastDuration);

  return scene.lastDuration !== undefined &&
    scene.lastDuration.trim() !== "" &&
    Number.isFinite(seconds) &&
    seconds > 0
    ? scene.lastDuration
    : null;
};

/** Seconds rounded to 0.1 s, as the text of a duration input. */
export const roundedSeconds = (seconds: number): string =>
  String(Math.max(0.1, Math.round(seconds * 10) / 10));

/** The duration a scene gets back when it stops being `auto`. */
const restoredDuration = (
  scene: VisualSceneDraft,
  resolvedSeconds: number,
): string => rememberedDuration(scene) ?? roundedSeconds(resolvedSeconds);

const withoutAutoMemory = (scene: VisualSceneDraft): VisualSceneDraft =>
  withoutKey(scene, "lastDuration") as VisualSceneDraft;

/**
 * Checks or unchecks `Auto (from voice)`. Checking needs a voice and remembers
 * the numeric duration; unchecking restores it (or `resolvedSeconds` rounded to
 * 0.1 s when none was remembered, for example a Story read from YAML).
 */
export const setSceneAuto = (
  scene: VisualSceneDraft,
  auto: boolean,
  resolvedSeconds: number,
): VisualSceneDraft => {
  if (auto) {
    return scene.voice === undefined || isAuto(scene)
      ? scene
      : {...scene, duration: "auto", lastDuration: scene.duration};
  }

  return isAuto(scene)
    ? {
        ...withoutAutoMemory(scene),
        duration: restoredDuration(scene, resolvedSeconds),
      }
    : scene;
};

/**
 * Selects, replaces or (with `null`) removes a scene's voice. Removing the
 * voice of an `auto` scene is ONE edit: the same candidate also gives the scene
 * its numeric duration back, so it validates and no pending draft appears.
 */
export const setSceneVoiceSource = (
  scene: VisualSceneDraft,
  src: AudioRef | null,
  resolvedSeconds: number,
): VisualSceneDraft => {
  if (src !== null) {
    return {...scene, voice: {...(scene.voice ?? {}), src} as AudioClip};
  }

  const withoutVoice = withoutKey(scene, "voice") as VisualSceneDraft;

  return isAuto(scene)
    ? {
        ...withoutAutoMemory(withoutVoice),
        duration: restoredDuration(scene, resolvedSeconds),
      }
    : withoutVoice;
};

export type AutoSceneView = {
  seconds: number;
  source: "voice" | "fallback";
};

/**
 * What an `auto` scene resolves to, through the very rule `compileTimeline`
 * uses (`sceneFrames`), so the text can never disagree with the Player.
 */
export const autoSceneView = (
  scene: Pick<VisualSceneDraft, "voice">,
  audioDurations: AudioDurationMap,
  fps: number,
): AutoSceneView => {
  const {durationInFrames, durationSource} = sceneFrames(
    {duration: "auto", voice: scene.voice},
    fps,
    audioDurations,
  );

  return {
    seconds: durationInFrames / fps,
    source: durationSource === "voice" ? "voice" : "fallback",
  };
};

/** `auto · 4.3 s`, or `auto · voice not available` for a scene on the fallback length. */
export const autoDurationText = (view: AutoSceneView): string =>
  view.source === "voice"
    ? `auto · ${view.seconds.toFixed(1)} s`
    : "auto · voice not available";
```

Create `src/web/audio/libraryAudioOptions.ts`:

```ts
import {isAudioPayload} from "../../localAssets/readiness.ts";
import {parseLocalAssetRef, type LocalAudioRef} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";
import {listLocalAssetPage} from "../assetLibrary/library.ts";
import type {AssetLibraryStore, AssetPageCursor} from "../assetLibrary/store.ts";
import type {LibraryAudioOption} from "./audioFields.ts";

/**
 * EVERY usable My audio row (a present row, never a damaged one), however many
 * there are: the library is read page by page with its own cursor (pages of
 * `LOCAL_ASSET_PAGE_SIZE`, metadata only, no bytes) until it is exhausted, so no
 * valid sound is ever unselectable and the result does not depend on storage
 * order. Order is the caller's concern (`audioSelectOptions` sorts by label).
 * Rows whose label another row shares also get their duration (one metadata
 * read each, no bytes), so the selects can tell them apart. Aborting `signal`
 * stops the read at the next page.
 */
export const readLibraryAudioOptions = async (
  store: Pick<AssetLibraryStore, "listAssets" | "getPayloadMeta">,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<LibraryAudioOption[]> => {
  const options: LibraryAudioOption[] = [];
  let cursor: AssetPageCursor = {};

  for (;;) {
    signal?.throwIfAborted();

    const page = await listLocalAssetPage(store as AssetLibraryStore, "audio", cursor);

    for (const entry of page.entries) {
      if (entry.row.status === "present") {
        options.push({
          ref: entry.ref as LocalAudioRef,
          label: entry.row.value.label,
        });
      }
    }

    const last = page.entries.at(-1);

    if (!page.hasNext || last === undefined) {
      break;
    }

    cursor = {after: last.ref};
  }

  const labelCounts = new Map<string, number>();

  for (const option of options) {
    labelCounts.set(option.label, (labelCounts.get(option.label) ?? 0) + 1);
  }

  for (const option of options) {
    const parsed = parseLocalAssetRef(option.ref);

    if ((labelCounts.get(option.label) ?? 0) > 1 && parsed !== null) {
      signal?.throwIfAborted();

      const meta = await store.getPayloadMeta(parsed.digest);

      if (meta.status === "present" && isAudioPayload(meta.value)) {
        option.durationSeconds = meta.value.durationSeconds;
      }
    }
  }

  return options;
};
```

### Task 2: Music panel, per-scene sound fields and `Auto (from voice)` in the visual editor

**Files:**
- Create: `src/web/audio/useLibraryAudioOptions.ts`, `src/web/components/AudioControls.tsx`, `src/web/components/MusicPanel.tsx`, `src/web/components/SceneAudioFields.tsx`
- Modify: `src/web/audio/audioFields.ts` (the select says `Loading audio…` until the library read settles), `src/web/components/VisualEditor.tsx`, `src/web/App.tsx`, `src/web/styles.css`
- Test (extend): `tests/audio-editor-fields.test.mjs`
- Modify (deliberately): `tests/browser/visual-editor.spec.mjs` (the interim `auto (from voice)` text is replaced by the resolved seconds)
- Test: `tests/browser/audio-editor.spec.mjs` (created here, appended to by Tasks 3, 5, 6 and 7)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 1): everything `audioFields.ts` produces, `readLibraryAudioOptions`, `replaceVisualScene`; `AssetLibraryContext` (`status`, `refreshToken`); `visualEditorOptions` etc. unchanged.
- Produces:
  - `src/web/audio/audioFields.ts` gains `LibraryAudioChoices = {rows: readonly LibraryAudioOption[]; settled: boolean}`, `LOADING_AUDIO_LABEL = "Loading audio…"` and an optional `librarySettled?: boolean` (default `true`) on `audioSelectOptions`: while it is `false`, a local ref the Story holds that no group offers reads `Loading audio…` instead of `Missing audio abcd…1234` (the library read may not have reached it yet). Bundled ids without a file keep `… (not available)`.
  - `useLibraryAudioOptions(): LibraryAudioChoices` (re-read when the library changes; the returned object is stable while neither the rows nor `settled` change). `libraryAudioChoices(status, read)` is the pure rule behind it: **not settled** while the library is opening (`OPENING_ASSET_LIBRARY`) and from the moment a ready library appears until its first read finishes; settled for a finished read and for a library that is unavailable for good (no rows). So a persisted local voice reads `Loading audio…` on page load, never `Missing audio …` first.
  - `AudioSelect` is memoized: it builds its option groups with `useMemo` on (value, rows, settled) and renders them through a memoized `SelectOptions` element, so a re-render of the editor (a caption keystroke) neither sorts nor diffs a large library again. It takes optional `id` (the `<select>`'s DOM id) and `describedBy`.
  - `AudioSelect`, `UnitSlider`, `NumberField` (`AudioControls.tsx`; `AudioSelect`'s `library` prop is a `LibraryAudioChoices`); `MusicPanel` (props `music, library, errors, onSourceChange, onNumberChange`); `SceneAudioFields` (props `scene, library, onSfxChange, onVoiceChange, onVolumeChange`).
  - `VisualEditor` props gain `audioDurations: AudioDurationMap` and `fps: number` (App passes the page's one duration map and the Player config's fps).

Create `tests/browser/audio-editor.spec.mjs` (it includes a test that a voice chosen through the UI, with a non-default `Voice volume`, is written to the exported YAML):

```js
import {expect, test} from "@playwright/test";
import {fixtureBytes} from "../helpers/audioBytes.mjs";
import {yamlSource} from "./helpers/localAssetFlows.mjs";
import {applyYaml, waitForOwner} from "./helpers/localAssetPage.mjs";

// The built bundle is served; the Player is the real one (muted start, arming).
test.use({viewport: {width: 1400, height: 1500}});

const SECTION = "[data-my-audio]";
const music = (page) => page.getByRole("combobox", {name: "Music", exact: true});
const sfx = (page) => page.getByRole("combobox", {name: "Sound effect", exact: true});
const voice = (page) => page.getByRole("combobox", {name: "Voice", exact: true});
const autoBox = (page) => page.getByLabel("Auto (from voice)");
const importInput = (page) => page.locator(`${SECTION} .my-assets-toolbar input[type="file"]`);
const upload = (name) => ({name, mimeType: "application/octet-stream", buffer: Buffer.from(fixtureBytes(name))});
const appShell = (page) => page.locator(".app-shell");

const openApp = async (page) => {
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
};

/** Imports the 2.2 s voice fixture and waits until its card is listed. */
const importVoiceFixture = async (page) => {
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-440-2.2s.wav"));
  await expect(page.locator(`${SECTION} [data-local-asset-card]`)).toHaveCount(1);
};

const twoSceneYaml = [
  "title: Audio fields",
  "scenes:",
  "  - type: intro",
  "    pose: formal",
  "    background: office",
  "    text: One",
  "    duration: 3",
  "  - type: dialogue",
  "    pose: formal",
  "    background: office",
  "    text: Two",
  "    duration: 4",
  "",
].join("\n");

test("the Music panel writes minimal canonical YAML and None removes the music", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);

  // Bundled effects are offered; the music files are not supplied yet, so no music entry exists.
  await expect(music(page).locator("option")).toHaveText(["None", "Whoosh", "Boom", "Ding", "Pop"]);
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Music volume")).toHaveCount(0);

  await music(page).selectOption("ding");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Music volume")).toHaveValue("1");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Music ducking")).toHaveValue("0.35");
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "false");
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toContain("music:\n  src: ding\nscenes:");

  await page.getByLabel("Music volume").fill("0.6");
  await page.getByLabel("Music ducking").fill("0.5");
  await page.getByLabel("Fade in (seconds)").fill("0.5");
  await page.getByLabel("Fade out (seconds)").fill("1.5");
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "false");
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toContain(
    "music:\n  src: ding\n  volume: 0.6\n  fadeIn: 0.5\n  fadeOut: 1.5\n  duck: 0.5\n",
  );

  // Values equal to their defaults leave the Story again.
  await page.getByLabel("Music volume").fill("1");
  await page.getByLabel("Music ducking").fill("0.35");
  await page.getByLabel("Fade in (seconds)").fill("0");
  await page.getByLabel("Fade out (seconds)").fill("0");
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toContain("music:\n  src: ding\nscenes:");

  await music(page).selectOption("");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Music volume")).toHaveCount(0);
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).not.toContain("music:");
});

test("an out-of-range fade keeps a pending draft with its error until it is fixed", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await music(page).selectOption("ding");
  await page.getByLabel("Fade in (seconds)").fill("11");
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "true");
  await expect(page.locator(".music-panel .field-error")).toHaveCount(1);
  await page.getByLabel("Fade in (seconds)").fill("2");
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "false");
  await expect(page.locator(".music-panel .field-error")).toHaveCount(0);
});

test("a scene's Sound effect and Voice selects write the Story and keep a volume across a change of sound", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await applyYaml(page, twoSceneYaml);
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();

  await sfx(page).selectOption("ding");
  await page.getByLabel("Sound effect volume").fill("0.8");
  await sfx(page).selectOption("boom");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Sound effect volume")).toHaveValue("0.8");
  await page.locator(".scene-list-item").nth(1).click();
  await expect(sfx(page)).toHaveValue("");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Sound effect volume")).toHaveCount(0);

  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toContain(
    "    text: One\n    sfx:\n      src: boom\n      volume: 0.8\n    duration: 3\n",
  );

  await page.locator(".scene-list-item").nth(0).click();
  await sfx(page).selectOption("");
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).not.toContain("sfx:");
});

test("Auto (from voice) needs a voice, shows the resolved seconds and Voice None gives the number back in one edit", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await applyYaml(page, twoSceneYaml);
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await importVoiceFixture(page);

  await expect(autoBox(page)).toBeDisabled();
  await expect(autoBox(page)).not.toBeChecked();

  await voice(page).selectOption({label: "tone-440-2.2s"});
  await expect(autoBox(page)).toBeEnabled();
  await autoBox(page).check();

  const duration = page.locator("label.field").filter({hasText: "Duration (seconds)"});

  await expect(duration.locator("output")).toHaveText("auto · 2.7 s");
  await expect(page.getByRole("spinbutton")).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "false");

  // Unchecking restores the number the scene had (3 s).
  await autoBox(page).uncheck();
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Duration (seconds)")).toHaveValue("3");
  await autoBox(page).check();
  await expect(duration.locator("output")).toHaveText("auto · 2.7 s");

  // Voice None on an auto scene: one valid edit, no pending draft, the number is back.
  await voice(page).selectOption("");
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "false");
  await expect(autoBox(page)).toBeDisabled();
  await expect(autoBox(page)).not.toBeChecked();
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Duration (seconds)")).toHaveValue("3");

  const source = await yamlSource(page);

  expect(source).toContain("duration: 3");
  expect(source).not.toContain("voice:");
  expect(source).not.toContain("duration: auto");
});

test("Auto (from voice) is written as duration: auto, and after a round trip through YAML unchecking uses the resolved seconds", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await applyYaml(page, twoSceneYaml);
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await importVoiceFixture(page);
  await voice(page).selectOption({label: "tone-440-2.2s"});
  await autoBox(page).check();
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "false");
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toContain("    duration: auto\n");

  // Opening YAML rebuilt the draft from the Story: the remembered 3 s is gone.
  await expect(autoBox(page)).toBeChecked();
  await autoBox(page).uncheck();
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Duration (seconds)")).toHaveValue("2.7");
});

test("a voice set through the UI, with a non-default volume, is written to the exported YAML", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await applyYaml(page, twoSceneYaml);
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();

      src: ${ref}
    duration: 3
`);

  await page.getByLabel("Voice volume").fill("0.5");

  expect(await yamlSource(page)).toContain(`    voice:
      src: ${ref}
      volume: 0.5
    duration: 3
`);

  await voice(page).selectOption("");
  expect(await yamlSource(page)).not.toContain("voice:");
});

test("Voice None on an auto scene read from YAML uses the resolved seconds rounded to 0.1 s", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);

  const ref = await page.locator(`${SECTION} [data-local-asset-card]`).getAttribute("data-local-asset-card");

  await expect(duration.locator("output")).toHaveText("auto · 2.7 s");
  await voice(page).selectOption("");
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "false");

test("a Story value the library no longer offers stays selectable instead of showing another sound", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);

  const ghost = `local:audio:sha256:${"a".repeat(64)}`;

  await applyYaml(
    page,

test("while authoring is locked every audio control is disabled", async ({page}) => {
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    const nativeDigest = crypto.subtle.digest.bind(crypto.subtle);
    let release = () => {};
    const gate = new Promise((resolve) => {
      release = resolve;
    });

    window.__holdDigest = false;
    window.__heldDigests = 0;
    window.__releaseDigest = () => {
      window.__holdDigest = false;
      release();
    };
    crypto.subtle.digest = async (...args) => {
      if (window.__holdDigest) {
        window.__heldDigests += 1;
        await gate;
      }

      return nativeDigest(...args);
    };
  });
  await openApp(page);
  await applyYaml(
    page,
    [
      "title: Locked",
      "music:",
      "  src: ding",
      "scenes:",
      "  - type: intro",
      "    pose: formal",
      "    background: office",
      "    text: One",
      "    sfx:",
      "      src: boom",
      "    voice:",
      "      src: pop",
      "    duration: 3",
      "",
    ].join("\n"),
  );
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();

  const controls = [
    music(page),
    page.getByLabel("Music volume"),
    page.getByLabel("Music ducking"),
    page.getByLabel("Fade in (seconds)"),
    page.getByLabel("Fade out (seconds)"),
    sfx(page),
    page.getByLabel("Sound effect volume"),
    voice(page),
    page.getByLabel("Voice volume"),
    autoBox(page),
  ];

  // Positive control: enabled before the lock.
  for (const control of controls) {
    await expect(control).toBeEnabled();
  }

  await page.evaluate(() => {
    window.__holdDigest = true;
  });
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-440-2.2s.wav"));
  await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => window.__heldDigests)).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-authoring-locked", "true");

  for (const control of controls) {
    await expect(control).toBeDisabled();
  }

  await page.evaluate(() => window.__releaseDigest());
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-authoring-locked", "false");

  for (const control of controls) {
    await expect(control).toBeEnabled();
  }
});
```

`src/web/audio/useLibraryAudioOptions.ts`:

```ts
import {useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState} from "react";
import type {AssetLibraryStatus} from "../assetLibrary/coordination.ts";
import type {AssetLibraryStore} from "../assetLibrary/store.ts";
import {
  AssetLibraryContext,
  OPENING_ASSET_LIBRARY,
} from "../assetLibraryContext.ts";
import type {LibraryAudioChoices, LibraryAudioOption} from "./audioFields.ts";
import {readLibraryAudioOptions} from "./libraryAudioOptions.ts";

const NO_OPTIONS: readonly LibraryAudioOption[] = Object.freeze([]);

/** What one finished read of the library produced, and the store it came from. */
export type LibraryRead = {
  forStore: AssetLibraryStore;
  rows: readonly LibraryAudioOption[];
};

/**
 * The rows and the `settled` flag for a library status. Not settled: while the
 * library is still opening (`OPENING_ASSET_LIBRARY`), and from the moment a
 * ready library appears until its first read finishes, so the selects say
 * `Loading audio…` instead of `Missing audio …` for a ref nothing has looked
 * up yet. Settled: a library that is unavailable for good (it offers no rows
 * and the selects still show the Story's own value) and a finished read.
 */
export const libraryAudioChoices = (
  status: AssetLibraryStatus,
  read: LibraryRead | null,
): {rows: readonly LibraryAudioOption[]; settled: boolean} => {
  if (status === OPENING_ASSET_LIBRARY) {
    return {rows: NO_OPTIONS, settled: false};
  }

  if (status.kind !== "ready") {
    return {rows: NO_OPTIONS, settled: true};
  }

  return read !== null && read.forStore === status.store
    ? {rows: read.rows, settled: true}
    : {rows: NO_OPTIONS, settled: false};
};

/**
 * The My audio rows for the sound selects. Re-read whenever the library
 * changes (an import, rename or delete in this or another tab); the previous
 * rows stay in place while it re-reads. The returned object is stable while
 * neither the rows nor `settled` change, so memoized selects keep their options.
 */
export const useLibraryAudioOptions = (): LibraryAudioChoices => {
  const context = useContext(AssetLibraryContext);
  const status = context?.status ?? OPENING_ASSET_LIBRARY;
  const store = status.kind === "ready" ? status.store : null;
  const refreshToken = context?.refreshToken ?? 0;
  const [read, setRead] = useState<LibraryRead | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (store === null) {
      return;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const settle = (rows: readonly LibraryAudioOption[]) => {
      if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
        setRead({forStore: store, rows});
      }
    };

    readLibraryAudioOptions(store, controller.signal).then(settle, () =>
      settle(NO_OPTIONS),
    );

    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [store, refreshToken]);

  const {rows, settled} = libraryAudioChoices(status, read);

  return useMemo(() => ({rows, settled}), [rows, settled]);
};
```

`src/web/components/AudioControls.tsx` (`NumberField` keeps what the user is typing, for example `1.` on the way to `1.5`, and reports only finite numbers, in range or not: the schema judges them, so an out-of-range fade becomes a pending draft with the field's error):

```tsx
import {memo, useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState} from "react";
import type {AudioRef} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import {
  NO_AUDIO_VALUE,
  audioSelectOptions,
  type AudioSelectGroups,
  type LibraryAudioChoices,
} from "../audio/audioFields.ts";

type AudioSelectProps = {
  /** Visible label and accessible name: "Music", "Sound effect", "Voice". */
  label: string;
  /** The Story's current ref for this field, if any. */
  value: string | undefined;
  library: LibraryAudioChoices;
  /** `null` is the None option. */
  onChange: (src: AudioRef | null) => void;
  /** The `<select>`'s DOM id (a notice's Retry button moves focus back to it). */
  id?: string;
  /** The id of the element that describes this field (its warnings). */
  describedBy?: string;
};

/**
 * The options of one select. The element is memoized on `groups`, which is
 * itself memoized on (rows, settled, value): a re-render of the editor (every
 * keystroke in a caption) hands React the same element, so a library of
 * hundreds of sounds is not diffed again.
 */
const SelectOptions = memo(({groups}: {groups: AudioSelectGroups}) => (
  <>
    <option value={NO_AUDIO_VALUE}>None</option>
    {groups.bundled.length > 0 ? (
      <optgroup label="Bundled">
        {groups.bundled.map((option) => (
          <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
            {option.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </optgroup>
    ) : null}
    {groups.library.length > 0 ? (
      <optgroup label="My audio">
        {groups.library.map((option) => (
          <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
            {option.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </optgroup>
    ) : null}
    {groups.current === null ? null : (
      <option value={groups.current.value}>{groups.current.label}</option>
    )}
  </>
));

SelectOptions.displayName = "SelectOptions";

/** A sound select: None, a Bundled group, a My audio group, and the Story's own value if neither offers it. */
export const AudioSelect = memo(
  ({label, value, library, onChange, id, describedBy}: AudioSelectProps) => {
    const {rows, settled} = library;
    // Sorting and labelling the rows runs once per (rows, settled, value), not per render.
    const groups = useMemo(
      () =>
        audioSelectOptions({current: value, library: rows, librarySettled: settled}),
      [value, rows, settled],
    );

    return (
      <label className="field">
        <span>{label}</span>
        <select
          id={id}
          value={value ?? NO_AUDIO_VALUE}
          aria-describedby={describedBy}
          onChange={(event) =>
            onChange(
              event.target.value === NO_AUDIO_VALUE
                ? null
                : (event.target.value as AudioRef),
            )
          }
        >
          <SelectOptions groups={groups} />
        </select>
      </label>
    );
  },
);

AudioSelect.displayName = "AudioSelect";

type UnitSliderProps = {
  /** The accessible name, for example "Music volume". */
  label: string;
  /** The visible caption: "Volume", "Ducking". */
  caption: string;
  value: number;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
};

/** A 0 to 1 slider in steps of 0.05 that shows its number. */
export const UnitSlider = ({label, caption, value, onChange}: UnitSliderProps) => (
  <div className="field range-field">
    <span aria-hidden="true">{caption}</span>
    <div className="range-row">
      <input
        type="range"
        aria-label={label}
        min={0}
        max={1}
        step={0.05}
        value={value}
        onChange={(event) =>
          onChange(Math.round(Number(event.target.value) * 100) / 100)
        }
      />
      <output aria-hidden="true">{String(value)}</output>
    </div>
  </div>
);

type NumberFieldProps = {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  step: number;
  /** Called with every finite number the user types, in range or not: the schema judges it. */
  onCommit: (value: number) => void;
  error?: string;
};

/**
 * A number input that keeps what the user is typing ("1." on the way to
 * "1.5", or an emptied field) and only reports finite numbers. When the
 * Story's value changes from outside (Discard, reset) the text follows it.
 */
export const NumberField = ({
  label,
  value,
  min,
  max,
  step,
  onCommit,
  error,
}: NumberFieldProps) => {
  const errorId = useId();
  const [text, setText] = useState(String(value));

  useEffect(() => {
    setText((current) =>
      current.trim() !== "" && Number(current) === value
        ? current
        : String(value),
    );
  }, [value]);

  return (
    <label className="field">
      <span>{label}</span>
      <input
        type="number"
        min={min}
        max={max}
        step={step}
        value={text}
        aria-invalid={error !== undefined}
        aria-describedby={error === undefined ? undefined : errorId}
        onChange={(event) => {
          setText(event.target.value);

          const typed = Number(event.target.value);

          if (event.target.value.trim() !== "" && Number.isFinite(typed)) {
            onCommit(typed);
          }
        }}
        onBlur={() =>
          setText((current) =>
            current.trim() !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(current))
              ? current
              : String(value),
          )
        }
      />
      {error === undefined ? null : (
        <small id={errorId} className="field-error">
          {error}
        </small>
      )}
    </label>
  );
};
```

`src/web/components/MusicPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import type {AudioRef} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import {
  MAX_FADE_SECONDS,
  DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME,
  DEFAULT_MUSIC_DUCK,
} from "../../audio/constants.ts";
import type {Music} from "../../story/schema.ts";
import type {
  LibraryAudioChoices,
  MusicNumberField,
} from "../audio/audioFields.ts";
import {AudioSelect, NumberField, UnitSlider} from "./AudioControls.tsx";

type MusicPanelProps = {
  music: Music | undefined;
  library: LibraryAudioChoices;
  /** Field errors of the visual draft, keyed `music.fadeIn` and so on. */
  errors: Record<string, string>;
  onSourceChange: (src: AudioRef | null) => void;
  onNumberChange: (field: MusicNumberField, value: number) => void;
};

/** The Story-level music controls, above the scene list. */
export const MusicPanel = ({
  music,
  library,
  errors,
  onSourceChange,
  onNumberChange,
}: MusicPanelProps) => (
  <section className="music-panel" aria-label="Music" data-music-panel="">
    <AudioSelect
      label="Music"
      value={music?.src}
      library={library}
      onChange={onSourceChange}
    />

    {music === undefined ? null : (
      <div className="music-settings">
        <UnitSlider
          label="Music volume"
          caption="Volume"
          value={music.volume ?? DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME}
          onChange={(value) => onNumberChange("volume", value)}
        />
        <UnitSlider
          label="Music ducking"
          caption="Ducking"
          value={music.duck ?? DEFAULT_MUSIC_DUCK}
          onChange={(value) => onNumberChange("duck", value)}
        />
        <NumberField
          label="Fade in (seconds)"
          value={music.fadeIn ?? 0}
          min={0}
          max={MAX_FADE_SECONDS}
          step={0.1}
          error={errors["music.fadeIn"]}
          onCommit={(value) => onNumberChange("fadeIn", value)}
        />
        <NumberField
          label="Fade out (seconds)"
          value={music.fadeOut ?? 0}
          min={0}
          max={MAX_FADE_SECONDS}
          step={0.1}
          error={errors["music.fadeOut"]}
          onCommit={(value) => onNumberChange("fadeOut", value)}
        />
      </div>
    )}
  </section>
);
```

`src/web/components/SceneAudioFields.tsx`:

```tsx
import type {AudioRef} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import {DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME} from "../../audio/constants.ts";
import type {
  LibraryAudioChoices,
  SceneAudioRole,
} from "../audio/audioFields.ts";
import type {VisualSceneDraft} from "../visualDraft.ts";
import {AudioSelect, UnitSlider} from "./AudioControls.tsx";

type SceneAudioFieldsProps = {
  scene: VisualSceneDraft;
  library: LibraryAudioChoices;
  onSfxChange: (src: AudioRef | null) => void;
  onVoiceChange: (src: AudioRef | null) => void;
  onVolumeChange: (role: SceneAudioRole, volume: number) => void;
};

/** The selected scene's Sound effect and Voice selects, each with a Volume slider when set. */
export const SceneAudioFields = ({
  scene,
  library,
  onSfxChange,
  onVoiceChange,
  onVolumeChange,
}: SceneAudioFieldsProps) => (
  <div className="scene-audio" data-scene-audio="">
    <div className="scene-audio-row">
      <AudioSelect
        label="Sound effect"
        value={scene.sfx?.src}
        library={library}
        onChange={onSfxChange}
      />
      {scene.sfx === undefined ? null : (
        <UnitSlider
          label="Sound effect volume"
          caption="Volume"
          value={scene.sfx.volume ?? DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME}
          onChange={(volume) => onVolumeChange("sfx", volume)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
    <div className="scene-audio-row">
      <AudioSelect
        label="Voice"
        value={scene.voice?.src}
        library={library}
        onChange={onVoiceChange}
      />
      {scene.voice === undefined ? null : (
        <UnitSlider
          label="Voice volume"
          caption="Volume"
          value={scene.voice.volume ?? DEFAULT_AUDIO_VOLUME}
          onChange={(volume) => onVolumeChange("voice", volume)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  </div>
);
```

/* AUDIO-004: music panel, scene audio and the Auto checkbox. */
.music-panel {
  margin-top: 20px;
  padding: 4px 14px 14px;

}

  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));

}

.scene-audio {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 0 12px;
}

.range-field .range-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
}

.range-field input[type="range"] {
  flex: 1 1 auto;
  min-width: 0;
  padding: 0;
  accent-color: #94a3b8;
}

.range-field output {

}

  gap: 8px;
  margin-top: 10px;
  color: #d7dfeb;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

@media (max-width: 620px) {
  .music-settings,
  .scene-audio {

}
