- Modify: `src/web/localAssetPageController.ts`, `src/web/localAssetUi.ts`, `src/web/assetLibraryContext.ts`, `src/web/components/LocalAssetCard.tsx`, `src/web/components/MyAudioSection.tsx`, `src/web/components/Preview.tsx`, `src/web/App.tsx`, `src/web/styles.css`
- Test: `tests/audio-waveform.test.mjs`, `tests/audio-preview.test.mjs`, `tests/audio-ui.test.mjs` (extend), `tests/browser/audio-editor.spec.mjs` (append)

Two behaviours beyond the card itself, both pinned below: (1) **Safari plays an element only when `play()` runs inside the user's gesture**, and reading and verifying the stored bytes takes an `await`, so `start` primes the one shared element synchronously inside the click with a quarter second of silence (`SILENT_PRIMER_URL`) and swaps in the verified URL afterwards; (2) **the preview's Stop button is an authoring control**, so when the authoring lock engages (import, render, asset dialog) App stops any running preview.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `IntegrityCache.verify(store, digest, {signal})`, `ObjectUrlPool.acquire/release`, `isAudioPayload`, `AssetLibraryStatus`, `collectStoryLocalAssetUsages`, `LocalAudioUsage` (`sceneIndexes` 0-based, `roles`), `WaveformRecord`, `store.getWaveform(digest)`.
- Produces:
  - `src/web/audio/waveformColumns.ts`: `WaveformPeaks = {peaksPerSecond: number; peaks: Uint8Array}`; `waveformColumns(waveform, seconds, columns): Float32Array` (values in [0, 1]).
  - `src/web/audio/drawWaveform.ts`: `WaveformContext`; `drawWaveformBars(context, columns, area: {x?; y?; width; height}, color): void`.
  - `src/web/audio/previewPlayback.ts`: `AUDIO_PREVIEW_FAILED_MESSAGE`; `PreviewUrlLease = {url: string; release: () => void}`; `AcquirePreviewUrl = (ref: LocalAudioRef, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<PreviewUrlLease>`; `PreviewState`; `SILENT_PRIMER_URL`; `createPreviewPlayback({createAudio})` returning `{getState, subscribe, toggle(ref, acquire), stop, stopRef(ref), playerStarted, setPlayerPause(pause | null)}`; `audioPreview(): PreviewPlayback` (the page singleton).
  - `src/web/audio/previewSource.ts`: `acquireAudioPreviewUrl({library, cache, pool, ref, signal}): Promise<PreviewUrlLease>`.
  - `src/web/audio/useAudioPreview.ts`: `useAudioPreviewState(): PreviewState`.
  - `LocalAssetPageEntry.waveform?: WaveformRecord`; `LocalAssetCardPreview = {active: boolean; error: string | null; onToggle: () => void}`; `AssetLibraryContextValue.acquirePreviewUrl: AcquirePreviewUrl`.
  - `src/web/localAssetUi.ts`: `activeStoryAudioUsage(usage: LocalAssetUsage): {sceneNumbers: number[]; usesMusic: boolean}`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/audio-waveform.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {drawWaveformBars} from "../src/web/audio/drawWaveform.ts";
import {waveformColumns} from "../src/web/audio/waveformColumns.ts";
import {createLocalAssetPageController} from "../src/web/localAssetPageController.ts";

const peaksOf = (values) => ({peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: Uint8Array.from(values)});

describe("waveformColumns", () => {
  it("takes the largest stored peak under each column", () => {
    // 4 peaks at 50 per second cover 0.08 s; two columns split them in pairs.
    const columns = waveformColumns(peaksOf([0, 255, 51, 102]), 0.08, 2);

    assert.deepEqual([...columns], [1, Math.fround(102 / 255)]);
  });

  it("gives one column per peak when they match, and repeats a peak when columns outnumber peaks", () => {
    assert.deepEqual([...waveformColumns(peaksOf([255, 0, 51]), 0.06, 3)], [1, 0, Math.fround(51 / 255)]);
    assert.deepEqual([...waveformColumns(peaksOf([255, 51]), 0.04, 4)], [1, 1, Math.fround(51 / 255), Math.fround(51 / 255)]);
  });

  it("shows only the first seconds asked for, and leaves columns past the stored peaks at zero", () => {
    const peaks = peaksOf([255, 255, 255, 255]);

    assert.deepEqual([...waveformColumns(peaks, 0.04, 2)], [1, 1]);
    // The four stored peaks cover 0.08 s: the last two of four columns are past them.
    assert.deepEqual([...waveformColumns(peaks, 0.16, 4)], [1, 1, 0, 0]);
  });

  it("returns zeros, never NaN, for unusable input", () => {
    const peaks = peaksOf([255]);

    assert.deepEqual([...waveformColumns(peaks, 0, 3)], [0, 0, 0]);
    assert.deepEqual([...waveformColumns(peaks, Number.NaN, 3)], [0, 0, 0]);
    assert.deepEqual([...waveformColumns({peaksPerSecond: 0, peaks: peaks.peaks}, 1, 2)], [0, 0]);
    assert.deepEqual([...waveformColumns(peaksOf([]), 1, 2)], [0, 0]);
    assert.equal(waveformColumns(peaks, 1, 0).length, 0);
    assert.equal(waveformColumns(peaks, 1, -4).length, 0);
    assert.equal(waveformColumns(peaks, 1, Number.NaN).length, 0);
  });
});

describe("drawWaveformBars", () => {
  const recorder = () => {
    const calls = [];

    return {
      calls,
      context: {
        fillStyle: "",
        clearRect: () => calls.push(["clear"]),
        fillRect: (...args) => calls.push(["fill", ...args]),
      },
    };
  };

  it("draws a mirrored bar for every sounding column and nothing for silence", () => {
    const {calls, context} = recorder();

    drawWaveformBars(context, Float32Array.from([1, 0, 0.5]), {width: 30, height: 20}, "#fff");

    assert.equal(context.fillStyle, "#fff");
    assert.deepEqual(calls, [
      ["fill", 0, 0, 9.5, 20],
      ["fill", 20, 5, 9.5, 10],
    ]);
  });

  it("keeps a very quiet column at least one pixel tall and clamps loud values", () => {
    const {calls, context} = recorder();

    drawWaveformBars(context, Float32Array.from([0.001, 7]), {width: 4, height: 10}, "red");

    assert.deepEqual(calls, [
      ["fill", 0, 4.5, 1.5, 1],
      ["fill", 2, 0, 1.5, 10],
    ]);
  });

  it("draws nothing for an empty or zero-sized area", () => {
    const {calls, context} = recorder();

    drawWaveformBars(context, new Float32Array(0), {width: 10, height: 10}, "red");
    drawWaveformBars(context, Float32Array.from([1]), {width: 0, height: 10}, "red");
    drawWaveformBars(context, Float32Array.from([1]), {width: 10, height: 0}, "red");
    assert.deepEqual(calls, []);
  });
});

describe("the My audio page carries stored waveforms", () => {
  const digestOf = (n) => n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0");
  const refOf = (n) => `local:audio:sha256:${digestOf(n)}`;
  const row = (n) => ({
    status: "present",
    value: {label: `Sound ${n}`, originalFilename: `s${n}.wav`, createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"},
  });
  const audioMeta = {
    kind: "audio", mimeType: "audio/wav", byteSize: 10, container: "wave", codec: "pcm",
    durationSeconds: 2, sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2,
  };
  const makeStore = (getWaveform) => {
    const refs = [1, 2, 3].map(refOf);

    return {
      async listAssets() {
        return refs.map((ref, index) => ({ref, row: row(index + 1)}));
      },
      async countAssets() {
        return refs.length;
      },
      async getAssetRow(ref) {
        return refs.includes(ref) ? row(refs.indexOf(ref) + 1) : {status: "absent"};
      },
      async getPayloadMeta() {
        return {status: "present", value: audioMeta};
      },
      getWaveform,
    };
  };

  it("adds the stored peaks to an entry, and leaves the key out when they are absent, corrupt or unreadable", async () => {
    const peaks = {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: Uint8Array.from([1, 2, 3])};
    const controller = createLocalAssetPageController({
      store: makeStore(async (digest) => {
        if (digest === digestOf(1)) return {status: "present", value: peaks};
        if (digest === digestOf(2)) return {status: "corrupt", reason: "bad"};

        throw new Error("read failed");
      }),
      category: "audio",
      createUrl: () => "blob:test",
      revokeUrl: () => {},
    });

    await controller.sync(null);

    const {entries} = controller.getSnapshot();

    assert.equal(entries[0].waveform, peaks);
    assert.equal("waveform" in entries[1], false);
    assert.equal("waveform" in entries[2], false);
    assert.equal(entries.every((entry) => entry.audio.durationSeconds === 2), true);
    controller.dispose();
  });
});
```

`tests/audio-preview.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {sha256Hex} from "../src/localAssets/hash.ts";
import {buildLocalAssetRef} from "../src/localAssets/refs.ts";
import {importLocalAsset} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/importAsset.ts";
import {IntegrityCache} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/integrity.ts";
import {
  AUDIO_PREVIEW_FAILED_MESSAGE,
  createPreviewPlayback,
} from "../src/web/audio/previewPlayback.ts";
import {acquireAudioPreviewUrl} from "../src/web/audio/previewSource.ts";
import {fixtureBytes} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";
import {createMemoryAssetStore, createMemoryLockManager} from "./helpers/memoryAssetStore.mjs";

const digestOf = (n) => n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0");
const refOf = (n) => buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digestOf(n));

/** A fake element that records what the coordinator does with it. */
const makeAudio = (log, {playRejects = false, primerRejects = false} = {}) => {
  const listeners = {};
  const audio = {
    src: "",
    listeners,
    play: async () => {
      log.push(`play ${audio.src}`);

      if (primerRejects && audio.src.startsWith("data:")) {
        throw new DOMException("interrupted", "AbortError");
      }

      if (playRejects) {
        throw new DOMException("blocked", "NotAllowedError");
      }
    },
    pause: () => log.push("pause"),
    addEventListener: (type, listener) => {
      listeners[type] = listener;
    },
  };

  return audio;
};

const makeLease = (ref, leases) => {
  const lease = {
    url: `blob:${ref.slice(-4)}`,
    released: 0,
    release() {
      lease.released += 1;
    },
  };

  leases.push(lease);

  return lease;
};

const harness = (options = {}) => {
  const log = [];
  const created = [];
  const leases = [];
  const preview = createPreviewPlayback({
    createAudio: () => {
      const audio = makeAudio(log, options);

      created.push(audio);

      return audio;
    },
  });
  const acquireFor = async (ref) => {
    log.push(`acquire ${ref.slice(-4)}`);

    return makeLease(ref, leases);
  };

  preview.setPlayerPause(() => log.push("player.pause"));

  return {preview, log, created, leases, acquireFor};
};

describe("the page's audio preview", () => {
  it("Play pauses the Player first, loads the verified URL and plays it", async () => {
    const {preview, log, acquireFor} = harness();
    const states = [];

    preview.subscribe(() => states.push(preview.getState().kind));
    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);

    assert.deepEqual(
      log
        .filter((entry) => entry !== "pause")
        .map((entry) => (entry.startsWith("play data:") ? "play primer" : entry)),
      ["player.pause", "play primer", "acquire 0001", "play blob:0001"],
    );
    assert.deepEqual(states, ["loading", "playing"]);
    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "playing", ref: refOf(1)});
  });

  it("primes the element synchronously inside the click, before the stored bytes are verified (Safari plays only inside the gesture)", async () => {
    const {preview, log, created, leases} = harness();
    let finish = () => {};
    const pending = preview.toggle(
      refOf(1),
      (ref) =>
        new Promise((resolve) => {
          log.push("acquire called");
          finish = () => resolve(makeLease(ref, leases));
        }),
    );

    // Nothing has been awaited yet: the primer already played, the real sound has not.
    assert.equal(created.length, 1);
    assert.match(created[0].src, /^data:audio\/wav;base64,UklGR/u);
    assert.deepEqual(
      log.filter((entry) => entry.startsWith("play ")).map((entry) => entry.slice(0, 15)),
      ["play data:audio"],
    );
    assert.ok(log.indexOf("acquire called") > log.findIndex((entry) => entry.startsWith("play data:")));
    assert.equal(preview.getState().kind, "loading");

    finish();
    await pending;
    assert.equal(created[0].src, "blob:0001");
    assert.equal(preview.getState().kind, "playing");
  });

  it("Stop or another Play while the primer plays still wins: the late URL is never played", async () => {
    const stopped = harness();
    let finishFirst = () => {};
    const first = stopped.preview.toggle(
      refOf(1),
      (ref) =>
        new Promise((resolve) => {
          finishFirst = () => resolve(makeLease(ref, stopped.leases));
        }),
    );

    stopped.preview.stop();
    finishFirst();
    await first;
    assert.deepEqual(stopped.preview.getState(), {kind: "idle"});
    assert.equal(stopped.leases[0].released, 1);
    assert.equal(stopped.log.some((entry) => entry.startsWith("play blob:")), false);
    assert.equal(stopped.log.at(-1), "pause");

    const replaced = harness();
    let finishOld = () => {};
    const old = replaced.preview.toggle(
      refOf(1),
      (ref) =>
        new Promise((resolve) => {
          finishOld = () => resolve(makeLease(ref, replaced.leases));
        }),
    );

    await replaced.preview.toggle(refOf(2), replaced.acquireFor);
    finishOld();
    await old;
    assert.deepEqual(replaced.preview.getState(), {kind: "playing", ref: refOf(2)});
    assert.deepEqual(
      replaced.log.filter((entry) => entry.startsWith("play blob:")),
      ["play blob:0002"],
    );
  });

  it("a rejected primer does not fail the preview: the real play() decides", async () => {
    const {preview, leases, acquireFor} = harness({primerRejects: true});

    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);
    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "playing", ref: refOf(1)});
    assert.equal(leases[0].released, 0);
  });

  it("Stop on the playing sound pauses it, releases its URL and goes idle", async () => {
    const {preview, leases, created, acquireFor} = harness();

    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);
    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);

    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "idle"});
    assert.equal(leases[0].released, 1);
    assert.equal(created.length, 1);
  });

  it("starting another sound stops the first: one preview at a time, one element", async () => {
    const {preview, log, leases, created, acquireFor} = harness();

    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);
    await preview.toggle(refOf(2), acquireFor);

    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "playing", ref: refOf(2)});
    assert.equal(leases[0].released, 1);
    assert.equal(leases[1].released, 0);
    assert.equal(created.length, 1);
    assert.equal(log.filter((entry) => entry.startsWith("play blob:")).length, 2);
    assert.ok(log.indexOf("play blob:0002") > log.lastIndexOf("pause"));
  });

  it("the Player starting stops the preview", async () => {
    const {preview, leases, acquireFor} = harness();

    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);
    preview.playerStarted();

    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "idle"});
    assert.equal(leases[0].released, 1);
  });

  it("stops by itself when the sound ends", async () => {
    const {preview, created, leases, acquireFor} = harness();

    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);
    created[0].listeners.ended();

    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "idle"});
    assert.equal(leases[0].released, 1);
  });

  it("a failed load shows the failure for that sound and a later Play tries again", async () => {
    const {preview, acquireFor} = harness();
    let attempt = 0;
    const flaky = async (ref, signal) => {
      attempt += 1;

      if (attempt === 1) {
        throw new Error("The stored audio is missing or damaged.");
      }

      return acquireFor(ref, signal);
    };

    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), flaky);
    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {
      kind: "failed",
      ref: refOf(1),
      message: AUDIO_PREVIEW_FAILED_MESSAGE,
    });

    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), flaky);
    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "playing", ref: refOf(1)});
  });

  it("a blocked play() and an element error both fail and release the URL", async () => {
    const blocked = harness({playRejects: true});

    await blocked.preview.toggle(refOf(1), blocked.acquireFor);
    assert.equal(blocked.preview.getState().kind, "failed");
    assert.equal(blocked.leases[0].released, 1);

    const broken = harness();

    await broken.preview.toggle(refOf(1), broken.acquireFor);
    broken.created[0].listeners.error();
    assert.equal(broken.preview.getState().kind, "failed");
    assert.equal(broken.leases[0].released, 1);
  });

  it("a URL that arrives after Stop is released and never played", async () => {
    const {preview, log, leases} = harness();
    let finish = () => {};
    const slow = (ref) =>
      new Promise((resolve) => {
        finish = () => resolve(makeLease(ref, leases));
      });

    const first = preview.toggle(refOf(1), slow);

    assert.equal(preview.getState().kind, "loading");
    preview.stop();
    finish();
    await first;

    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "idle"});
    assert.equal(leases[0].released, 1);
    assert.equal(log.some((entry) => entry.startsWith("play blob:")), false);
  });

  it("a URL that arrives after another Play started is released and never played", async () => {
    const {preview, log, leases, acquireFor} = harness();
    let finish = () => {};
    const slow = (ref) =>
      new Promise((resolve) => {
        finish = () => resolve(makeLease(ref, leases));
      });

    const first = preview.toggle(refOf(1), slow);

    await preview.toggle(refOf(2), acquireFor);
    finish();
    await first;

    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "playing", ref: refOf(2)});
    assert.equal(leases.find((lease) => lease.url === "blob:0001").released, 1);
    assert.deepEqual(
      log.filter((entry) => entry.startsWith("play blob:")),
      ["play blob:0002"],
    );
  });

  it("Stop while loading aborts the acquire", async () => {
    const {preview} = harness();
    let signal;

    void preview.toggle(refOf(1), (_ref, abortSignal) => {
      signal = abortSignal;

      return new Promise(() => {});
    });

    assert.equal(preview.getState().kind, "loading");
    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), async () => assert.fail("Stop must not acquire"));
    assert.equal(signal.aborted, true);
    assert.deepEqual(preview.getState(), {kind: "idle"});
  });

  it("stopRef stops only the sound that is going away", async () => {
    const {preview, acquireFor} = harness();

    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);
    preview.stopRef(refOf(2));
    assert.equal(preview.getState().kind, "playing");
    preview.stopRef(refOf(1));
    assert.equal(preview.getState().kind, "idle");
  });

  it("publishes a new state object only on a change and stops notifying after unsubscribe", async () => {
    const {preview, acquireFor} = harness();
    const idle = preview.getState();
    let notified = 0;
    const unsubscribe = preview.subscribe(() => {
      notified += 1;
    });

    preview.stop();
    preview.playerStarted();
    assert.equal(preview.getState(), idle);
    assert.equal(notified, 0);

    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);

    const playing = preview.getState();

    assert.notEqual(playing, idle);
    assert.equal(preview.getState(), playing);

    const before = notified;

    unsubscribe();
    preview.stop();
    assert.equal(notified, before);
  });

  it("works without a Player", async () => {
    const {preview, acquireFor} = harness();

    preview.setPlayerPause(null);
    await preview.toggle(refOf(1), acquireFor);
    assert.equal(preview.getState().kind, "playing");
  });
});

describe("acquireAudioPreviewUrl", () => {
  const importFixture = async () => {
    const library = {
      store: createMemoryAssetStore(),
      locks: createMemoryLockManager(),
      channel: {post() {}},
    };
    const bytes = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");

    await importLocalAsset(new File([bytes], "voice.wav"), "audio", library, {
      decodePeaks: async () => ({
        decodedSeconds: 2.2,
        peaks: Uint8Array.from([1]),
        peaksPerSecond: 50,
      }),
    });

    const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);

    return {
      library,
      status: {kind: "ready", ...library},
      digest,
      ref: buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digest),
    };
  };
  const makePool = () => {
    const calls = [];

    return {
      calls,
      acquire: (digest, blob) => {
        calls.push(["acquire", digest, blob.type]);

        return `blob:${digest.slice(0, 4)}`;
      },
      release: (digest) => calls.push(["release", digest]),
    };
  };

  it("verifies the stored bytes and leases the pool's URL until released (once)", async () => {
    const {status, digest, ref} = await importFixture();
    const pool = makePool();
    const lease = await acquireAudioPreviewUrl({
      library: status,
      cache: new IntegrityCache(),
      pool,
      ref,
      signal: new AbortController().signal,
    });

    assert.equal(lease.url, `blob:${digest.slice(0, 4)}`);
    assert.deepEqual(pool.calls, [["acquire", digest, "audio/wav"]]);
    lease.release();
    lease.release();
    assert.deepEqual(pool.calls.at(-1), ["release", digest]);
    assert.equal(pool.calls.filter(([kind]) => kind === "release").length, 1);
  });

  it("refuses a library that is not ready, an absent copy and a damaged one", async () => {
    const {library, status, digest, ref} = await importFixture();
    const signal = new AbortController().signal;
    const attempt = (overrides) =>
      acquireAudioPreviewUrl({
        library: status,
        cache: new IntegrityCache(),
        pool: makePool(),
        ref,
        signal,
        ...overrides,
      });

    await assert.rejects(attempt({library: null}), /not available/u);
    await assert.rejects(
      attempt({library: {kind: "unavailable", message: "no"}}),
      /not available/u,
    );
    await assert.rejects(attempt({ref: refOf(77)}), /missing or damaged/u);

    library.store.setRaw("blobs", digest, {blob: new Blob([new Uint8Array(10)])});
    await assert.rejects(attempt({}), /missing or damaged/u);
  });

  it("a verification that finishes after an abort leases nothing", async () => {
    const {status, ref} = await importFixture();
    const pool = makePool();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const pending = acquireAudioPreviewUrl({
      library: status,
      cache: new IntegrityCache(),
      pool,
      ref,
      signal: controller.signal,
    });

    controller.abort();
    await assert.rejects(pending);
    assert.deepEqual(pool.calls, []);
  });
});
```

```js

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// My audio cards: waveform thumbnail, preview, single-preview rule (AUDIO-004 task 3)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const card = (page, label) =>
  page.locator(`${SECTION} [data-local-asset-card]`).filter({hasText: label});
const playButton = (page, label) => page.getByRole("button", {name: `Play ${label}`, exact: true});
const stopButton = (page, label) => page.getByRole("button", {name: `Stop ${label}`, exact: true});
const trackPlayedAudio = (page) =>
  page.addInitScript(() => {
    window.__played = [];

    const original = HTMLMediaElement.prototype.play;

    HTMLMediaElement.prototype.play = function play(...args) {
      if (this.src.startsWith("blob:")) {
        window.__played.push(this);
      }

      return original.apply(this, args);
    };
  });
const soundingPreviews = (page) =>
  page.evaluate(
    () => new Set(window.__played.filter((element) => !element.paused && !element.ended)).size,
  );

test("a card draws its waveform from the stored peaks", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-440-2.2s.wav"));

  const canvas = card(page, "tone-440-2.2s").locator("canvas[data-waveform-thumbnail]");

  await expect(canvas).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(canvas).toHaveAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  // A full-scale tone fills the canvas height: bars cover most of the middle row.
  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      canvas.evaluate((element) => {
        const {data} = element.getContext("2d").getImageData(0, 0, element.width, element.height);
        let painted = 0;

        for (let index = 3; index < data.length; index += 4) {
          if (data[index] > 0) {
            painted += 1;
          }
        }

        return painted;
      }),
    )
    .toBeGreaterThan(1000);
});

test("Play and Stop toggle one preview on the verified payload, and only one card plays at a time", async ({page}) => {
  await trackPlayedAudio(page);
  await openApp(page);
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-220-10s.wav"));
  await expect(card(page, "tone-220-10s")).toHaveCount(1);
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-880-0.4s.wav"));
  await expect(card(page, "tone-880-0.4s")).toHaveCount(1);
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-220-12s.wav"));
  await expect(card(page, "tone-220-12s")).toHaveCount(1);

  await playButton(page, "tone-220-10s").click();
  await expect(stopButton(page, "tone-220-10s")).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => soundingPreviews(page)).toBe(1);
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.__played[0].src.startsWith("blob:"))).toBe(true);

  // Another card takes over: the first goes back to Play.
  await playButton(page, "tone-220-12s").click();
  await expect(stopButton(page, "tone-220-12s")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(playButton(page, "tone-220-10s")).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => soundingPreviews(page)).toBe(1);

  await stopButton(page, "tone-220-12s").click();
  await expect(playButton(page, "tone-220-12s")).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => soundingPreviews(page)).toBe(0);
});

  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Play video"}).click();

  await expect.poll(() => soundingPreviews(page)).toBe(0);

});

test("deleting the sound that is playing stops it", async ({page}) => {
  await trackPlayedAudio(page);

test("the preview controls are disabled while authoring is locked, and a preview that is playing is stopped when the lock engages", async ({page}) => {
  await trackPlayedAudio(page);
  await openApp(page);
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-220-10s.wav"));
  await expect(playButton(page, "tone-220-10s")).toBeEnabled();
  await playButton(page, "tone-220-10s").click();
  await expect.poll(() => soundingPreviews(page)).toBe(1);

  await page.evaluate(() => {
    const nativeDigest = crypto.subtle.digest.bind(crypto.subtle);
    let release = () => {};
    const gate = new Promise((resolve) => {
      release = resolve;
    });

    window.__heldDigests = 0;
    window.__releaseDigest = () => release();
    crypto.subtle.digest = async (...args) => {
      window.__heldDigests += 1;
      await gate;

      return nativeDigest(...args);
    };
  });
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-440-2.2s.wav"));
  await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => window.__heldDigests)).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-authoring-locked", "true");
  await expect(playButton(page, "tone-220-10s")).toBeDisabled();
  // The lock stopped the preview (its Stop button is disabled, so nothing else could).
  await expect.poll(() => soundingPreviews(page)).toBe(0);
  await page.evaluate(() => window.__releaseDigest());
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-authoring-locked", "false");
  await expect(playButton(page, "tone-220-10s")).toBeEnabled();
});
```

`src/web/audio/waveformColumns.ts`:

```ts
/** The stored peaks of one sound (`WaveformRecord` and the bundled store's `peaks` have this shape). */
export type WaveformPeaks = {
  peaksPerSecond: number;
  peaks: Uint8Array;
};

/**
 * Reduces the first `seconds` of a waveform to `columns` values in [0, 1]: the
 * largest stored peak under each column. A column past the end of the peaks (a
 * track longer than its stored waveform) is 0. Bad input gives zeros, never NaN.
 */
export const waveformColumns = (
  waveform: WaveformPeaks,
  seconds: number,
  columns: number,
): Float32Array => {
  const count = Number.isFinite(columns) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(columns)) : 0;
  const result = new Float32Array(count);
  const {peaks, peaksPerSecond} = waveform;

  if (
    count === 0 ||
    !Number.isFinite(seconds) ||
    seconds <= 0 ||
    !Number.isFinite(peaksPerSecond) ||
    peaksPerSecond <= 0
  ) {
    return result;
  }

  for (let column = 0; column < count; column += 1) {
    const first = Math.floor(((column * seconds) / count) * peaksPerSecond);
    const end = Math.max(
      first + 1,
      Math.ceil((((column + 1) * seconds) / count) * peaksPerSecond),
    );
    let peak = 0;

    for (let index = first; index < end && index < peaks.length; index += 1) {
      if (peaks[index] > peak) {
        peak = peaks[index];
      }
    }

    result[column] = peak / 255;
  }

  return result;
};
```

`src/web/audio/drawWaveform.ts`:

```ts
/** The few canvas members the drawing needs (a recording fake in Node tests). */
export type WaveformContext = {
  clearRect: (x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number) => void;
  fillRect: (x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number) => void;
  fillStyle: string | CanvasGradient | CanvasPattern;
};

/**
 * Draws `columns` (values in [0, 1]) as mirrored bars around the middle of a
 * `width` x `height` area. A silent column draws nothing; any sound is at least
 * one pixel tall so a quiet passage stays visible.
 */
export const drawWaveformBars = (
  context: WaveformContext,
  columns: Float32Array,
  area: {x?: number; y?: number; width: number; height: number},
  color: string,
): void => {
  const {x = 0, y = 0, width, height} = area;

  if (columns.length === 0 || width <= 0 || height <= 0) {
    return;
  }

  const barWidth = width / columns.length;

  context.fillStyle = color;

  for (let index = 0; index < columns.length; index += 1) {
    const level = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, columns[index]));

    if (level > 0) {
      const barHeight = Math.max(1, level * height);

      context.fillRect(
        x + index * barWidth,
        y + (height - barHeight) / 2,
        Math.max(1, barWidth - 0.5),
        barHeight,
      );
    }
  }
};
```

`src/web/audio/previewPlayback.ts` (one `Audio` element for the whole page, so "one preview at a time" is structural; the element is primed synchronously, before the first `await`, so Safari accepts the later `play()`; a URL or play() result that arrives after Stop or after another Play is released and never played):

```ts
import type {LocalAudioRef} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";

/**
 * A quarter of a second of silence (8 kHz, 8-bit, mono). `start` plays it
 * synchronously inside the click, before the verified URL is known: Safari
 * only lets an element play when `play()` is called within the user's gesture,
 * and reading and verifying the stored bytes takes an `await` or more.
 */
export const SILENT_PRIMER_URL =
  "data:audio/wav;base64,UklGRvQHAABXQVZFZm10IBAAAAABAAEAQB8AAEAfAAABAAgAZGF0YdAHAACAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgA==";

/** The text a card shows when a preview could not start. */
export const AUDIO_PREVIEW_FAILED_MESSAGE = "This audio could not be played.";

/** A verified object URL for one sound, leased until `release`. */
export type PreviewUrlLease = {url: string; release: () => void};

export type AcquirePreviewUrl = (
  ref: LocalAudioRef,
  signal: AbortSignal,
) => Promise<PreviewUrlLease>;

/** The few members of `HTMLAudioElement` the coordinator uses (a fake in Node tests). */
export type PreviewAudioElement = {
  src: string;
  play: () => Promise<void>;
  pause: () => void;
  addEventListener: (type: "ended" | "error", listener: () => void) => void;
};

export type PreviewState =
  | {kind: "idle"}
  | {kind: "loading"; ref: LocalAudioRef}
  | {kind: "playing"; ref: LocalAudioRef}
  | {kind: "failed"; ref: LocalAudioRef; message: string};

const IDLE: PreviewState = {kind: "idle"};

/**
 * The page's ONE audio preview. A single element plays at most one sound, so
 * "one preview at a time across the page" cannot be broken by two cards. The
 * Player and the preview exclude each other: `toggle` pauses the Player it was
 * given, and `playerStarted` (called from the Player's `play` event) stops the
 * preview.
 *
 * The state object is replaced on every change and kept otherwise, so it can
 * feed `useSyncExternalStore`.
 */
export const createPreviewPlayback = (deps: {
  createAudio: () => PreviewAudioElement;
}) => {
  let audio: PreviewAudioElement | null = null;
  let state: PreviewState = IDLE;
  let generation = 0;
  let abort: AbortController | null = null;
  let lease: PreviewUrlLease | null = null;
  let pausePlayer: (() => void) | null = null;
  const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  const publish = (next: PreviewState) => {
    state = next;

    for (const listener of [...listeners]) {
      listener();
    }
  };

  const releaseLease = () => {
    const held = lease;

    lease = null;
    held?.release();
  };

  const element = (): PreviewAudioElement => {
    if (audio === null) {
      const created = deps.createAudio();
      const current = (): boolean => audio === created;

      created.addEventListener("ended", () => {
        if (current() && state.kind === "playing") {
          stop();
        }
      });
      created.addEventListener("error", () => {
        if (current() && state.kind === "playing") {
          fail(state.ref);
        }
      });
      audio = created;
    }

    return audio;
  };

  const halt = () => {
    generation += 1;
    abort?.abort();
    abort = null;
    audio?.pause();
    releaseLease();
  };

  const fail = (ref: LocalAudioRef) => {
    halt();
    publish({kind: "failed", ref, message: AUDIO_PREVIEW_FAILED_MESSAGE});
  };

  const stop = () => {
    halt();

    if (state.kind !== "idle") {
      publish(IDLE);
    }
  };

  const start = async (ref: LocalAudioRef, acquire: AcquirePreviewUrl) => {
    halt();

    const mine = generation;
    const controller = new AbortController();

    abort = controller;
    publish({kind: "loading", ref});
    // Pause the Player now, not when the sound is ready: the two never overlap.
    pausePlayer?.();

    // Prime the shared element inside the click, before the first await (see
    // SILENT_PRIMER_URL); the verified URL replaces the source below. A rejected
    // primer is harmless: the real play() below decides success or failure.
    const primed = element();

    primed.src = SILENT_PRIMER_URL;
    primed.play().catch(() => {});

    let acquired: PreviewUrlLease;

    try {
      acquired = await acquire(ref, controller.signal);
    } catch {
      if (generation === mine) {
        fail(ref);
      }

      return;
    }

    if (generation !== mine) {
      acquired.release();

      return;
    }

    lease = acquired;

    try {
      const playing = element();

      playing.src = acquired.url;
      await playing.play();
    } catch {
      if (generation === mine) {
        fail(ref);
      }

      return;
    }

    if (generation === mine) {
      publish({kind: "playing", ref});
    }
  };

  return {
    getState: (): PreviewState => state,
    subscribe: (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
      listeners.add(listener);

      return () => {
        listeners.delete(listener);
      };
    },
    /** Play or Stop for `ref`: stops this sound when it is the one loading or playing, otherwise starts it (stopping any other). */
    toggle: async (ref: LocalAudioRef, acquire: AcquirePreviewUrl) => {
      if (
        (state.kind === "loading" || state.kind === "playing") &&
        state.ref === ref
      ) {
        stop();

        return;
      }

      await start(ref, acquire);
    },
    stop,
    /** Stops the preview only when it belongs to `ref` (the card is going away). */
    stopRef: (ref: LocalAudioRef) => {
      if (state.kind !== "idle" && state.ref === ref) {
        stop();
      }
    },
    /** Called from the Player's `play` event. */
    playerStarted: stop,
    /** The Player's `pause`, or null when no Player is mounted. */
    setPlayerPause: (pause: (() => void) | null) => {
      pausePlayer = pause;
    },
  };
};

export type PreviewPlayback = ReturnType<typeof createPreviewPlayback>;

let page: PreviewPlayback | null = null;

/** The page's preview, created on first use with a real `Audio` element. */
export const audioPreview = (): PreviewPlayback => {
  page ??= createPreviewPlayback({
    createAudio: () => new Audio() as PreviewAudioElement,
  });

  return page;
};
```

`src/web/audio/previewSource.ts`:

```ts
import {isAudioPayload} from "../../localAssets/readiness.ts";
import {parseLocalAssetRef, type LocalAudioRef} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";
import type {AssetLibraryStatus} from "../assetLibrary/coordination.ts";
import type {IntegrityCache} from "../assetLibrary/integrity.ts";
import type {LocalAssetUrlPool} from "../localAssetState.ts";
import type {PreviewUrlLease} from "./previewPlayback.ts";

/**
 * A verified object URL for a library sound, for the My audio preview. The bytes
 * go through the same integrity check as the Player's (`IntegrityCache.verify`,
 * INV-12) and the URL comes from the page's one pool (the same pool as images),
 * leased for as long as the preview holds it. Rejects when the library is not
 * ready, the stored copy is missing or damaged, or it is not audio.
 */
export const acquireAudioPreviewUrl = async (input: {
  library: AssetLibraryStatus | null;
  cache: Pick<IntegrityCache, "verify">;
  pool: LocalAssetUrlPool;
  ref: LocalAudioRef;
  signal: AbortSignal;
}): Promise<PreviewUrlLease> => {
  const {library, cache, pool, ref, signal} = input;
  const parsed = parseLocalAssetRef(ref);

  if (library?.kind !== "ready" || parsed === null) {
    throw new Error("My audio is not available.");
  }

  const verification = await cache.verify(library.store, parsed.digest, {signal});

  signal.throwIfAborted();

  if (!verification.ok || !isAudioPayload(verification.payload)) {
    throw new Error("The stored audio is missing or damaged.");
  }

  const url = pool.acquire(parsed.digest, verification.blob);
  let released = false;

  return {
    url,
    release: () => {
      if (!released) {
        released = true;
        pool.release(parsed.digest);
      }
    },
  };
};
```

`src/web/audio/useAudioPreview.ts`:

```ts
import {useSyncExternalStore} from "react";
import {audioPreview, type PreviewState} from "./previewPlayback.ts";

/** The page's audio preview state (idle, loading, playing or failed, and for which sound). */
export const useAudioPreviewState = (): PreviewState => {
  const preview = audioPreview();

  return useSyncExternalStore(
    preview.subscribe,
    preview.getState,
    preview.getState,
  );
};
```

```diff
+ * counted from that same usage: a pending visual draft can neither hide the
+ * problem it describes nor invent one. (The delete warning of a regular card

+export const activeStoryAudioUsage = (
+  usage: LocalAssetUsage,
+): {sceneNumbers: number[]; usesMusic: boolean} =>
+  usage.category === "audio"
+    ? {
+        sceneNumbers: usage.sceneIndexes.map((index) => index + 1),
+        usesMusic: usage.roles.includes("music"),
```

`src/web/components/WaveformCanvas.tsx`:

```tsx
import {useEffect, useRef} from "react";
import {drawWaveformBars} from "../audio/drawWaveform.ts";
import {waveformColumns, type WaveformPeaks} from "../audio/waveformColumns.ts";

type WaveformCanvasProps = {
  waveform: WaveformPeaks;
  /** How many seconds of the sound the canvas shows. */
  seconds: number;
  className?: string;
};

const WIDTH = 240;
const HEIGHT = 48;
const COLUMNS = 120;

/** A My audio card's waveform thumbnail, drawn once from the stored peaks. Decorative. */
export const WaveformCanvas = ({waveform, seconds, className}: WaveformCanvasProps) => {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const context = canvasRef.current?.getContext("2d");

    if (context === null || context === undefined) {
      return;
    }

    context.clearRect(0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT);
    drawWaveformBars(
      context,
      waveformColumns(waveform, seconds, COLUMNS),
      {width: WIDTH, height: HEIGHT},
      "#94a3b8",
    );
  }, [waveform, seconds]);

  return (
    <canvas
      ref={canvasRef}
      className={className}
      width={WIDTH}
      height={HEIGHT}
      aria-hidden="true"
      data-waveform-thumbnail=""
    />
  );
};
```

### Task 4: The bundled-audio store

The page-session store AUDIO-005 consumes verbatim. Pure and dependency-injected, so every transition is a Node test with a fake fetch, fake decode and a manual clock.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts`
- Test: `tests/bundled-audio-store.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (on `main`): `bundledAudioCatalog`, `BundledAudio`, `BundledAudioEntry`, `isBundledAudio` (`src/audio/catalog.ts`); `isDecodeComplete` (`src/audio/decodeCheck.ts`); `inspectAudio`, `AudioInspection`, `AudioMetadata` (`src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts`); `decodeAudioPeaks`, `AudioDecodeError`, `AUDIO_DECODE_FAILED_MESSAGE`, `DecodedAudioSummary` (`src/web/assetLibrary/audioPeaks.ts`); `AUDIO_TRUNCATED_MESSAGE` (`src/web/assetLibrary/importAudio.ts`); `AudioSource` (`src/audio/sources.ts`); `staticFile` (`remotion`); `WaveformPeaks` (Task 3).
- Produces (the contract with AUDIO-005; names and types verbatim):
  - `type BundledAudioState = {kind: "loading"} | {kind: "retrying"; attempt: 1 | 2 | 3} | {kind: "ready"; peaks: WaveformPeaks; blob: Blob; url: string} | {kind: "fetch-failed"; message: string} | {kind: "unusable"; message: string}`
  - `requestBundledAudio(ids: Iterable<BundledAudio>): void`, `retryBundledAudio(id: BundledAudio): void`, `subscribeBundledAudio(listener: () => void): () => void`, `getBundledAudioStates(): ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState>`, `useBundledAudioStates(): ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState>`
  - `createBundledAudioStore(deps: BundledAudioDeps)` with `{request, retry, subscribe, getStates}`; `BUNDLED_AUDIO_RETRY_DELAYS_MS`, `BUNDLED_AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE` (`Audio could not be loaded.`), `BUNDLED_AUDIO_NOT_SUPPLIED_MESSAGE`
  - `storyBundledAudio(story): BundledAudio[]`, `audioSourceForBundledState(state | undefined): AudioSource | undefined`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`tests/bundled-audio-store.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {bundledAudioCatalog} from "../src/audio/catalog.ts";
import {AudioDecodeError} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/audioPeaks.ts";
import {
  BUNDLED_AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE,
  BUNDLED_AUDIO_NOT_SUPPLIED_MESSAGE,
  BUNDLED_AUDIO_RETRY_DELAYS_MS,
  audioSourceForBundledState,
  createBundledAudioStore,
  getBundledAudioStates,
  requestBundledAudio,
  retryBundledAudio,
  storyBundledAudio,
  subscribeBundledAudio,
  useBundledAudioStates,
} from "../src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts";

const METADATA = {container: "wave", codec: "pcm", durationSeconds: 1.2, sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2};
const SUMMARY = {decodedSeconds: 1.2, peaks: Uint8Array.from([10, 255, 30]), peaksPerSecond: 2.5};
const flush = async () => {
  for (let index = 0; index < 12; index += 1) {
    await Promise.resolve();
  }
};

/** A manual clock: timers only run when the test fires them. */
const makeClock = () => {
  const timers = [];

  return {
    timers,
    setTimer: (callback, delayMs) => {
      const timer = {callback, delayMs, cleared: false, fired: false};

      timers.push(timer);

      return timer;
    },
    clearTimer: (timer) => {
      timer.cleared = true;
    },
    pending: () => timers.filter((timer) => !timer.cleared && !timer.fired),
    /** Fires the single pending timer. */
    fire: async () => {
      const [timer] = timers.filter((candidate) => !candidate.cleared && !candidate.fired);

      assert.ok(timer, "a timer is pending");
      timer.fired = true;
      timer.callback();
      await flush();

      return timer.delayMs;
    },
  };
};

/**
 * `behaviours[n]` decides the n-th fetch: "ok", "network" (rejects), "http"
 * (a 503 response), "body" (the body read rejects), or {hold: true} (stays
 * pending until released or aborted).
 */
const makeFetch = (behaviours) => {
  const calls = [];
  const fetch = (url, {signal}) => {
    const behaviour = behaviours[calls.length] ?? behaviours.at(-1);
    const call = {url, signal, release: () => {}};

    calls.push(call);

    if (behaviour === "network") {
      return Promise.reject(new TypeError("Failed to fetch"));
    }

    if (behaviour === "http") {
      return Promise.resolve({ok: false, blob: async () => new Blob([])});
    }

    if (behaviour === "body") {
      return Promise.resolve({ok: true, blob: () => Promise.reject(new TypeError("body"))});
    }

    const respond = () => ({ok: true, blob: async () => new Blob([`bytes ${calls.length}`])});

    if (behaviour === "ok") {
      return Promise.resolve(respond());
    }

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      call.release = (response = respond()) => resolve(response);
      signal.addEventListener("abort", () => reject(new DOMException("aborted", "AbortError")));
    });
  };

  return {fetch, calls};
};

const makeStore = (overrides = {}) => {
  const clock = makeClock();
  const log = {inspect: 0, decode: 0, objectUrls: 0};
  const remote = makeFetch(overrides.behaviours ?? ["ok"]);
  const store = createBundledAudioStore({
    fetch: remote.fetch,
    inspect: async () => {
      log.inspect += 1;

      return overrides.inspect?.() ?? {ok: true, metadata: METADATA};
    },
    decode: async (_blob, _metadata, signal) => {
      log.decode += 1;

      return overrides.decode?.(signal) ?? SUMMARY;
    },
    fileUrl: (file) => `/${file}`,
    createObjectUrl: (blob) => {
      log.objectUrls += 1;

      return `blob:test/${log.objectUrls}`;
    },
    setTimer: clock.setTimer,
    clearTimer: clock.clearTimer,
    catalog: bundledAudioCatalog,
    retryDelaysMs: BUNDLED_AUDIO_RETRY_DELAYS_MS,
  });
  const snapshots = [];

  store.subscribe(() => snapshots.push(store.getStates()));

  return {store, clock, log, remote, snapshots};
};

const kinds = (store, id) => store.getStates().get(id)?.kind;

describe("the bundled-audio store: checking an id", () => {
  it("fetches the catalog file once, inspects, decodes and ends ready with peaks, the Blob and ONE object URL", async () => {
    const {store, remote, log} = makeStore();

    store.request(["ding"]);
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "loading");
    await flush();

    const state = store.getStates().get("ding");

    assert.equal(state.kind, "ready");
    assert.deepEqual(state.peaks, {peaksPerSecond: 2.5, peaks: Uint8Array.from([10, 255, 30])});
    assert.ok(state.blob instanceof Blob);
    assert.equal(state.url, "blob:test/1");
    assert.deepEqual(remote.calls.map((call) => call.url), ["/audio/sfx/ding.wav"]);
    assert.equal(log.objectUrls, 1);

    store.request(["ding"]);
    store.request([]);
    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(store.getStates().get("ding"), state);
    assert.equal(remote.calls.length, 1);
    assert.equal(log.objectUrls, 1);
  });

  it("two scenes (or two requests) using one id cause one fetch", async () => {
    const {store, remote} = makeStore();

    store.request(["ding", "ding", "pop"]);
    store.request(["pop", "ding"]);
    await flush();

    assert.deepEqual(
      remote.calls.map((call) => call.url).sort(),
      ["/audio/sfx/ding.wav", "/audio/sfx/pop.wav"],
    );
  });

  it("an id whose catalog file is null is unusable at once, without any fetch", async () => {
    const {store, remote} = makeStore();

    store.request(["calm-loop"]);
    assert.deepEqual(store.getStates().get("calm-loop"), {
      kind: "unusable",
      message: BUNDLED_AUDIO_NOT_SUPPLIED_MESSAGE,
    });
    await flush();
    assert.equal(remote.calls.length, 0);
  });
});

describe("the bundled-audio store: transitions publish", () => {
  it("every transition publishes a NEW map, a request that changes nothing publishes nothing", async () => {
    const {store, snapshots} = makeStore();
    const initial = store.getStates();

    store.request([]);
    assert.equal(snapshots.length, 0);
    assert.equal(store.getStates(), initial);

    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(snapshots.length, 2); // loading, ready
    assert.equal(new Set([initial, ...snapshots]).size, 3);
    assert.equal(snapshots[0].get("ding").kind, "loading");
    assert.equal(snapshots[1].get("ding").kind, "ready");

    // Nothing transitions: the same object, no notification.
    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(snapshots.length, 2);
    assert.equal(store.getStates(), snapshots[1]);
  });

  it("a map that was published never changes afterwards", async () => {
    const {store, snapshots} = makeStore();

    store.request(["ding", "pop"]);
    await flush();

    assert.equal(snapshots[0].has("pop"), false);
    assert.equal(snapshots[0].get("ding").kind, "loading");
  });

  it("unsubscribe stops the notifications", async () => {
    const {store} = makeStore();
    const seen = [];
    const unsubscribe = store.subscribe(() => seen.push(1));

    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    const before = seen.length;

    unsubscribe();
    store.request(["ding", "pop"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(seen.length, before);
  });
});

describe("the bundled-audio store: network failures are transient", () => {
  it("retries after 1 s, 2 s and 4 s (retrying 1, 2, 3), then fetch-failed, and Retry goes loading then ready", async () => {
    const {store, clock, remote, snapshots} = makeStore({behaviours: ["network", "network", "network", "network", "ok"]});

    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.deepEqual(store.getStates().get("ding"), {kind: "retrying", attempt: 1});
    assert.equal(clock.pending()[0].delayMs, 1_000);

    assert.equal(await clock.fire(), 1_000);
    assert.deepEqual(store.getStates().get("ding"), {kind: "retrying", attempt: 2});
    assert.equal(clock.pending()[0].delayMs, 2_000);

    assert.equal(await clock.fire(), 2_000);
    assert.deepEqual(store.getStates().get("ding"), {kind: "retrying", attempt: 3});
    assert.equal(clock.pending()[0].delayMs, 4_000);

    assert.equal(await clock.fire(), 4_000);
    assert.deepEqual(store.getStates().get("ding"), {kind: "fetch-failed", message: BUNDLED_AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE});
    assert.equal(remote.calls.length, 4);
    assert.equal(clock.pending().length, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(
      snapshots.map((snapshot) => snapshot.get("ding").kind + (snapshot.get("ding").attempt ?? "")),
      ["loading", "retrying1", "retrying2", "retrying3", "fetch-failed"],
    );

    store.retry("ding");
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "loading");
    await flush();
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "ready");
    assert.equal(remote.calls.length, 5);
  });

  it("a retry that succeeds publishes ready like a first success, without any other interaction", async () => {
    const {store, clock, log} = makeStore({behaviours: ["network", "ok"]});

    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "retrying");
    await clock.fire();
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "ready");
    assert.equal(store.getStates().get("ding").url, "blob:test/1");
    assert.equal(log.objectUrls, 1);
    assert.equal(clock.pending().length, 0);
  });

  it("an HTTP error and a body that fails to read are transient too", async () => {
    for (const behaviour of ["http", "body"]) {
      const {store, clock} = makeStore({behaviours: [behaviour, "ok"]});

      store.request(["ding"]);
      await flush();
      assert.deepEqual(store.getStates().get("ding"), {kind: "retrying", attempt: 1}, behaviour);
      await clock.fire();
      assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "ready", behaviour);
    }
  });

  it("Retry does nothing in any other state", async () => {
    const {store, remote} = makeStore({behaviours: ["ok"]});

    store.retry("ding");
    store.request(["ding", "calm-loop"]);
    await flush();
    store.retry("ding");
    store.retry("calm-loop");
    store.retry("pop");
    await flush();
    assert.equal(remote.calls.length, 1);
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "ready");
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "calm-loop"), "unusable");
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "pop"), undefined);
  });
});

describe("the bundled-audio store: terminal failures are memoized", () => {
  const cases = {
    "an inspection that rejects the file": {inspect: () => ({ok: false, message: "Video files are not supported. Use an audio file."}), message: "Video files are not supported. Use an audio file."},
    "an inspection that throws": {inspect: () => { throw new Error("boom"); }, message: "This audio file could not be decoded."},
    "a decode error": {decode: () => { throw new AudioDecodeError(); }, message: "This audio file could not be decoded."},
    "a truncated decode": {decode: () => ({...SUMMARY, decodedSeconds: 0.5}), message: "This audio file looks truncated."},
    "a decode that produced nothing": {decode: () => ({...SUMMARY, decodedSeconds: 0}), message: "This audio file looks truncated."},
  };

  for (const [name, {message, ...overrides}] of Object.entries(cases)) {
    it(`${name} ends unusable with its message and is never fetched, retried or re-checked again`, async () => {
      const {store, clock, remote, log} = makeStore(overrides);

      store.request(["ding"]);
      await flush();

      const state = store.getStates().get("ding");

      assert.deepEqual(state, {kind: "unusable", message});
      assert.equal(clock.timers.length, 0);

      store.retry("ding");
      store.request([]);
      store.request(["ding"]);
      store.request(["ding", "pop"]);
      await flush();
      assert.equal(store.getStates().get("ding"), state);
      assert.equal(remote.calls.filter((call) => call.url.endsWith("ding.wav")).length, 1);
      assert.equal(log.objectUrls, 0, "an unusable id never gets an object URL");
    });
  }
});

describe("the bundled-audio store: ids that are no longer requested", () => {
  it("abort the in-flight fetch, forget the state and publish nothing for the stale result", async () => {
    const {store, remote, snapshots} = makeStore({behaviours: [{hold: true}, "ok"]});

    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "loading");

    store.request([]);
    assert.equal(remote.calls[0].signal.aborted, true);
    assert.equal(store.getStates().has("ding"), false);

    const count = snapshots.length;

    remote.calls[0].release();
    await flush();
    assert.equal(snapshots.length, count);
    assert.equal(store.getStates().has("ding"), false);

    // A later request starts it afresh.
    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(remote.calls.length, 2);
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "ready");
  });

  it("clear the pending retry timer and a retry that would have run never fetches", async () => {
    const {store, clock, remote} = makeStore({behaviours: ["network", "ok"]});

    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(clock.pending().length, 1);

    store.request(["pop"]);
    assert.equal(clock.pending().length, 0);
    assert.equal(clock.timers[0].cleared, true);
    assert.equal(store.getStates().has("ding"), false);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(remote.calls.filter((call) => call.url.endsWith("ding.wav")).length, 1);
  });

  it("forget a fetch-failed id, so showing it again retries by itself", async () => {
    const {store, clock, remote} = makeStore({behaviours: ["network", "network", "network", "network", "ok"]});

    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    await clock.fire();
    await clock.fire();
    await clock.fire();
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "fetch-failed");

    store.request([]);
    assert.equal(store.getStates().has("ding"), false);
    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "ready");
    assert.equal(remote.calls.length, 5);
  });

  it("keep ready and unusable ids for the page session", async () => {
    const {store, remote} = makeStore();

    store.request(["ding", "calm-loop"]);
    await flush();

    const ready = store.getStates().get("ding");

    store.request([]);
    assert.equal(store.getStates().get("ding"), ready);
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "calm-loop"), "unusable");
    store.request(["ding"]);
    assert.equal(remote.calls.length, 1);
  });

  it("abort the decode of an id that was dropped while decoding, and publish nothing for it", async () => {
    let decodeSignal;
    let finish = () => {};
    const {store, snapshots} = makeStore({
      decode: (signal) => {
        decodeSignal = signal;

        return new Promise((resolve) => {
          finish = () => resolve(SUMMARY);
        });
      },
    });

    store.request(["ding"]);
    await flush();
    assert.equal(kinds(store, "ding"), "loading");
    store.request([]);
    assert.equal(decodeSignal.aborted, true);

    const count = snapshots.length;

    finish();
    await flush();
    assert.equal(snapshots.length, count);
    assert.equal(store.getStates().has("ding"), false);
  });
});

describe("the page-level API (AUDIO-005 consumes it as is)", () => {
  it("exposes request, retry, subscribe, the states snapshot and the hook", () => {
    for (const member of [requestBundledAudio, retryBundledAudio, subscribeBundledAudio, getBundledAudioStates, useBundledAudioStates]) {
      assert.equal(typeof member, "function");
    }

    const states = getBundledAudioStates();

    assert.equal(states instanceof Map, true);
    assert.equal(states.size, 0);
    assert.equal(getBundledAudioStates(), states);

    const unsubscribe = subscribeBundledAudio(() => {});

    assert.equal(typeof unsubscribe, "function");
    unsubscribe();
  });
});

describe("what the Player and the story helpers take from the store", () => {
  it("maps each state to the Player's source: pending, url, unavailable, or no entry", () => {
    const blob = new Blob(["x"]);

    assert.equal(audioSourceForBundledState(undefined), undefined);
    assert.deepEqual(audioSourceForBundledState({kind: "loading"}), {kind: "pending"});
    assert.deepEqual(audioSourceForBundledState({kind: "retrying", attempt: 2}), {kind: "pending"});
    assert.deepEqual(audioSourceForBundledState({kind: "fetch-failed", message: "x"}), {kind: "pending"});
    assert.deepEqual(audioSourceForBundledState({kind: "unusable", message: "x"}), {kind: "unavailable"});
    assert.deepEqual(
      audioSourceForBundledState({kind: "ready", peaks: {peaksPerSecond: 1, peaks: new Uint8Array(1)}, blob, url: "blob:a"}),
      {kind: "url", url: "blob:a"},
    );
  });

  it("lists only the bundled ids of a Story", () => {
    const local = `local:audio:sha256:${"1".repeat(64)}`;
    const story = {
      title: "T",
      music: {src: "calm-loop"},
      scenes: [
        {type: "intro", pose: "formal", background: "office", text: "A", duration: 3, sfx: {src: "ding"}, voice: {src: local}},
        {type: "intro", pose: "formal", background: "office", text: "B", duration: 3, sfx: {src: "ding"}},
      ],
    };

    assert.deepEqual(storyBundledAudio(story), ["calm-loop", "ding"]);
    assert.deepEqual(storyBundledAudio({title: "T", scenes: [story.scenes[0]].map((scene) => ({...scene, sfx: undefined, voice: undefined}))}), []);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the store**

`src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts`:

```ts
import {useSyncExternalStore} from "react";
import {staticFile} from "remotion";
import {
  bundledAudioCatalog,
  isBundledAudio,
  type BundledAudio,
  type BundledAudioEntry,
} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import {isDecodeComplete} from "../../audio/decodeCheck.ts";
import {storyAudioRefs} from "../../audio/storyAudio.ts";
import type {AudioSource} from "../../audio/sources.ts";
import {
  inspectAudio,
  type AudioInspection,
  type AudioMetadata,
} from "../../localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import type {Story} from "../../story/types.ts";
import {
  AUDIO_DECODE_FAILED_MESSAGE,
  decodeAudioPeaks,
  type DecodedAudioSummary,
} from "../assetLibrary/audioPeaks.ts";
import {AUDIO_TRUNCATED_MESSAGE} from "../assetLibrary/importAudio.ts";
import type {WaveformPeaks} from "./waveformColumns.ts";

/** After the first failed fetch the store retries after 1 s, 2 s and 4 s, then gives up until `Retry`. */
export const BUNDLED_AUDIO_RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [1_000, 2_000, 4_000] as const;

/** The text of a field whose bundled sound could not be fetched (a `Retry` button follows). */
export const BUNDLED_AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE = "Audio could not be loaded.";

/** A catalog entry without a file (the owner has not supplied the music yet). */
export const BUNDLED_AUDIO_NOT_SUPPLIED_MESSAGE =
  "This bundled sound has no file in this build.";

export type BundledAudioState =
  | {kind: "loading"}
  | {kind: "retrying"; attempt: 1 | 2 | 3}
  /** `url` is ONE object URL of `blob`, created once for the page session. */
  | {kind: "ready"; peaks: WaveformPeaks; blob: Blob; url: string}
  | {kind: "fetch-failed"; message: string}
  | {kind: "unusable"; message: string};

/** The few members of a fetch Response the store uses. */
export type BundledAudioResponse = {
  ok: boolean;
  blob: () => Promise<Blob>;
};

export type BundledAudioDeps = {
  fetch: (
    url: string,
    init: {signal: AbortSignal},
  ) => Promise<BundledAudioResponse>;
  inspect: (blob: Blob) => Promise<AudioInspection>;
  decode: (
    blob: Blob,
    metadata: AudioMetadata,
    signal: AbortSignal,
  ) => Promise<DecodedAudioSummary>;
  /** `staticFile(entry.file)` in the browser. */
  fileUrl: (file: string) => string;
  createObjectUrl: (blob: Blob) => string;
  setTimer: (callback: () => void, delayMs: number) => unknown;
  clearTimer: (handle: unknown) => void;
  catalog: Readonly<Record<BundledAudio, BundledAudioEntry>>;
  retryDelaysMs: readonly number[];
};

type Outcome =
  | {kind: "ready"; peaks: WaveformPeaks; blob: Blob}
  | {kind: "transient"}
  | {kind: "terminal"; message: string};

type Run = {controller: AbortController; timer: unknown};

const NO_STATES: ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState> = new Map();

/**
 * The page-session store of bundled sounds: the ONE source of truth for their
 * peaks, their playability and (AUDIO-005) their render readiness. It is built
 * from injected dependencies so Node tests drive every transition; the page
 * uses the module-level functions below.
 *
 * Each id is checked once per page session: fetch the file, `inspectAudio`,
 * decode the peaks (`decodeAudioPeaks`) and apply the D-9 truncation rule. A
 * network failure is transient (retries after 1 s, 2 s and 4 s, then
 * `fetch-failed` until `retry`); an inspection or decode failure, or a
 * truncated file, is terminal (`unusable`, memoized, never fetched again,
 * because the files are fixed per build). `ready` is memoized too. Ids that
 * stop being requested lose their in-flight fetch, pending retry and any
 * non-terminal state, so a later request starts them afresh.
 *
 * `getStates()` returns a NEW map on every transition and the same object
 * otherwise (it feeds `useSyncExternalStore`).
 */
export const createBundledAudioStore = (deps: BundledAudioDeps) => {
  let states: ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState> = NO_STATES;
  let requested: ReadonlySet<BundledAudio> = new Set();
  const runs = new Map<BundledAudio, Run>();
  const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  const publish = (next: ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState>) => {
    states = next;

    for (const listener of [...listeners]) {
      listener();
    }
  };

  const setState = (id: BundledAudio, state: BundledAudioState) => {
    publish(new Map(states).set(id, state));
  };

  const dropState = (id: BundledAudio) => {
    const next = new Map(states);

    next.delete(id);
    publish(next);
  };

  const check = async (
    id: BundledAudio,
    file: string,
    signal: AbortSignal,
  ): Promise<Outcome> => {
    let blob: Blob;

    try {
      const response = await deps.fetch(deps.fileUrl(file), {signal});

      if (!response.ok) {
        return {kind: "transient"};
      }

      blob = await response.blob();
    } catch {
      return {kind: "transient"};
    }

    if (signal.aborted) {
      return {kind: "transient"};
    }

    try {
      const inspected = await deps.inspect(blob);

      if (!inspected.ok) {
        return {kind: "terminal", message: inspected.message};
      }

      const summary = await deps.decode(blob, inspected.metadata, signal);

      return isDecodeComplete(summary.decodedSeconds, inspected.metadata.durationSeconds)
        ? {
            kind: "ready",
            blob,
            peaks: {peaksPerSecond: summary.peaksPerSecond, peaks: summary.peaks},
          }
        : {kind: "terminal", message: AUDIO_TRUNCATED_MESSAGE};
    } catch {
      return {kind: "terminal", message: AUDIO_DECODE_FAILED_MESSAGE};
    }
  };

  const attempt = async (
    id: BundledAudio,
    file: string,
    run: Run,
    retriesDone: number,
  ) => {
    const outcome = await check(id, file, run.controller.signal);

    // Stopped being requested while the check ran: nothing to publish.
    if (run.controller.signal.aborted) {
      return;
    }

    if (outcome.kind === "ready") {
      runs.delete(id);
      setState(id, {
        kind: "ready",
        peaks: outcome.peaks,
        blob: outcome.blob,
        url: deps.createObjectUrl(outcome.blob),
      });

      return;
    }

    if (outcome.kind === "terminal") {
      runs.delete(id);
      setState(id, {kind: "unusable", message: outcome.message});

      return;
    }

    if (retriesDone >= deps.retryDelaysMs.length) {
      runs.delete(id);
      setState(id, {
        kind: "fetch-failed",
        message: BUNDLED_AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE,
      });

      return;
    }

    setState(id, {kind: "retrying", attempt: (retriesDone + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3});
    run.timer = deps.setTimer(() => {
      run.timer = null;
      void attempt(id, file, run, retriesDone + 1);
    }, deps.retryDelaysMs[retriesDone]);
  };

  const start = (id: BundledAudio) => {
    const {file} = deps.catalog[id];

    // No file in this build (the music is not supplied yet): nothing to fetch.
    if (file === null) {
      setState(id, {kind: "unusable", message: BUNDLED_AUDIO_NOT_SUPPLIED_MESSAGE});

      return;
    }

    const run: Run = {controller: new AbortController(), timer: null};

    runs.set(id, run);
    setState(id, {kind: "loading"});
    void attempt(id, file, run, 0);
  };

  const cancel = (id: BundledAudio) => {
    const run = runs.get(id);

    if (run !== undefined) {
      run.controller.abort();

      if (run.timer !== null) {
        deps.clearTimer(run.timer);
      }

      runs.delete(id);
    }

    const state = states.get(id);

    // ready and unusable are memoized for the page session.
    if (state !== undefined && state.kind !== "ready" && state.kind !== "unusable") {
      dropState(id);
    }
  };

  return {
    /**
     * Starts the check of every id not started yet and cancels the ones that
     * are no longer requested. The argument is the whole set that is wanted now.
     */
    request: (ids: Iterable<BundledAudio>) => {
      const wanted = new Set(ids);

      for (const id of requested) {
        if (!wanted.has(id)) {
          cancel(id);
        }
      }

      requested = wanted;

      for (const id of wanted) {
        if (!states.has(id)) {
          start(id);
        }
      }
    },
    /** `fetch-failed` back to `loading` with a fresh retry schedule; any other state is left alone. */
    retry: (id: BundledAudio) => {
      if (states.get(id)?.kind === "fetch-failed" && requested.has(id)) {
        start(id);
      }
    },
    subscribe: (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
      listeners.add(listener);

      return () => {
        listeners.delete(listener);
      };
    },
    getStates: (): ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState> => states,
  };
};

export type BundledAudioStore = ReturnType<typeof createBundledAudioStore>;

let pageStore: BundledAudioStore | null = null;

const store = (): BundledAudioStore => {
  pageStore ??= createBundledAudioStore({
    fetch: (url, init) => fetch(url, init),
    inspect: (blob) => inspectAudio(blob),
    decode: (blob, metadata, signal) => decodeAudioPeaks(blob, metadata, {signal}),
    fileUrl: (file) => staticFile(file),
    createObjectUrl: (blob) => URL.createObjectURL(blob),
    setTimer: (callback, delayMs) => setTimeout(callback, delayMs),
    clearTimer: (handle) => clearTimeout(handle as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>),
    catalog: bundledAudioCatalog,
    retryDelaysMs: BUNDLED_AUDIO_RETRY_DELAYS_MS,
  });

  return pageStore;
};

export const requestBundledAudio = (ids: Iterable<BundledAudio>): void =>
  store().request(ids);

export const retryBundledAudio = (id: BundledAudio): void => store().retry(id);

export const subscribeBundledAudio = (listener: () => void): (() => void) =>
  store().subscribe(listener);

export const getBundledAudioStates = (): ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState> =>
  store().getStates();

export const useBundledAudioStates = (): ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState> =>
  useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeBundledAudio,
    getBundledAudioStates,
    getBundledAudioStates,
  );

/** The bundled ids a Story references, in `storyAudioRefs` order. */
export const storyBundledAudio = (story: Story): BundledAudio[] =>
  storyAudioRefs(story).filter(isBundledAudio);

/**
 * What the Player's source map holds for a bundled id: `pending` while the
 * check runs, retries or waits for `Retry`; the object URL of the checked Blob
 * when ready; `unavailable` for a terminal failure. An id without a state is
 * left out (under the `"explicit"` policy that resolves to `pending`).
 */
export const audioSourceForBundledState = (
  state: BundledAudioState | undefined,
): AudioSource | undefined => {
  if (state === undefined) {
    return undefined;
  }

  switch (state.kind) {
    case "ready":
      return {kind: "url", url: state.url};
    case "unusable":
      return {kind: "unavailable"};
    default:
      return {kind: "pending"};
  }
};
```
