- Test: `tests/player-bundled-audio.test.mjs` (new), `tests/story-audio-durations.test.mjs` (extend), `tests/browser/audio-editor.spec.mjs` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 4): `useBundledAudioStates`, `requestBundledAudio`, `audioSourceForBundledState`, `storyBundledAudio`; on `main`: `buildAudioSourceMap(story, localSources, bundled)`, `buildStoryAudioMounts`, `compileAudioPlan`, `resolveAudioSrc`.
- Produces:
  - `bundledAudioKey(story): string` (distinct bundled ids, sorted, comma-joined) and `parseBundledAudioKey(key): BundledAudio[]` (`bundledAudio.ts`).
  - `Preview` props gain a required `audioSources: AudioSourceMap`; `Preview` no longer builds the map and passes `bundledAudioPolicy: "explicit"`.
  - `startAudioDurationRead(store | null, refs, settle): () => void` (`useStoryAudioDurations.ts`).
  - In App: `bundledStates` (the store's snapshot) and `audioSources`, which later tasks and AUDIO-005 read.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/player-bundled-audio.test.mjs` (the Player's path as pure code: store states -> source map -> plan -> the mounts `StoryAudio` renders, plus source contracts for App and Preview):

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {readFile} from "node:fs/promises";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {buildStoryAudioMounts} from "../src/audio/mounts.ts";
import {compileAudioPlan} from "../src/audio/plan.ts";
import {
  audioSourceState,
  buildAudioSourceMap,
  resolveAudioSrc,
} from "../src/audio/sources.ts";
import {storyAudioDurations} from "../src/audio/storyAudio.ts";
import {compileTimeline} from "../src/story/timeline.ts";
import {
  audioSourceForBundledState,
  bundledAudioKey,
  parseBundledAudioKey,
} from "../src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts";

const read = (path) => readFile(new URL(`../${path}`, import.meta.url), "utf8");
const LOCAL = `local:audio:sha256:${"c".repeat(64)}`;
const scene = (overrides = {}) => ({
  type: "dialogue",
  pose: "formal",
  background: "office",
  text: "Hi",
  duration: 3,
  ...overrides,
});
const story = {
  title: "Bundled",
  scenes: [
    scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}}),
    scene({sfx: {src: "pop"}, voice: {src: LOCAL}}),
    scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}}),
  ],
};
const ready = (id) => ({
  kind: "ready",
  peaks: {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: new Uint8Array(2)},
  blob: new Blob([id]),
  url: `blob:http://localhost/${id}`,
});

/** What the Player does for these store states: the App's source map, then StoryAudio's plan and mounts. */
const playerFor = (states, localSources = {[LOCAL]: {kind: "url", url: "blob:http://localhost/voice"}}) => {
  const sources = buildAudioSourceMap(story, localSources, (id) =>
    audioSourceForBundledState(states[id]),
  );
  const durations = storyAudioDurations(story, {[LOCAL]: 2.2});
  const plan = compileAudioPlan({
    story,
    timeline: compileTimeline(story, 30, durations),
    fps: 30,
    audioDurations: durations,
    sourceState: (ref) => audioSourceState(resolveAudioSrc(ref, sources, "explicit")),
  });
  const mounts = buildStoryAudioMounts({plan, sources, policy: "explicit", audioArmed: true});

  return {sources, plan, mounts};
};
const issueKinds = (plan) => plan.issues.map((issue) => `${issue.kind}:${issue.ref}`).sort();

describe("the Player's bundled audio follows the store (policy explicit)", () => {
  it("mounts no bundled track before the store has an answer, and the local voice still plays", () => {
    const {mounts, plan} = playerFor({});

    assert.deepEqual(issueKinds(plan), ["pending-audio:ding", "pending-audio:ding", "pending-audio:pop"]);
    assert.deepEqual(mounts.map((mount) => mount.src), ["blob:http://localhost/voice"]);
  });

  it("loading, retrying and fetch-failed all keep the track out, as pending (never missing)", () => {
    for (const state of [
      {kind: "loading"},
      {kind: "retrying", attempt: 2},
      {kind: "fetch-failed", message: "Audio could not be loaded."},
    ]) {
      const {mounts, plan} = playerFor({ding: state, pop: state});

      assert.equal(plan.issues.every((issue) => issue.kind === "pending-audio"), true, state.kind);
      assert.equal(mounts.length, 1, state.kind);
    }
  });

  it("an unusable id is missing: no track and one missing-audio issue per use, while the other sounds play", () => {
    const {mounts, plan} = playerFor({ding: {kind: "unusable", message: "x"}, pop: ready("pop")});

    assert.deepEqual(issueKinds(plan), ["missing-audio:ding", "missing-audio:ding"]);
    assert.deepEqual(mounts.map((mount) => mount.src).sort(), ["blob:http://localhost/pop", "blob:http://localhost/voice"]);
  });

  it("a ready id mounts from the store's blob: URL, never from the static file, once per use", () => {
    const {mounts, plan} = playerFor({ding: ready("ding"), pop: ready("pop")});

    assert.deepEqual(plan.issues, []);
    assert.equal(mounts.length, 4);
    assert.equal(mounts.every((mount) => mount.src.startsWith("blob:")), true);
    assert.equal(mounts.some((mount) => mount.src.includes("/audio/sfx/")), false);
    assert.deepEqual(
      mounts.filter((mount) => mount.src.endsWith("/ding")).map((mount) => mount.from),
      [0, 180],
    );
  });

  it("a track appears as soon as the store turns ready, with nothing else changing", () => {
    const before = playerFor({ding: {kind: "loading"}, pop: ready("pop")});
    const after = playerFor({ding: ready("ding"), pop: ready("pop")});

    assert.equal(before.mounts.length, 2);
    assert.equal(after.mounts.length, 4);
    assert.deepEqual(
      before.plan.tracks.map((track) => track.key),
      after.plan.tracks.map((track) => track.key).filter((key) => key === "voice-1" || key === "sfx-1"),
    );
  });

  it("passes a local entry through as the same object and builds no entry for a Story without audio", () => {
    const entry = {kind: "url", url: "blob:http://localhost/voice"};
    const {sources} = playerFor({}, {[LOCAL]: entry});

    assert.equal(sources[LOCAL], entry);
    assert.deepEqual(
      buildAudioSourceMap({title: "Silent", scenes: [scene()]}, undefined, () => ({kind: "pending"})),
      {},
    );
  });
});

describe("the requested bundled set", () => {
  it("is the Story's distinct bundled ids, sorted, and does not depend on scene order or local audio", () => {
    assert.equal(bundledAudioKey(story), "ding,pop");
    assert.equal(bundledAudioKey({...story, scenes: [...story.scenes].reverse()}), "ding,pop");
    assert.equal(bundledAudioKey({title: "T", scenes: [scene()]}), "");
    assert.equal(bundledAudioKey({title: "T", scenes: [scene({voice: {src: LOCAL}, duration: "auto"})]}), "");
    assert.deepEqual(parseBundledAudioKey("ding,pop"), ["ding", "pop"]);
    assert.deepEqual(parseBundledAudioKey(""), []);
  });
});

describe("App and Preview wiring (the Player no longer falls back to the static file)", () => {
  it("Preview takes the sources from App and resolves bundled ids explicitly", async () => {
    const source = await read("src/web/components/Preview.tsx");

    assert.match(source, /audioSources: AudioSourceMap;/);
    assert.match(source, /bundledAudioPolicy: "explicit" as const/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(source, /static-fallback/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(source, /noBundledAudio|buildAudioSourceMap\(/);
  });

  it("App requests the Story's bundled ids from the store and builds the Player's map from its states", async () => {
    const source = await read("src/web/App.tsx");

    assert.match(source, /const bundledStates = useBundledAudioStates\(\);/);
    assert.match(source, /requestBundledAudio\(parseBundledAudioKey\(bundledKey\)\)/);
    assert.match(source, /buildAudioSourceMap\(activeStory, localAssets\.sources,/);
    assert.match(source, /audioSourceForBundledState\(bundledStates\.get\(id\)\)/);
    assert.match(source, /audioSources=\{audioSources\}/);
  });
});
```

`tests/story-audio-durations.test.mjs` (the carry from AUDIO-003's review: a refresh re-reads, a stale result is discarded, a rejection settles the empty map; the diff adds two imports and a block at the end):

```diff
--- a/tests/story-audio-durations.test.mjs
+++ b/tests/story-audio-durations.test.mjs
@@ -6,7 +6,9 @@ import {
   localAudioRefsKey,
   parseLocalAudioRefsKey,
   sameDurationMap,
+  startAudioDurationRead,
 } from "../src/web/useStoryAudioDurations.ts";
+import {createMemoryAssetStore} from "./helpers/memoryAssetStore.mjs";
 
 const audioRef = (n) => `local:audio:sha256:${n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0")}`;
 const scene = (extra = {}) => ({type: "intro", pose: "formal", background: "office", text: "Hi", duration: 3, ...extra});
@@ -70,3 +72,150 @@ describe("one duration map per page (AUDIO-001 final-review follow-up)", () => {
     assert.match(app, /audioDurations=\{audioDurations\}/u);
   });
 });
+
+describe("startAudioDurationRead (the hook's effect, AUDIO-003 follow-up)", () => {
+  const seed = (store, n, durationSeconds) => {
+    store.setRaw("assets", audioRef(n), {
+      label: `Sound ${n}`,
+      originalFilename: `s${n}.wav`,
+      createdAt: "2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z",
+    });
+    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0"), {
+      kind: "audio",
+      mimeType: "audio/wav",
+      byteSize: 10,
+      container: "wave",
+      codec: "pcm",
+      durationSeconds,
+      sampleRate: 48_000,
+      channels: 2,
+    });
+  };
+  const flush = async () => {
+    for (let index = 0; index < 20; index += 1) {
+      await Promise.resolve();
+    }
+  };
+  /** Holds the store's first row read until `release()`. */
+  const gatedStore = (store) => {
+    let release = () => {};
+    const gate = new Promise((resolve) => {
+      release = resolve;
+    });
+    let first = true;
+
+    return {
+      release,
+      store: {
+        getPayloadMeta: (digest) => store.getPayloadMeta(digest),
+        getAssetRow: async (ref) => {
+          if (first) {
+            first = false;
+            await gate;
+          }
+
+          return store.getAssetRow(ref);
+        },
+      },
+    };
+  };
+
+  it("settles once with the durations of the Story's own refs, whichever library page they are on", async () => {
+    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
+
+    seed(store, 1, 2.2);
+    seed(store, 2, 4.5);
+
+    const settled = [];
+
+    startAudioDurationRead(store, [audioRef(1), audioRef(2), audioRef(9)], (map) => settled.push(map));
+    await flush();
+    assert.deepEqual(settled, [{[audioRef(1)]: 2.2, [audioRef(2)]: 4.5}]);
+  });
+
+  it("a refresh re-reads: cancelling the first read and starting a second shows the new library, and the late first result is discarded", async () => {
+    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
+
+    seed(store, 1, 2.2);
+
+    const first = gatedStore(store);
+    const settled = [];
+    const cancelFirst = startAudioDurationRead(first.store, [audioRef(1)], (map) => settled.push(["first", map]));
+
+    await flush();
+    assert.deepEqual(settled, []);
+
+    // The library changes and the effect re-runs: cleanup, then a new read.
+    seed(store, 1, 3.4);
+    cancelFirst();
+    startAudioDurationRead(store, [audioRef(1)], (map) => settled.push(["second", map]));
+    await flush();
+    assert.deepEqual(settled, [["second", {[audioRef(1)]: 3.4}]]);
+
+    first.release();
+    await flush();
+    assert.deepEqual(settled, [["second", {[audioRef(1)]: 3.4}]]);
+  });
+
+  it("a read that fails settles with the empty map, once", async () => {
+    const settled = [];
+    const broken = {
+      getAssetRow: async () => {
+        throw new Error("the connection was closed");
+      },
+      getPayloadMeta: async () => ({status: "absent"}),
+    };
+
+    startAudioDurationRead(broken, [audioRef(1)], (map) => settled.push(map));
+    await flush();
+    assert.deepEqual(settled, [{}]);
+  });
+
+  it("a failed read that was cancelled settles nothing", async () => {
+    const settled = [];
+    const cancel = startAudioDurationRead(
+      {
+        getAssetRow: async () => {
+          throw new Error("closed");
+        },
+        getPayloadMeta: async () => ({status: "absent"}),
+      },
+      [audioRef(1)],
+      (map) => settled.push(map),
+    );
+
+    cancel();
+    await flush();
+    assert.deepEqual(settled, []);
+  });
+
+  it("no library or no local refs settles the empty map at once and reads nothing", () => {
+    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
+    const settled = [];
+
+    startAudioDurationRead(null, [audioRef(1)], (map) => settled.push(map));
+    startAudioDurationRead(store, [], (map) => settled.push(map));
+    assert.deepEqual(settled, [{}, {}]);
+    assert.equal(store.reads.assets, 0);
+  });
+
+  it("cancelling before the result discards it", async () => {
+    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
+
+    seed(store, 1, 2.2);
+
+    const settled = [];
+    const cancel = startAudioDurationRead(store, [audioRef(1)], (map) => settled.push(map));
+
+    cancel();
+    await flush();
+    assert.deepEqual(settled, []);
+  });
+
+  it("the hook runs it as its effect, restarted when the library, the refs or the refresh token change", () => {
+    const source = readFileSync("src/web/useStoryAudioDurations.ts", "utf8");
+
+    assert.match(source, /startAudioDurationRead\(store, parseLocalAudioRefsKey\(refsKey\)/u);
+    assert.match(source, /\[store, refsKey, refreshToken\]/u);
+  });
+});
```

Append to `tests/browser/audio-editor.spec.mjs` (`installStartCounter` counts every sound the page starts; the routed `ding.wav` bytes drive the store):

```js

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// The Player's bundled audio comes from the page's bundled-audio store (AUDIO-004 task 5)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const DING = "**/audio/sfx/ding.wav";
const dingYaml = (sceneCount = 10) =>
  [
    "title: Bundled ding",
    "scenes:",
    ...Array.from({length: sceneCount}, (_, index) => [
      "  - type: dialogue",
      "    pose: confused",
      "    background: office",
      `    text: Scene ${index + 1}`,
      "    sfx:",
      "      src: ding",
      "    duration: 1",
    ]).flat(),
    "",
  ].join("\n");
const dingRequests = (page) => {
  const urls = [];

  page.on("request", (request) => {
    if (request.url().endsWith("/audio/sfx/ding.wav")) {
      urls.push(request.url());
    }
  });

  return urls;
};
// Counts every sound the page starts (Web Audio sources and media elements), except Remotion's silent pool.
const installStartCounter = () => {
  window.__starts = 0;

  for (const prototype of [AudioBufferSourceNode.prototype, AudioScheduledSourceNode.prototype]) {
    const original = prototype.start;

    prototype.start = function start(...args) {
      window.__starts += 1;

      return original.apply(this, args);
    };
  }

  const originalPlay = HTMLMediaElement.prototype.play;

  HTMLMediaElement.prototype.play = function play(...args) {
    if (!this.src.startsWith("data:")) {
      window.__starts += 1;
    }

    return originalPlay.apply(this, args);
  };
};
const starts = (page) => page.evaluate(() => window.__starts);
const volumeToggle = (page) => page.locator('.preview-frame button[aria-label$=" sound"]');
const unmuteAndPlay = async (page) => {
  await volumeToggle(page).click();
  await expect(volumeToggle(page)).toHaveAttribute("aria-label", "Mute sound");
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Play video"}).click();
};
const importStory = (page, source) =>
  page.locator(".project-toolbar input[type=file]").setInputFiles({
    name: "story.yaml",
    mimeType: "text/yaml",
    buffer: Buffer.from(source),
  });
const armed = (page) =>
  expect(page.locator(".preview-frame")).toHaveAttribute("data-tora-audio-armed", "true");

test("ten scenes using ding fetch /audio/sfx/ding.wav once, and the Player plays it from a blob: URL without another request", async ({page}) => {
  const requests = dingRequests(page);

  await page.addInitScript(installStartCounter);
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await importStory(page, dingYaml());
  await armed(page);
  await expect.poll(() => requests.length).toBe(1);

  await unmuteAndPlay(page);
  // Positive control: the Player does sound its ding once the check passed.
  await expect.poll(() => starts(page), {timeout: 10_000}).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(1);
});

test("a ding file that cannot be decoded is unusable: the Player mounts nothing for it and makes no media request", async ({page}) => {
  const requests = dingRequests(page);

  await page.addInitScript(installStartCounter);
  await page.route(DING, (route) =>
    route.fulfill({status: 200, contentType: "audio/wav", body: "this is not audio"}),
  );
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await importStory(page, dingYaml());
  await armed(page);
  await expect.poll(() => requests.length).toBe(1);

  await unmuteAndPlay(page);
  await expect.poll(() => page.locator(".preview-frame").getAttribute("data-tora-frame")).not.toBe("0");
  await page.waitForTimeout(1_500);
  expect(await starts(page)).toBe(0);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(1);
});

test("an unusable ding does not silence the Story's music, which still plays after unmute", async ({page}) => {
  const requests = dingRequests(page);

  await page.addInitScript(installStartCounter);
  await page.route(DING, (route) =>
    route.fulfill({status: 200, contentType: "audio/wav", body: "this is not audio"}),
  );
  await openApp(page);
  await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload("tone-220-12s.wav"));
  await expect(card(page, "tone-220-12s")).toHaveCount(1);

  const ref = await card(page, "tone-220-12s").getAttribute("data-local-asset-card");

  await importStory(
    page,
    dingYaml(3).replace("scenes:", `music:\n  src: ${ref}\nscenes:`),
  );
  await armed(page);
  await expect.poll(() => requests.length).toBe(1);
  await unmuteAndPlay(page);
  await expect.poll(() => starts(page), {timeout: 10_000}).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(1);
});

test("a failed first request recovers by itself: the retry mounts the track with no navigation, select or Story change", async ({page}) => {
  test.setTimeout(40_000);

  const requests = dingRequests(page);
  let attempts = 0;

  await page.addInitScript(installStartCounter);
  await page.route(DING, (route) => {
    attempts += 1;

    return attempts === 1 ? route.abort("failed") : route.continue();
  });
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await importStory(page, dingYaml(20));
  await armed(page);
  await unmuteAndPlay(page);
  await expect.poll(() => requests.length).toBe(2);
  // The 1 s retry succeeded while the Player was playing: a later scene's ding sounds.
  await expect.poll(() => starts(page), {timeout: 15_000}).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(2);
});

test("while the check is pending the Player plays nothing for ding, and releasing the route with valid bytes mounts it from a blob: URL (no second request)", async ({page}) => {
  const requests = dingRequests(page);
  let release = () => {};
  const gate = new Promise((resolve) => {
    release = resolve;
  });

  await page.addInitScript(installStartCounter);
  await page.route(DING, async (route) => {
    await gate;
    await route.continue();
  });
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await importStory(page, dingYaml(20));
  await armed(page);
  await expect.poll(() => requests.length).toBe(1);
  await unmuteAndPlay(page);
  await expect
    .poll(async () => Number(await page.locator(".preview-frame").getAttribute("data-tora-frame")))
    .toBeGreaterThan(40);
  expect(await starts(page)).toBe(0);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(1);

  release();
  await expect.poll(() => starts(page), {timeout: 15_000}).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(1);
});

test("a held route released with undecodable bytes never mounts ding", async ({page}) => {
  const requests = dingRequests(page);
  let release = () => {};
  const gate = new Promise((resolve) => {
    release = resolve;
  });

  await page.addInitScript(installStartCounter);
  await page.route(DING, async (route) => {
    await gate;
    await route.fulfill({status: 200, contentType: "audio/wav", body: "this is not audio"});
  });
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await importStory(page, dingYaml(20));
  await armed(page);
  await expect.poll(() => requests.length).toBe(1);
  await unmuteAndPlay(page);
  release();
  await expect
    .poll(async () => Number(await page.locator(".preview-frame").getAttribute("data-tora-frame")))
    .toBeGreaterThan(75);
  expect(await starts(page)).toBe(0);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(1);
});

test("when every request fails the store gives up after four requests and the Player never mounts ding", async ({page}) => {
  test.setTimeout(40_000);

  const requests = dingRequests(page);

  await page.addInitScript(installStartCounter);
  await page.route(DING, (route) => route.abort("failed"));
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await importStory(page, dingYaml(3));
  await armed(page);
  // 1 + 3 retries at 1 s, 2 s and 4 s.
  await expect.poll(() => requests.length, {timeout: 15_000}).toBe(4);
  await page.waitForTimeout(1_500);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(4);
  await unmuteAndPlay(page);
  await page.waitForTimeout(1_200);
  expect(await starts(page)).toBe(0);
});
```

`src/web/useStoryAudioDurations.ts` (the effect body becomes `startAudioDurationRead`, same behaviour, now testable):

```diff

+    cancelled = true;
+  };
```

### Task 6: The audio timeline under the Player

**Files:**
- Create: `src/web/audio/audioPlanView.ts`, `src/web/audio/audioTimeline.ts`, `src/web/audio/drawTimelineLane.ts`, `src/web/audio/storyWaveforms.ts`, `src/web/components/AudioTimeline.tsx`
- Modify: `src/web/components/Preview.tsx`, `src/web/App.tsx`, `src/web/styles.css`
- Test: `tests/audio-timeline.test.mjs`, `tests/story-waveforms.test.mjs`, `tests/browser/audio-editor.spec.mjs` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `compileTimeline`, `compileAudioPlan` (`AudioPlan`, `AudioPlanTrack`, `AudioRole`), `resolveAudioSrc`, `audioSourceState`, `readWaveform(store, digest)`, `localAudioRefsKey`, `parseLocalAudioRefsKey`; Task 3's `waveformColumns`, `drawWaveformBars`, `WaveformPeaks`; Task 4's `BundledAudioState`; Task 5's `audioSources`, `bundledStates` in App.
- Produces:
  - `src/web/audio/audioPlanView.ts`: `EditorAudioPlanView = {timeline: Timeline; plan: AudioPlan; fps: number; audioDurations: AudioDurationMap}`; `compileEditorAudioPlan({story, fps, audioDurations, audioSources}): EditorAudioPlanView` (policy `"explicit"`, the same inputs `StoryAudio` uses).
  - `src/web/audio/audioTimeline.ts` (pure layout): `Span`, `frameSpan`, `sceneBlocks`, `trackSpan`, `timelinePlaceholders(view): TimelinePlaceholder[]` (hatched for `missing-audio`, plain for `pending-audio`), `playheadPercent`, `frameFromPointer`, `sceneIndexAtFrame`, `seekTarget(key, frame, timeline): number | null`, `timelineLabel(frame, timeline, fps)`, `sampleEnvelope(envelopeAt, totalFrames, columns): Float32Array`, `laneOfRole`, `bandOfRole`, `roleLabel`.
  - `src/web/audio/drawTimelineLane.ts`: `LaneContext`, `drawMusicLane(context, {view, waveforms, width, height})`, `drawEffectsLane(...)`.
  - `src/web/audio/storyWaveforms.ts`: `StoryWaveforms = ReadonlyMap<AudioRef, WaveformPeaks>`; `readStoryWaveforms(store, refs)`, `startWaveformRead(store | null, refs, settle): () => void`, `sameWaveforms`, `mergeWaveforms(local, bundledStates)`, `useStoryWaveforms(story, library, refreshToken, bundledStates)`.
  - `Preview` props gain `audioPlanView: EditorAudioPlanView` and `waveforms: StoryWaveforms`; `AudioTimeline({view, waveforms, playerRef})`.

`tests/audio-timeline.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {compileAudioPlan} from "../src/audio/plan.ts";
import {audioSourceState, resolveAudioSrc} from "../src/audio/sources.ts";
import {storyAudioDurations} from "../src/audio/storyAudio.ts";
import {compileTimeline} from "../src/story/timeline.ts";
import {compileEditorAudioPlan} from "../src/web/audio/audioPlanView.ts";
import {
  bandOfRole,
  frameFromPointer,
  frameSpan,
  laneOfRole,
  playheadPercent,
  sampleEnvelope,
  sceneBlocks,
  sceneIndexAtFrame,
  seekTarget,
  timelineLabel,
  timelinePlaceholders,
  trackSpan,
} from "../src/web/audio/audioTimeline.ts";
import {
  drawEffectsLane,
  drawMusicLane,
} from "../src/web/audio/drawTimelineLane.ts";

const FPS = 30;
const MUSIC = `local:audio:sha256:${"a".repeat(64)}`;
const VOICE = `local:audio:sha256:${"b".repeat(64)}`;
const scene = (extra = {}) => ({
  type: "dialogue",
  pose: "formal",
  background: "office",
  text: "Hi",
  duration: 3,
  ...extra,
});
const url = (name) => ({kind: "url", url: `blob:http://localhost/${name}`});
const viewFor = (story, {local = {}, sources = {}} = {}) =>
  compileEditorAudioPlan({
    story,
    fps: FPS,
    audioDurations: storyAudioDurations(story, local),
    audioSources: sources,
  });
// Three 3 s scenes (270 frames): a ding at the start of scene 1, a 2.2 s voice in scene 2, 10 s of music with a 1 s fade in.
const fullStory = {
  title: "Timeline",
  music: {src: MUSIC, fadeIn: 1},
  scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}}), scene({voice: {src: VOICE}}), scene()],
};
const fullSources = {ding: url("ding"), [MUSIC]: url("music"), [VOICE]: url("voice")};
const fullView = () => viewFor(fullStory, {local: {[MUSIC]: 10, [VOICE]: 2.2}, sources: fullSources});

/** A recording 2D context. */
const recorder = () => {
  const calls = [];

  return {
    calls,
    context: {
      fillStyle: "",
      strokeStyle: "",
      lineWidth: 1,
      clearRect: (...args) => calls.push(["clear", ...args]),
      fillRect: (...args) => calls.push(["fill", ...args]),
      beginPath: () => calls.push(["begin"]),
      moveTo: (...args) => calls.push(["move", ...args]),
      lineTo: (...args) => calls.push(["line", ...args]),
      stroke: () => calls.push(["stroke"]),
    },
  };
};
const fills = (calls) => calls.filter(([kind]) => kind === "fill");

describe("timeline layout", () => {
  it("frameSpan is the extent in percent of the Story, kept inside it", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(frameSpan(30, 60, 300), {leftPct: 10, widthPct: 20});
    assert.deepEqual(frameSpan(0, 300, 300), {leftPct: 0, widthPct: 100});
    assert.deepEqual(frameSpan(250, 100, 300), {leftPct: (250 / 300) * 100, widthPct: (50 / 300) * 100});
    assert.deepEqual(frameSpan(-20, 40, 300), {leftPct: 0, widthPct: (20 / 300) * 100});
    assert.deepEqual(frameSpan(400, 40, 300), {leftPct: 100, widthPct: 0});
    assert.deepEqual(frameSpan(0, 10, 0), {leftPct: 0, widthPct: 0});
  });

  it("scene blocks are proportional to durationInFrames and carry the scene number", () => {
    const timeline = compileTimeline(
      {title: "T", scenes: [scene({duration: 3}), scene({duration: 2}), scene({duration: 1})]},
      FPS,
      {},
    );
    const blocks = sceneBlocks(timeline);

    assert.deepEqual(blocks.map((block) => block.number), [1, 2, 3]);
    assert.deepEqual(blocks.map((block) => block.leftPct), [0, 50, (150 / 180) * 100]);
    assert.deepEqual(blocks.map((block) => block.widthPct), [50, (60 / 180) * 100, (30 / 180) * 100]);
    assert.equal(blocks.reduce((sum, block) => sum + block.widthPct, 0), 100);
  });

  it("a track's span follows its fromFrame and duration", () => {
    const view = fullView();
    const ding = view.plan.tracks.find((track) => track.key === "sfx-0");
    const voice = view.plan.tracks.find((track) => track.key === "voice-1");

    assert.deepEqual(trackSpan(ding, 270), {leftPct: 0, widthPct: (36 / 270) * 100});
    assert.deepEqual(trackSpan(voice, 270), {leftPct: (90 / 270) * 100, widthPct: (66 / 270) * 100});
  });

  it("the playhead is at frame / total, between 0 and 100", () => {
    assert.equal(playheadPercent(0, 300), 0);
    assert.equal(playheadPercent(150, 300), 50);
    assert.equal(playheadPercent(299, 300), (299 / 300) * 100);
    assert.equal(playheadPercent(-5, 300), 0);
    assert.equal(playheadPercent(900, 300), 100);
    assert.equal(playheadPercent(5, 0), 0);
  });

  it("a pointer maps to a frame, the right edge to the last frame", () => {
    assert.equal(frameFromPointer(0, 400, 300), 0);
    assert.equal(frameFromPointer(200, 400, 300), 150);
    assert.equal(frameFromPointer(399.9, 400, 300), 299);
    assert.equal(frameFromPointer(400, 400, 300), 299);
    assert.equal(frameFromPointer(-30, 400, 300), 0);
    assert.equal(frameFromPointer(10, 0, 300), 0);
  });

  it("roles map to lanes and bands: music alone, sfx and voice share the effects lane", () => {
    assert.equal(laneOfRole("music"), "music");
    assert.equal(laneOfRole("sfx"), "effects");
    assert.equal(laneOfRole("voice"), "effects");
    assert.equal(bandOfRole("voice"), "voice");
  });
});

describe("timeline keyboard and label", () => {
  const timeline = compileTimeline(
    {title: "T", scenes: [scene({duration: 3}), scene({duration: 2}), scene({duration: 1})]},
    FPS,
    {},
  );

  it("ArrowRight goes to the next scene start, then to the last frame", () => {
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowRight", 0, timeline), 90);
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowRight", 89, timeline), 90);
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowRight", 90, timeline), 150);
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowRight", 150, timeline), 179);
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowRight", 179, timeline), 179);
  });

  it("ArrowLeft goes to the previous scene start, then to the first frame", () => {
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowLeft", 179, timeline), 150);
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowLeft", 150, timeline), 90);
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowLeft", 100, timeline), 90);
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowLeft", 90, timeline), 0);
    assert.equal(seekTarget("ArrowLeft", 0, timeline), 0);
  });

  it("Home and End go to the first and last frame; other keys are not handled", () => {
    assert.equal(seekTarget("Home", 100, timeline), 0);
    assert.equal(seekTarget("End", 10, timeline), 179);
    for (const key of ["ArrowUp", "ArrowDown", "Enter", " ", "a", "PageDown"]) {
      assert.equal(seekTarget(key, 10, timeline), null, key);
    }
  });

  it("the scene at a frame is the last one starting at or before it", () => {
    assert.deepEqual([0, 89, 90, 149, 150, 179].map((frame) => sceneIndexAtFrame(timeline, frame)), [0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 2]);
  });

  it("the label announces the current scene and the time", () => {
    assert.equal(timelineLabel(0, timeline, FPS), "Audio timeline, scene 1 of 3, 0:00 of 0:06");
    assert.equal(timelineLabel(100, timeline, FPS), "Audio timeline, scene 2 of 3, 0:03 of 0:06");
    assert.equal(timelineLabel(179, timeline, FPS), "Audio timeline, scene 3 of 3, 0:05 of 0:06");

    const long = compileTimeline({title: "T", scenes: [scene({duration: 125})]}, FPS, {});

    assert.equal(timelineLabel(3_700, long, FPS), "Audio timeline, scene 1 of 1, 2:03 of 2:05");
  });
});

describe("envelope sampling", () => {
  it("reads the plan's envelope once per column, at that column's centre frame", () => {
    const frames = [];
    const samples = sampleEnvelope((frame) => {
      frames.push(frame);

      return frame / 100;
    }, 100, 4);

    assert.deepEqual(frames, [12, 37, 62, 87]);
    assert.deepEqual([...samples], [0.12, 0.37, 0.62, 0.87].map(Math.fround));
  });

  it("never samples more columns than frames, keeps frames inside the Story and values inside 0 to 1", () => {
    const frames = [];
    const samples = sampleEnvelope((frame) => {
      frames.push(frame);

      return frame === 0 ? -3 : 9;
    }, 5, 1_000);

    assert.equal(samples.length, 5);
    assert.deepEqual(frames, [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]);
    assert.deepEqual([...samples], [0, 1, 1, 1, 1]);
    assert.equal(sampleEnvelope(() => 1, 5, 0).length, 0);
    assert.equal(sampleEnvelope(() => 1, 0, 10).length, 0);
    assert.equal(sampleEnvelope(() => 1, 5, Number.NaN).length, 0);
  });

  it("a music fade in rises along the plan's own envelope (no mixing in the UI)", () => {
    const view = viewFor(
      {title: "T", music: {src: MUSIC, fadeIn: 1}, scenes: [scene()]},
      {local: {[MUSIC]: 10}, sources: {[MUSIC]: url("music")}},
    );
    const samples = sampleEnvelope(view.plan.musicEnvelopeAt, 90, 90);

    assert.equal(samples[0] < 0.05, true);
    assert.equal(samples[15] > 0.4 && samples[15] < 0.6, true);
    assert.equal(samples[40], 1);
    assert.equal(samples[0], Math.fround(view.plan.musicEnvelopeAt(0)));
  });

  it("the envelope shows the ducking of a sound effect that the plan computed", () => {
    const view = fullView();
    const samples = sampleEnvelope(view.plan.musicEnvelopeAt, 270, 270);

    // The ding sounds for frames 0 to 36 and the 0.35 duck ramps back up after it.
    assert.equal(samples[30] < 0.4, true);
    assert.equal(samples[60], 1);
  });
});

describe("the editor's plan is the Player's plan", () => {
  it("compiles the very timeline and plan StoryAudio compiles from the same inputs", () => {
    const view = fullView();
    const durations = storyAudioDurations(fullStory, {[MUSIC]: 10, [VOICE]: 2.2});
    const direct = compileAudioPlan({
      story: fullStory,
      timeline: compileTimeline(fullStory, FPS, durations),
      fps: FPS,
      audioDurations: durations,
      sourceState: (ref) => audioSourceState(resolveAudioSrc(ref, fullSources, "explicit")),
    });

    assert.deepEqual(view.timeline, compileTimeline(fullStory, FPS, durations));
    assert.deepEqual(view.plan.tracks.map((track) => [track.key, track.fromFrame, track.durationInFrames]),
      direct.tracks.map((track) => [track.key, track.fromFrame, track.durationInFrames]));
    assert.deepEqual(view.plan.issues, direct.issues);
    assert.equal(view.plan.maxSimultaneousGain, direct.maxSimultaneousGain);
  });

  it("a bundled id without a source entry is pending under the explicit policy", () => {
    const view = viewFor({title: "T", scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}})]});

    assert.deepEqual(view.plan.tracks, []);
    assert.deepEqual(view.plan.issues.map((issue) => issue.kind), ["pending-audio"]);
  });
});

describe("placeholder blocks for sounds without a track", () => {
  it("a missing local voice is a hatched block over its scene, labelled with its role", () => {
    const view = viewFor(
      {title: "T", scenes: [scene(), scene({voice: {src: VOICE}})]},
      {sources: {[VOICE]: {kind: "unavailable"}}},
    );
    const [block, ...rest] = timelinePlaceholders(view);

    assert.equal(rest.length, 0);
    assert.equal(block.appearance, "hatched");
    assert.equal(block.label, "Voice");
    assert.equal(block.lane, "effects");
    assert.equal(block.band, "voice");
    assert.deepEqual([block.leftPct, block.widthPct], [50, 50]);
  });

  it("an unusable or missing bundled sound is hatched, one still loading is plain, both with the catalog length", () => {
    const story = {title: "T", scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}}), scene({sfx: {src: "pop"}})]};
    const view = viewFor(story, {sources: {ding: {kind: "unavailable"}, pop: {kind: "pending"}}});
    const blocks = timelinePlaceholders(view);

    assert.deepEqual(blocks.map((block) => [block.ref, block.appearance, block.label]), [
      ["ding", "hatched", "Sound effect"],
      ["pop", "plain", "Sound effect"],
    ]);
    // ding is 1.2 s = 36 frames of 180; pop is 0.12 s = round(3.6) = 4 frames starting at frame 90.
    assert.deepEqual([blocks[0].leftPct, blocks[0].widthPct], [0, (36 / 180) * 100]);
    assert.deepEqual([blocks[1].leftPct, blocks[1].widthPct], [50, (4 / 180) * 100]);
  });

  it("an sfx block is cut at the Story end and a voice block at its scene end; unknown lengths fall back to the scene", () => {
    const story = {
      title: "T",
      scenes: [scene({duration: 1}), scene({duration: 1, voice: {src: VOICE}, sfx: {src: MUSIC}})],
    };
    const view = viewFor(story, {
      local: {[VOICE]: 5, [MUSIC]: 5},
      sources: {[VOICE]: {kind: "pending"}, [MUSIC]: {kind: "pending"}},
    });
    const byRole = Object.fromEntries(timelinePlaceholders(view).map((block) => [block.role, block]));

    // The Story is 60 frames; scene 2 spans frames 30 to 60.
    assert.deepEqual([byRole.voice.leftPct, byRole.voice.widthPct], [50, 50]);
    assert.deepEqual([byRole.sfx.leftPct, byRole.sfx.widthPct], [50, 50]);

    const unknown = timelinePlaceholders(
      viewFor(story, {sources: {[VOICE]: {kind: "unavailable"}, [MUSIC]: {kind: "unavailable"}}}),
    );

    assert.deepEqual(unknown.map((block) => [block.leftPct, block.widthPct]), [[50, 50], [50, 50]]);
  });

  it("a placeholder has exactly the span its track will have once the sound is ready (the plan's own clamps)", () => {
    // ding (1.2 s) runs past its 1 s scene, pop is 0.12 s (4 frames), the 5 s voice is cut at its scene end,
    // and boom (0.8 s) is cut at the end of a Story that stops 6 frames after it starts.
    const story = {
      title: "T",
      scenes: [
        scene({duration: 1, sfx: {src: "ding"}}),
        scene({duration: 1, sfx: {src: "pop"}}),
        scene({duration: 1, voice: {src: VOICE}}),
        scene({duration: 0.2, sfx: {src: "boom"}}),
      ],
    };
    const refs = ["ding", "pop", "boom", VOICE];
    const waiting = viewFor(story, {
      local: {[VOICE]: 5},
      sources: Object.fromEntries(refs.map((ref) => [ref, {kind: "pending"}])),
    });
    const ready = viewFor(story, {
      local: {[VOICE]: 5},
      sources: Object.fromEntries(refs.map((ref) => [ref, url("x")])),
    });
    const blocks = timelinePlaceholders(waiting);

    assert.equal(blocks.length, 4);

    for (const block of blocks) {
      const track = ready.plan.tracks.find((candidate) => candidate.key === `${block.role}-${block.sceneIndex}`);

      assert.ok(track, block.key);
      assert.deepEqual(
        [block.leftPct, block.widthPct],
        [trackSpan(track, ready.timeline.durationInFrames).leftPct, trackSpan(track, ready.timeline.durationInFrames).widthPct],
        block.key,
      );
    }
  });

  it("missing music spans the whole Story in the music lane", () => {
    const view = viewFor(
      {title: "T", music: {src: "calm-loop"}, scenes: [scene(), scene()]},
      {sources: {"calm-loop": {kind: "unavailable"}}},
    );
    const [block] = timelinePlaceholders(view);

    assert.deepEqual([block.lane, block.role, block.appearance, block.leftPct, block.widthPct], ["music", "music", "hatched", 0, 100]);
  });

  it("other plan issues draw no block and a Story with every sound ready has none", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(timelinePlaceholders(fullView()), []);
    assert.deepEqual(timelinePlaceholders(viewFor({title: "T", scenes: [scene()]})), []);
  });
});

describe("lane drawing", () => {
  const peaks = {peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: Uint8Array.from({length: 600}, () => 200)};
  const waveforms = new Map([["ding", peaks], [MUSIC, peaks], [VOICE, peaks]]);

  it("draws a sound effect in the upper band at its fromFrame and a voice in the lower band, clipped to the track", () => {
    const view = fullView();
    const {calls, context} = recorder();

    drawEffectsLane(context, {view, waveforms, width: 270, height: 64});

    const bars = fills(calls);
    const ding = bars.filter(([, , y, , h]) => y + h <= 32 + 1e-9);
    const voice = bars.filter(([, , y]) => y >= 32 - 1e-9 && y < 64);

    assert.equal(ding.length + voice.length, bars.length);
    // ding: frames 0 to 36 of 270 on a 270 px canvas = x 0 to 36; voice: frames 90 to 156.
    assert.equal(Math.min(...ding.map(([, x]) => x)), 0);
    assert.equal(Math.max(...ding.map(([, x, , w]) => x + w)) <= 36.5, true);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(Math.min(...voice.map(([, x]) => x)) - 90) < 1e-6);
    assert.equal(Math.max(...voice.map(([, x, , w]) => x + w)) <= 156.5, true);
    assert.equal(calls[0][0], "clear");
  });

  it("draws the music waveform per plan copy and the plan's envelope as one line with one point per column", () => {
    const story = {title: "T", music: {src: MUSIC, fadeIn: 1}, scenes: [scene({duration: 20})]};
    const view = viewFor(story, {local: {[MUSIC]: 10}, sources: {[MUSIC]: url("music")}});
    const {calls, context} = recorder();

    // 10 s of music in a 20 s Story (600 frames) loops: three copies, at frames 0, 291 and 582.
    assert.equal(view.plan.tracks.filter((track) => track.role === "music").length, 3);
    drawMusicLane(context, {view, waveforms, width: 300, height: 48});

    const line = calls.filter(([kind]) => kind === "move" || kind === "line");

    assert.equal(line.length, 300);
    assert.equal(calls.filter(([kind]) => kind === "stroke").length, 1);
    // The fade in starts silent (line at the bottom) and reaches full level (top).
    assert.equal(line[0][2] > 40, true);
    assert.equal(Math.min(...line.map(([, , y]) => y)), 0);
    assert.equal(Math.max(...fills(calls).map(([, x, , w]) => x + w)) <= 300.5, true);
  });

  it("draws no envelope line when the music has no track", () => {
    const view = viewFor(
      {title: "T", music: {src: MUSIC}, scenes: [scene()]},
      {sources: {[MUSIC]: {kind: "pending"}}},
    );
    const {calls, context} = recorder();

    drawMusicLane(context, {view, waveforms, width: 200, height: 48});
    assert.deepEqual(calls.map(([kind]) => kind), ["clear"]);
  });

  it("draws a track whose peaks are unknown as a quiet flat bar, and nothing for a lane without sounds", () => {
    const view = fullView();
    const {calls, context} = recorder();

    drawEffectsLane(context, {view, waveforms: new Map(), width: 270, height: 64});
    assert.equal(fills(calls).length > 0, true);
    assert.equal(fills(calls).every(([, , , , height]) => height <= 32 * 0.1), true);

    const silent = recorder();

    drawEffectsLane(silent.context, {view: viewFor({title: "T", scenes: [scene()]}), waveforms, width: 270, height: 64});
    assert.deepEqual(silent.calls.map(([kind]) => kind), ["clear"]);
  });

  it("drawing is a pure function of the plan: the same inputs record the same calls", () => {
    const view = fullView();
    const first = recorder();
    const second = recorder();

    drawMusicLane(first.context, {view, waveforms, width: 321, height: 48});
    drawMusicLane(second.context, {view, waveforms, width: 321, height: 48});
    assert.deepEqual(first.calls, second.calls);
  });
});
```

`tests/story-waveforms.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {readFileSync} from "node:fs";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {
  mergeWaveforms,
  readStoryWaveforms,
  sameWaveforms,
  startWaveformRead,
} from "../src/web/audio/storyWaveforms.ts";
import {createMemoryAssetStore} from "./helpers/memoryAssetStore.mjs";

const digestOf = (n) => n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0");
const audioRef = (n) => `local:audio:sha256:${digestOf(n)}`;
const peaksOf = (...values) => ({peaksPerSecond: 50, peaks: Uint8Array.from(values)});
const flush = async () => {
  for (let index = 0; index < 20; index += 1) {
    await Promise.resolve();
  }
};

describe("readStoryWaveforms", () => {
  it("returns the stored peaks of exactly the asked refs and leaves out absent or corrupt ones", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(1), peaksOf(1, 2, 3));
    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(2), {peaksPerSecond: 0, peaks: Uint8Array.from([1])});
    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(4), peaksOf(9));

    const waveforms = await readStoryWaveforms(store, [audioRef(1), audioRef(2), audioRef(3), audioRef(1)]);

    assert.deepEqual([...waveforms.keys()], [audioRef(1)]);
    assert.deepEqual(waveforms.get(audioRef(1)), peaksOf(1, 2, 3));
  });

  it("never lets a record under one digest answer for another (identity is the key)", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(1), {...peaksOf(1), digest: digestOf(2)});
    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(2), peaksOf(2));

    const waveforms = await readStoryWaveforms(store, [audioRef(1), audioRef(2)]);

    assert.deepEqual([...waveforms.keys()], [audioRef(2)]);
  });
});

describe("startWaveformRead (the hook's effect)", () => {
  it("settles once with the Story's peaks; a cancelled read settles nothing; a failed read settles the empty map", async () => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();

    store.setRaw("waveforms", digestOf(1), peaksOf(5, 6));

    const settled = [];

    startWaveformRead(store, [audioRef(1)], (map) => settled.push(map));
    await flush();
    assert.equal(settled.length, 1);
    assert.deepEqual(settled[0].get(audioRef(1)), peaksOf(5, 6));

    const late = [];
    const cancel = startWaveformRead(store, [audioRef(1)], (map) => late.push(map));

    cancel();
    await flush();
    assert.deepEqual(late, []);

    const failed = [];

    startWaveformRead(
      {getWaveform: async () => { throw new Error("closed"); }},
      [audioRef(1)],
      (map) => failed.push(map),
    );
    await flush();
    assert.equal(failed.length, 1);
    assert.equal(failed[0].size, 0);
  });

  it("no library or no local refs settles the empty map at once", () => {
    const settled = [];

    startWaveformRead(null, [audioRef(1)], (map) => settled.push(map));
    startWaveformRead(createMemoryAssetStore(), [], (map) => settled.push(map));
    assert.deepEqual(settled.map((map) => map.size), [0, 0]);
  });

  it("the hook restarts it when the library, the refs or the refresh token change", () => {
    const source = readFileSync("src/web/audio/storyWaveforms.ts", "utf8");

    assert.match(source, /startWaveformRead\(store, parseLocalAudioRefsKey\(refsKey\)/u);
    assert.match(source, /\[store, refsKey, refreshToken\]/u);
  });
});

describe("waveform maps", () => {
  it("sameWaveforms compares by content, so a re-read of an unchanged library keeps the map", () => {
    const a = new Map([[audioRef(1), peaksOf(1, 2, 3)]]);

    assert.equal(sameWaveforms(a, new Map([[audioRef(1), peaksOf(1, 2, 3)]])), true);
    assert.equal(sameWaveforms(a, new Map([[audioRef(1), peaksOf(1, 2, 4)]])), false);
    assert.equal(sameWaveforms(a, new Map([[audioRef(1), peaksOf(1, 2)]])), false);
    assert.equal(sameWaveforms(a, new Map([[audioRef(2), peaksOf(1, 2, 3)]])), false);
    assert.equal(sameWaveforms(a, new Map()), false);
    assert.equal(sameWaveforms(new Map(), new Map()), true);
    assert.equal(
      sameWaveforms(a, new Map([[audioRef(1), {peaksPerSecond: 25, peaks: Uint8Array.from([1, 2, 3])}]])),
      false,
    );
  });

  it("merges the bundled store's ready peaks with the local ones and ignores every other state", () => {
    const local = new Map([[audioRef(1), peaksOf(1)]]);
    const merged = mergeWaveforms(local, new Map([
      ["ding", {kind: "ready", peaks: peaksOf(7), blob: new Blob([]), url: "blob:a"}],
      ["pop", {kind: "loading"}],
      ["boom", {kind: "unusable", message: "x"}],
      ["whoosh", {kind: "fetch-failed", message: "x"}],
    ]));

    assert.deepEqual([...merged.keys()].sort(), [audioRef(1), "ding"].sort());
    assert.deepEqual(merged.get("ding"), peaksOf(7));
    assert.equal(local.size, 1, "the local map is not changed");
  });
});
```

```js

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// The audio timeline under the Player (AUDIO-004 task 6)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const timeline = (page) => page.locator("[data-audio-timeline]");
const lane = (page, name) => page.locator(`[data-timeline-canvas="${name}"]`);
const paintedPixels = (canvas) =>
  canvas.evaluate((element) => {
    const {data} = element.getContext("2d").getImageData(0, 0, element.width, element.height);
    let painted = 0;

    for (let index = 3; index < data.length; index += 4) {
      if (data[index] > 0) {
        painted += 1;
      }
    }

    return painted;
  });
const frameOf = async (page) => Number(await page.locator(".preview-frame").getAttribute("data-tora-frame"));
const sceneStory = (extras = {}) =>
  [
    "title: Timeline fixture",
    ...(extras.music === undefined ? [] : ["music:", `  src: ${extras.music}`, ...(extras.musicTail ?? [])]),
    "scenes:",
    "  - type: intro",
    "    pose: formal",
    "    background: office",
    "    text: One",
    "    sfx:",
    "      src: ding",
    "    duration: 2",
    "  - type: dialogue",
    "    pose: formal",
    "    background: office",
    "    text: Two",
    ...(extras.voice === undefined ? [] : ["    voice:", `      src: ${extras.voice}`]),
    "    duration: 2",
    "  - type: chaos",
    "    pose: panic",
    "    background: office",
    "    text: Three",
    "    duration: 2",
    "",
  ].join("\n");

test("a Story with sfx ding draws a non-empty effects-lane waveform with no library import, and a silent Story has no timeline", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveCount(0);

  await importStory(page, sceneStory());
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-audio-tracks", "sfx-0");
  await expect(timeline(page).locator("[data-timeline-scene]")).toHaveCount(3);
  await expect.poll(() => paintedPixels(lane(page, "effects"))).toBeGreaterThan(50);
  expect(await paintedPixels(lane(page, "music"))).toBe(0);
  await expect(page.locator("[data-timeline-placeholder]")).toHaveCount(0);

  await importStory(page, "title: Quiet\nscenes:\n  - type: intro\n    pose: formal\n    background: office\n    text: Quiet\n    duration: 2\n");
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveCount(0);
});

test("scene blocks are proportional to their length and carry the scene number", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await importStory(
    page,
    sceneStory().replace("    duration: 2\n  - type: dialogue", "    duration: 4\n  - type: dialogue"),
  );

  const blocks = timeline(page).locator("[data-timeline-scene]");

  await expect(blocks).toHaveCount(3);
  await expect(blocks.nth(0)).toHaveText("1");
  await expect(blocks.nth(2)).toHaveText("3");

  const widths = await blocks.evaluateAll((elements) =>
    elements.map((element) => element.getBoundingClientRect().width),
  );

  // 4 s : 2 s : 2 s.
  expect(widths[0] / widths[1]).toBeCloseTo(2, 1);
  expect(widths[1] / widths[2]).toBeCloseTo(1, 1);
});

test("clicking the timeline seeks the Player and moves the playhead", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await importStory(page, sceneStory());
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveCount(1);

  const box = await timeline(page).boundingBox();

  await page.mouse.click(box.x + box.width * 0.5, box.y + 30);
  // 180 frames: the middle is frame 90, the start of scene 2.
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBe(90);
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuenow", "90");

  const playheadLeft = await page.locator("[data-timeline-playhead]").evaluate((element) => element.style.left);

  expect(Number.parseFloat(playheadLeft)).toBeCloseTo(50, 0);

  await page.mouse.click(box.x + box.width - 1, box.y + 30);
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBe(179);
});

test("the timeline is one focusable slider: arrows jump between scene starts, Home and End to the ends, and the label says where it is", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await importStory(page, sceneStory());
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("role", "slider");
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("tabindex", "0");
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("aria-label", "Audio timeline, scene 1 of 3, 0:00 of 0:06");

  await timeline(page).focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBe(60);
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("aria-label", "Audio timeline, scene 2 of 3, 0:02 of 0:06");
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBe(120);
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBe(179);
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowLeft");
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBe(120);
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowLeft");
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowLeft");
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBe(0);
  await page.keyboard.press("End");
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBe(179);
  await page.keyboard.press("Home");
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBe(0);
});

test("while the Player plays only the playhead moves: the lanes are not redrawn per frame", async ({page}) => {
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    window.__laneDraws = 0;

    const original = CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype.fillRect;

    CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype.fillRect = function fillRect(...args) {
      window.__laneDraws += 1;

      return original.apply(this, args);
    };
  });
  await openApp(page);
  await importStory(page, sceneStory());
  await expect.poll(() => paintedPixels(lane(page, "effects"))).toBeGreaterThan(50);
  await page.waitForTimeout(300);

  const draws = await page.evaluate(() => window.__laneDraws);
  const before = await page.locator("[data-timeline-playhead]").evaluate((element) => element.style.left);

  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Play video"}).click();
  await expect.poll(() => frameOf(page)).toBeGreaterThan(30);
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Pause video"}).click();

  const after = await page.locator("[data-timeline-playhead]").evaluate((element) => element.style.left);

  expect(Number.parseFloat(after)).toBeGreaterThan(Number.parseFloat(before));
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.__laneDraws)).toBe(draws);
});

test("the lanes follow a resize of the page", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);
  await importStory(page, sceneStory());
  await expect.poll(() => paintedPixels(lane(page, "effects"))).toBeGreaterThan(50);

  const width = () => lane(page, "effects").evaluate((element) => element.width);

  await expect.poll(width).toBeLessThan(wide);

});

test("an unusable ding shows a hatched block with the role label instead of a waveform, and a track that is still loading a plain one", async ({page}) => {
  const requests = dingRequests(page);

  await expect(block).toHaveAttribute("data-timeline-role", "sfx");

test("a ding whose first request fails shows a plain block that turns into a waveform by itself", async ({page}) => {
  test.setTimeout(30_000);

  let attempts = 0;

  await page.route(DING, (route) => {
    attempts += 1;

  });

  await expect(page.locator("[data-timeline-placeholder]")).toHaveAttribute("data-timeline-placeholder", "plain");
  await expect(page.locator("[data-timeline-placeholder]")).toHaveCount(0, {timeout: 10_000});
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-audio-tracks", "sfx-0");
  await expect.poll(() => paintedPixels(lane(page, "effects"))).toBeGreaterThan(50);
});

test("music and voice draw in their lanes from the stored peaks, and deleting the voice from My audio turns it into a hatched block while ding stays", async ({page}) => {

  await expect(card(page, "tone-440-2.2s")).toHaveCount(0);

  const hatched = page.locator('[data-timeline-placeholder="hatched"]');

  await expect(hatched).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(hatched).toHaveText("Voice");
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-audio-tracks", "music-0 sfx-0");
  await expect.poll(() => paintedPixels(lane(page, "effects"))).toBeGreaterThan(20);
```

`src/web/audio/audioPlanView.ts`:

```ts
import {compileAudioPlan, type AudioPlan} from "../../audio/plan.ts";
import type {AudioDurationMap} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import {
  audioSourceState,
  resolveAudioSrc,
  type AudioSourceMap,
} from "../../audio/sources.ts";
import {compileTimeline, type Timeline} from "../../story/timeline.ts";
import type {Story} from "../../story/types.ts";

/** The timeline and the audio plan the editor shows (the Player's own, recompiled from the same inputs). */
export type EditorAudioPlanView = {
  timeline: Timeline;
  plan: AudioPlan;
  fps: number;
  audioDurations: AudioDurationMap;
};

/**
 * Compiles, for the editor's timeline and warnings, exactly what `StoryAudio`
 * compiles inside the Player: the same Story, the same duration map, the same
 * source map and the `"explicit"` policy. The UI only reads the plan; it never
 * recomputes mixing (AINV-4).
 */
export const compileEditorAudioPlan = (input: {
  story: Story;
  fps: number;
  audioDurations: AudioDurationMap;
  audioSources: AudioSourceMap;
}): EditorAudioPlanView => {
  const {story, fps, audioDurations, audioSources} = input;
  const timeline = compileTimeline(story, fps, audioDurations);
  const plan = compileAudioPlan({
    story,
    timeline,
    fps,
    audioDurations,
    sourceState: (ref) =>
      audioSourceState(resolveAudioSrc(ref, audioSources, "explicit")),
  });

  return {timeline, plan, fps, audioDurations};
};
```

`src/web/audio/audioTimeline.ts`:

```ts
import type {AudioRef} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import type {AudioPlanTrack, AudioRole} from "../../audio/plan.ts";
import type {Timeline} from "../../story/timeline.ts";
import type {EditorAudioPlanView} from "./audioPlanView.ts";

/** A horizontal extent as percentages of the whole Story length. */
export type Span = {leftPct: number; widthPct: number};

const clamp = (value: number, low: number, high: number): number =>
  Math.min(high, Math.max(low, value));

/** `fromFrame` and `durationInFrames` as percentages of `totalFrames`, kept inside the Story. */
export const frameSpan = (
  fromFrame: number,
  durationInFrames: number,
  totalFrames: number,
): Span => {
  if (totalFrames <= 0) {
    return {leftPct: 0, widthPct: 0};
  }

  const left = clamp(fromFrame, 0, totalFrames);
  const right = clamp(fromFrame + durationInFrames, left, totalFrames);

  return {
    leftPct: (left / totalFrames) * 100,
    widthPct: ((right - left) / totalFrames) * 100,
  };
};

export type SceneBlock = Span & {
  index: number;
  /** 1-based, as the scene list shows it. */
  number: number;
};

/** One block per scene, proportional to its `durationInFrames`. */
export const sceneBlocks = (timeline: Timeline): SceneBlock[] =>
  timeline.scenes.map((scene, index) => ({
    index,
    number: index + 1,
    ...frameSpan(scene.from, scene.durationInFrames, timeline.durationInFrames),
  }));

export type TimelineLane = "music" | "effects";
/** Effects and voices share the effects lane in two bands so they never overlap each other. */
export type TimelineBand = "music" | "sfx" | "voice";

export const bandOfRole = (role: AudioRole): TimelineBand => role;
export const laneOfRole = (role: AudioRole): TimelineLane =>
  role === "music" ? "music" : "effects";

export const roleLabel = (role: AudioRole): string =>
  role === "music" ? "Music" : role === "sfx" ? "Sound effect" : "Voice";

export const trackSpan = (track: AudioPlanTrack, totalFrames: number): Span =>
  frameSpan(track.fromFrame, track.durationInFrames, totalFrames);

export type TimelinePlaceholder = Span & {
  key: string;
  band: TimelineBand;
  lane: TimelineLane;
  role: AudioRole;
  ref: AudioRef;
  sceneIndex: number | null;
  /** Missing or unusable audio is hatched; audio that is still loading is plain. */
  appearance: "hatched" | "plain";
  label: string;
};

const usableSeconds = (seconds: number | undefined): seconds is number =>
  typeof seconds === "number" && Number.isFinite(seconds) && seconds > 0;

/**
 * One block for every sound the plan could not emit a track for: `missing-audio`
 * is hatched, `pending-audio` is plain, both carry the role label. A block has
 * the length the track would have (the plan's own sfx and voice clamps) when
 * the sound's duration is known, otherwise its scene's length; the music spans
 * the Story.
 */
export const timelinePlaceholders = (
  view: EditorAudioPlanView,
): TimelinePlaceholder[] => {
  const {timeline, plan, fps, audioDurations} = view;
  const total = timeline.durationInFrames;

  return plan.issues.flatMap((issue): TimelinePlaceholder[] => {
    if (issue.kind !== "missing-audio" && issue.kind !== "pending-audio") {
      return [];
    }

    const scene =
      issue.sceneIndex === null ? undefined : timeline.scenes[issue.sceneIndex];
    let from = 0;
    let length = total;

    if (issue.sceneIndex !== null) {
      if (scene === undefined) {
        return [];
      }

      const seconds = audioDurations[issue.ref];
      const known = usableSeconds(seconds)
        ? Math.max(1, Math.round(seconds * fps))
        : scene.durationInFrames;

      from = scene.from;
      length =
        issue.role === "voice"
          ? Math.min(known, scene.durationInFrames)
          : Math.min(known, total - scene.from);
    }

    return [
      {
        key: `${issue.kind}:${issue.role}:${issue.sceneIndex ?? "story"}`,
        band: bandOfRole(issue.role),
        lane: laneOfRole(issue.role),
        role: issue.role,
        ref: issue.ref,
        sceneIndex: issue.sceneIndex,
        appearance: issue.kind === "missing-audio" ? "hatched" : "plain",
        label: roleLabel(issue.role),
        ...frameSpan(from, length, total),
      },
    ];
  });
};

/** The playhead's left edge, in percent of the Story length. */
export const playheadPercent = (frame: number, totalFrames: number): number =>
  totalFrames <= 0 ? 0 : clamp((frame / totalFrames) * 100, 0, 100);

/** The frame under a pointer `offsetX` pixels from the left of a `width` px wide timeline. */
export const frameFromPointer = (
  offsetX: number,
  width: number,
  totalFrames: number,
): number =>
  width <= 0 || totalFrames <= 0
    ? 0
    : clamp(Math.floor((offsetX / width) * totalFrames), 0, totalFrames - 1);

/** The scene that contains `frame` (the last one starting at or before it). */
export const sceneIndexAtFrame = (timeline: Timeline, frame: number): number => {
  let index = 0;

  for (const [candidate, scene] of timeline.scenes.entries()) {
    if (scene.from <= frame) {
      index = candidate;
    }
  }

  return index;
};

/**
 * Where a key moves the playhead, or null when the key is not a timeline key.
 * `ArrowRight` goes to the next scene start (the last frame when there is none),
 * `ArrowLeft` to the previous scene start (the first frame when there is none),
 * `Home` and `End` to the first and last frame.
 */
export const seekTarget = (
  key: string,
  frame: number,
  timeline: Timeline,
): number | null => {
  const last = Math.max(0, timeline.durationInFrames - 1);

  switch (key) {
    case "Home":
      return 0;
    case "End":
      return last;
    case "ArrowRight": {
      const next = timeline.scenes.find((scene) => scene.from > frame);

      return next === undefined ? last : next.from;
    }
    case "ArrowLeft": {
      const previous = [...timeline.scenes]
        .reverse()
        .find((scene) => scene.from < frame);

      return previous === undefined ? 0 : previous.from;
    }
    default:
      return null;
  }
};

const clock = (seconds: number): string => {
  const whole = Math.max(0, Math.floor(seconds));

  return `${Math.floor(whole / 60)}:${String(whole % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
};

/** What a screen reader hears: the current scene and the time, `Audio timeline, scene 2 of 3, 0:03 of 0:09`. */
export const timelineLabel = (
  frame: number,
  timeline: Timeline,
  fps: number,
): string =>
  `Audio timeline, scene ${sceneIndexAtFrame(timeline, frame) + 1} of ${timeline.scenes.length}, ${clock(frame / fps)} of ${clock(timeline.durationInFrames / fps)}`;

/**
 * The music envelope for display, sampled ONCE per column: `columns` values
 * (never more than one per frame) at the centre frame of each column. The
 * envelope is the plan's `musicEnvelopeAt`; this only reads it.
 */
export const sampleEnvelope = (
  envelopeAt: (frame: number) => number,
  totalFrames: number,
  columns: number,
): Float32Array => {
  const count = Math.min(
    Number.isFinite(columns) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(columns)) : 0,
    Math.max(0, totalFrames),
  );
  const samples = new Float32Array(count);

  for (let index = 0; index < count; index += 1) {
    const frame = Math.min(
      totalFrames - 1,
      Math.floor(((index + 0.5) * totalFrames) / count),
    );

    samples[index] = clamp(envelopeAt(frame), 0, 1);
  }

  return samples;
};
```

`src/web/audio/drawTimelineLane.ts` (waveform per plan copy, the plan's envelope as one line sampled once per column; nothing is mixed here):

```ts
import type {AudioPlanTrack} from "../../audio/plan.ts";
import type {EditorAudioPlanView} from "./audioPlanView.ts";
import {sampleEnvelope, trackSpan} from "./audioTimeline.ts";
import {drawWaveformBars, type WaveformContext} from "./drawWaveform.ts";
import {waveformColumns, type WaveformPeaks} from "./waveformColumns.ts";

/** The canvas members the lanes use (a recording fake in Node tests). */
export type LaneContext = WaveformContext & {
  strokeStyle: string | CanvasGradient | CanvasPattern;
  lineWidth: number;
  beginPath: () => void;
  moveTo: (x: number, y: number) => void;
  lineTo: (x: number, y: number) => void;
  stroke: () => void;
};

export const MUSIC_WAVEFORM_COLOR = "#64748b";
export const MUSIC_ENVELOPE_COLOR = "#f8fafc";
export const SFX_WAVEFORM_COLOR = "#f59e0b";
export const VOICE_WAVEFORM_COLOR = "#38bdf8";

/** A track whose peaks are not available is drawn as a quiet flat bar, so it is still visibly there. */
const PLACEHOLDER_LEVEL = 0.08;

type LaneInput = {
  view: EditorAudioPlanView;
  waveforms: ReadonlyMap<string, WaveformPeaks>;
  /** CSS pixels. */
  width: number;
  height: number;
};

const drawTrack = (
  context: LaneContext,
  track: AudioPlanTrack,
  input: LaneInput,
  area: {y: number; height: number},
  color: string,
) => {
  const {view, waveforms, width} = input;
  const span = trackSpan(track, view.timeline.durationInFrames);
  const x = (span.leftPct / 100) * width;
  const trackWidth = Math.max(1, (span.widthPct / 100) * width);
  const columns = Math.max(1, Math.round(trackWidth));
  const peaks = waveforms.get(track.ref);
  const values =
    peaks === undefined
      ? new Float32Array(columns).fill(PLACEHOLDER_LEVEL)
      : waveformColumns(peaks, track.durationInFrames / view.fps, columns);

  drawWaveformBars(
    context,
    values,
    {x, y: area.y, width: trackWidth, height: area.height},
    color,
  );
};

/**
 * The music lane: every music copy of the plan with its waveform (each copy
 * from the start of the sound, at its own `fromFrame`), then the plan's gain
 * envelope (`musicEnvelopeAt`: fades x ducking, no crossfade factors) as a
 * line, sampled once per column. Nothing is mixed here (AINV-4).
 */
export const drawMusicLane = (context: LaneContext, input: LaneInput): void => {
  const {view, width, height} = input;
  const copies = view.plan.tracks.filter((track) => track.role === "music");

  context.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

  for (const track of copies) {
    drawTrack(context, track, input, {y: 0, height}, MUSIC_WAVEFORM_COLOR);
  }

  const envelope = view.plan.musicEnvelopeAt;

  if (envelope === null || copies.length === 0 || width < 1) {
    return;
  }

  const samples = sampleEnvelope(
    envelope,
    view.timeline.durationInFrames,
    Math.round(width),
  );

  context.strokeStyle = MUSIC_ENVELOPE_COLOR;
  context.lineWidth = 1.5;
  context.beginPath();

  for (const [index, gain] of samples.entries()) {
    const x = ((index + 0.5) / samples.length) * width;
    const y = height - gain * height;

    if (index === 0) {
      context.moveTo(x, y);
    } else {
      context.lineTo(x, y);
    }
  }

  context.stroke();
};

/** The effects/voice lane: sound effects in the upper half, voices in the lower half, each at its `fromFrame`. */
export const drawEffectsLane = (context: LaneContext, input: LaneInput): void => {
  const {view, width, height} = input;
  const half = height / 2;

  context.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

  for (const track of view.plan.tracks) {
    if (track.role === "sfx") {
      drawTrack(context, track, input, {y: 0, height: half}, SFX_WAVEFORM_COLOR);
    } else if (track.role === "voice") {
      drawTrack(context, track, input, {y: half, height: half}, VOICE_WAVEFORM_COLOR);
    }
  }
};
```

`src/web/audio/storyWaveforms.ts`:

```ts
import {useEffect, useMemo, useState} from "react";
import type {AudioRef, BundledAudio} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import {parseLocalAssetRef, type LocalAudioRef} from "../../localAssets/refs.ts";
import type {Story} from "../../story/types.ts";
import type {AssetLibraryStatus} from "../assetLibrary/coordination.ts";
import {readWaveform} from "../assetLibrary/library.ts";
import type {AssetLibraryStore} from "../assetLibrary/store.ts";
import {localAudioRefsKey, parseLocalAudioRefsKey} from "../useStoryAudioDurations.ts";
import type {BundledAudioState} from "./bundledAudio.ts";
import type {WaveformPeaks} from "./waveformColumns.ts";

/** The peaks of every sound of the Story that has some, by ref. */
export type StoryWaveforms = ReadonlyMap<AudioRef, WaveformPeaks>;

const NO_WAVEFORMS: StoryWaveforms = new Map();

/**
 * The stored peaks (`readWaveform(store, digest)`, AUDIO-003) of exactly these
 * local refs. A ref with no stored or readable waveform is simply left out.
 */
export const readStoryWaveforms = async (
  store: Pick<AssetLibraryStore, "getWaveform">,
  refs: Iterable<LocalAudioRef>,
): Promise<Map<LocalAudioRef, WaveformPeaks>> => {
  const waveforms = new Map<LocalAudioRef, WaveformPeaks>();

  for (const ref of new Set(refs)) {
    const parsed = parseLocalAssetRef(ref);
    const record =
      parsed === null ? null : await readWaveform(store, parsed.digest);

    if (record !== null) {
      waveforms.set(ref, record);
    }
  }

  return waveforms;
};

/**
 * One read for the effect of `useStoryWaveforms`; the same contract as
 * `startAudioDurationRead`: `settle` runs once unless the returned cancel ran
 * first, and a failed read or no library settles the empty map.
 */
export const startWaveformRead = (
  store: Pick<AssetLibraryStore, "getWaveform"> | null,
  refs: readonly LocalAudioRef[],
  settle: (waveforms: StoryWaveforms) => void,
): (() => void) => {
  let cancelled = false;
  const deliver = (next: StoryWaveforms) => {
    if (!cancelled) {
      settle(next);
    }
  };

  if (store === null || refs.length === 0) {
    deliver(NO_WAVEFORMS);
  } else {
    void readStoryWaveforms(store, refs).then(deliver, () =>
      deliver(NO_WAVEFORMS),
    );
  }

  return () => {
    cancelled = true;
  };
};

const samePeaks = (a: WaveformPeaks, b: WaveformPeaks): boolean =>
  a.peaksPerSecond === b.peaksPerSecond &&
  a.peaks.length === b.peaks.length &&
  a.peaks.every((value, index) => value === b.peaks[index]);

/** Entry-wise equality by content, so an unchanged library keeps the previous map (and the canvases are not redrawn). */
export const sameWaveforms = (a: StoryWaveforms, b: StoryWaveforms): boolean =>
  a.size === b.size &&
  [...a].every(([ref, peaks]) => {
    const other = b.get(ref);

    return other !== undefined && samePeaks(peaks, other);
  });

/** The local peaks plus the peaks of every bundled id the store has checked (`ready`). */
export const mergeWaveforms = (
  local: StoryWaveforms,
  bundled: ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState>,
): StoryWaveforms => {
  const merged = new Map<AudioRef, WaveformPeaks>(local);

  for (const [id, state] of bundled) {
    if (state.kind === "ready") {
      merged.set(id, state.peaks);
    }
  }

  return merged;
};

/**
 * The waveforms the timeline draws: local peaks read from the library for the
 * Story's own local refs (again when the library changes), bundled peaks from
 * the bundled-audio store.
 */
export const useStoryWaveforms = (
  story: Story,
  library: AssetLibraryStatus | null,
  refreshToken: number,
  bundled: ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState>,
): StoryWaveforms => {
  const refsKey = localAudioRefsKey(story);
  const store = library?.kind === "ready" ? library.store : null;
  const [local, setLocal] = useState<StoryWaveforms>(NO_WAVEFORMS);

  useEffect(
    () =>
      startWaveformRead(store, parseLocalAudioRefsKey(refsKey), (next) =>
        setLocal((current) => (sameWaveforms(current, next) ? current : next)),
      ),
    [store, refsKey, refreshToken],
  );

  return useMemo(() => mergeWaveforms(local, bundled), [local, bundled]);
};
```

`src/web/components/AudioTimeline.tsx` (one canvas per lane, redrawn only when the Story, the plan, the waveforms or the width change; only the playhead and the `aria-*` values move per Player frame):

```tsx
import type {PlayerRef} from "@remotion/player";
import {
  memo,
  useEffect,
  useLayoutEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type KeyboardEvent,
  type MouseEvent,
  type RefObject,
} from "react";
import type {EditorAudioPlanView} from "../audio/audioPlanView.ts";
import {
  frameFromPointer,
  playheadPercent,
  sceneBlocks,
  seekTarget,
  timelineLabel,
  timelinePlaceholders,
  type TimelineLane,
} from "../audio/audioTimeline.ts";
import {
  drawEffectsLane,
  drawMusicLane,
  type LaneContext,
} from "../audio/drawTimelineLane.ts";
import type {StoryWaveforms} from "../audio/storyWaveforms.ts";

const MUSIC_LANE_HEIGHT = 48;
const EFFECTS_LANE_HEIGHT = 64;

/** The width of an element in CSS pixels, kept current while it is resized. */
const useElementWidth = (ref: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>): number => {
  const [width, setWidth] = useState(0);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const element = ref.current;

    if (element === null) {
      return;
    }

    setWidth(Math.floor(element.getBoundingClientRect().width));

    const observer = new ResizeObserver((entries) => {
      setWidth(Math.floor(entries[0]?.contentRect.width ?? 0));
    });

    observer.observe(element);

    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [ref]);

  return width;
};

type LaneCanvasProps = {
  lane: TimelineLane;
  view: EditorAudioPlanView;
  waveforms: StoryWaveforms;
  width: number;
  height: number;
};

/**
 * One canvas per lane. It is redrawn when the Story, the plan, the waveforms or
 * the width change, never per Player frame (only the playhead moves).
 */
const LaneCanvas = ({lane, view, waveforms, width, height}: LaneCanvasProps) => {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const ratio = typeof window === "undefined" ? 1 : window.devicePixelRatio || 1;

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    const context = canvas?.getContext("2d");

    if (canvas === null || context === null || context === undefined || width < 1) {
      return;
    }

    context.setTransform(ratio, 0, 0, ratio, 0, 0);
    (lane === "music" ? drawMusicLane : drawEffectsLane)(
      context as unknown as LaneContext,
      {view, waveforms, width, height},
    );
  }, [lane, view, waveforms, width, height, ratio]);

  return (
    <canvas
      ref={canvasRef}
      className="timeline-canvas"
      width={Math.max(1, Math.round(width * ratio))}
      height={Math.max(1, Math.round(height * ratio))}
      style={{width, height}}
      aria-hidden="true"
      data-timeline-canvas={lane}
    />
  );
};

type LanesProps = {view: EditorAudioPlanView; waveforms: StoryWaveforms};

/** Everything that does not change per Player frame: scene blocks, both lanes and the placeholder blocks. */
const TimelineLanes = memo(({view, waveforms}: LanesProps) => {
  const surfaceRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const width = useElementWidth(surfaceRef);
  const placeholders = timelinePlaceholders(view);

  return (
    <div className="timeline-lanes" ref={surfaceRef}>
      <div className="timeline-scenes">
        {sceneBlocks(view.timeline).map((block) => (
          <div
            key={block.index}
            className="timeline-scene"
            style={{left: `${block.leftPct}%`, width: `${block.widthPct}%`}}
            data-timeline-scene={block.number}
          >
            <span>{block.number}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      {(["music", "effects"] as const).map((lane) => {
        const height = lane === "music" ? MUSIC_LANE_HEIGHT : EFFECTS_LANE_HEIGHT;

        return (
          <div
            key={lane}
            className={`timeline-lane timeline-lane-${lane}`}
            style={{height}}
            data-timeline-lane={lane}
          >
            <LaneCanvas
              lane={lane}
              view={view}
              waveforms={waveforms}
              width={width}
              height={height}
            />
            {placeholders
              .filter((block) => block.lane === lane)
              .map((block) => (
                <div
                  key={block.key}
                  className={`timeline-block timeline-block-${block.appearance} timeline-band-${block.band}`}
                  style={{left: `${block.leftPct}%`, width: `${block.widthPct}%`}}
                  data-timeline-placeholder={block.appearance}
                  data-timeline-role={block.role}
                >
                  <span>{block.label}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
});

TimelineLanes.displayName = "TimelineLanes";

type AudioTimelineProps = {
  view: EditorAudioPlanView;
  waveforms: StoryWaveforms;
  playerRef: RefObject<PlayerRef | null>;
};

/**
 * The read-only audio timeline under the Player: scene blocks, a music lane
 * and an effects/voice lane, a playhead synced to the Player and one focusable
 * element that seeks (click or tap, Left and Right arrows between scene
 * starts, Home and End).
 */
export const AudioTimeline = ({view, waveforms, playerRef}: AudioTimelineProps) => {
  const [frame, setFrame] = useState(0);
  const total = view.timeline.durationInFrames;
  const shown = Math.min(frame, Math.max(0, total - 1));

  useEffect(() => {
    const player = playerRef.current;

    if (player === null) {
      return;
    }

    setFrame(player.getCurrentFrame());

    const onFrame = ({detail}: {detail: {frame: number}}) => setFrame(detail.frame);

    player.addEventListener("frameupdate", onFrame);

    return () => player.removeEventListener("frameupdate", onFrame);
  }, [playerRef]);

  const seek = (target: number) => {
    playerRef.current?.seekTo(target);
    setFrame(target);
  };
  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const target = seekTarget(event.key, shown, view.timeline);

    if (target !== null) {
      event.preventDefault();
      seek(target);
    }
  };
  const onClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const rect = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();

    seek(frameFromPointer(event.clientX - rect.left, rect.width, total));
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="audio-timeline"
      tabIndex={0}
      role="slider"
      aria-label={timelineLabel(shown, view.timeline, view.fps)}
      aria-valuemin={0}
      aria-valuemax={Math.max(0, total - 1)}
      aria-valuenow={shown}
      data-audio-timeline=""
      data-audio-tracks={view.plan.tracks.map((track) => track.key).join(" ")}
      onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <TimelineLanes view={view} waveforms={waveforms} />
      <div
        className="timeline-playhead"
        style={{left: `${playheadPercent(shown, total)}%`}}
        aria-hidden="true"
        data-timeline-playhead=""
      />
    </div>
  );
};
```
