`src/web/styles.css` (append):

```css

/* AUDIO-004: the audio timeline under the Player. */
.audio-timeline {
  position: relative;
  margin: 8px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: rgba(5, 8, 13, 0.72);
  cursor: pointer;
  overflow: hidden;
  touch-action: manipulation;
}

.audio-timeline:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid #94a3b8;
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

.timeline-lanes {
  position: relative;
}

.timeline-scenes {
  position: relative;
  height: 20px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
}

.timeline-scene {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-right: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18);
  color: #cbd5e1;
  font-size: 10px;
  line-height: 20px;
  text-align: center;
}

.timeline-lane {
  position: relative;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
}

.timeline-canvas {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: block;
}

.timeline-block {
  position: absolute;
  top: 3px;
  bottom: 3px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.45);
  border-radius: 4px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  font-size: 10px;
  line-height: 14px;
}

.timeline-block span {
  padding: 0 4px;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.timeline-block-plain {
  background: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.16);
}

.timeline-block-hatched {
  border-color: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.6);
  background: repeating-linear-gradient(
    135deg,
    rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.28) 0 4px,
    transparent 4px 8px
  );
}

.timeline-band-sfx {
  bottom: 50%;
}

.timeline-band-voice {
  top: 50%;
}

.timeline-playhead {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  width: 2px;
  margin-left: -1px;
  background: #f8fafc;
  pointer-events: none;
}
```

### Task 7: Plan warnings next to the controls

**Files:**
- Create: `src/web/audio/audioNotices.ts`, `src/web/components/AudioNotices.tsx`
- Modify: `src/web/components/MusicPanel.tsx`, `src/web/components/SceneAudioFields.tsx`, `src/web/components/VisualEditor.tsx`, `src/web/App.tsx`, `src/web/styles.css`
- Test: `tests/audio-notices.test.mjs`, `tests/browser/audio-editor.spec.mjs` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `EditorAudioPlanView` (Task 6), `BundledAudioState` and `retryBundledAudio` (Task 4), `bundledAudioCatalog`, `isBundledAudio`, `AudioPlanIssue` kinds.
- Produces:
  - `src/web/audio/audioNotices.ts`: the message constants and `voiceCutMessage`, `clipMessage`; `AudioNotice = {text: string; retry?: {id: BundledAudio; label: string}}`; `SceneAudioNotices = {sfx; voice}`; `AudioFieldNotices = {music: AudioNotice[]; scenes: ReadonlyMap<number, SceneAudioNotices>}`; `NO_AUDIO_NOTICES`, `NO_SCENE_NOTICES`; `audioFieldNotices(view, bundledStates): AudioFieldNotices`.
  - `AudioNotices({notices, id, name, focusId})`: a `role="status"` block that is **always mounted** (only its text changes: a live region that appears already full is not announced reliably), with `data-audio-notices={name}` (`music`, `sfx`, `voice`). Its `id` is named by the field's select in `aria-describedby`, and its `Retry` button moves focus to the select (`focusId`) because the button disappears as soon as the sound is loading again.
  - `voiceCutMessage` rounds the cut UP to one decimal like `clipMessage` (a one-frame cut reads `0.1 s`, never `0.0 s`).
  - `VisualEditor` props gain `audioPlanView: EditorAudioPlanView`; `MusicPanel` gains `notices`; `SceneAudioFields` gains `notices`.

`tests/audio-notices.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {storyAudioDurations} from "../src/audio/storyAudio.ts";
import {compileEditorAudioPlan} from "../src/web/audio/audioPlanView.ts";
import {
  AUDIO_NOT_AVAILABLE_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_NOT_LOADED_MESSAGE,
  FADES_SCALED_MESSAGE,
  MUSIC_TOO_SHORT_MESSAGE,
  NO_AUDIO_NOTICES,
  audioFieldNotices,
  clipMessage,
  voiceCutMessage,
} from "../src/web/audio/audioNotices.ts";

const FPS = 30;
const LOCAL = `local:audio:sha256:${"d".repeat(64)}`;
const VOICE = `local:audio:sha256:${"e".repeat(64)}`;
const scene = (extra = {}) => ({
  type: "dialogue",
  pose: "formal",
  background: "office",
  text: "Hi",
  duration: 3,
  ...extra,
});
const url = (name) => ({kind: "url", url: `blob:http://localhost/${name}`});
const noticesFor = (story, {local = {}, sources = {}, bundled = new Map()} = {}) =>
  audioFieldNotices(
    compileEditorAudioPlan({
      story,
      fps: FPS,
      audioDurations: storyAudioDurations(story, local),
      audioSources: sources,
    }),
    bundled,
  );
const texts = (notices) => notices.map((notice) => notice.text);

describe("the warning texts", () => {
  it("have the copy of the spec", () => {
    assert.equal(AUDIO_NOT_AVAILABLE_MESSAGE, "Audio not available: render blocked.");
    assert.equal(AUDIO_NOT_LOADED_MESSAGE, "Audio could not be loaded.");
    assert.equal(FADES_SCALED_MESSAGE, "Fades shortened to fit the Story.");
    assert.equal(MUSIC_TOO_SHORT_MESSAGE, "This music is too short to loop (under 1 s); it plays once.");
    assert.equal(voiceCutMessage(1.2), "Voice is cut by 1.2 s.");
    assert.equal(voiceCutMessage(0.05), "Voice is cut by 0.1 s.");
    // One frame at 30 FPS is the smallest cut the plan reports: it must not read 0.0 s.
    assert.equal(voiceCutMessage(1 / 30), "Voice is cut by 0.1 s.");
    assert.equal(voiceCutMessage(0.1), "Voice is cut by 0.1 s.");
    assert.equal(voiceCutMessage(1.25), "Voice is cut by 1.3 s.");
    assert.equal(voiceCutMessage(2), "Voice is cut by 2.0 s.");
    assert.equal(clipMessage(1.4), "The mix may clip (peak gain 1.4).");
  });

  it("a clipping peak is rounded up, so it never reads 1.0", () => {
    assert.equal(clipMessage(1.0001), "The mix may clip (peak gain 1.1).");
    assert.equal(clipMessage(Math.SQRT2), "The mix may clip (peak gain 1.5).");
    assert.equal(clipMessage(2), "The mix may clip (peak gain 2.0).");
  });
});

describe("audioFieldNotices", () => {
  it("has no warnings for a Story without audio or with every sound ready", () => {
    assert.equal(noticesFor({title: "T", scenes: [scene()]}), NO_AUDIO_NOTICES);
    assert.equal(
      noticesFor(
        {title: "T", scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}})]},
        {sources: {ding: url("ding")}},
      ),
      NO_AUDIO_NOTICES,
    );
  });

  it("puts Audio not available on the field whose sound is missing, per role and scene", () => {
    const story = {
      title: "T",
      music: {src: "calm-loop"},
      scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: LOCAL}}), scene({voice: {src: VOICE}})],
    };
    const notices = noticesFor(story, {
      sources: {"calm-loop": {kind: "unavailable"}, [LOCAL]: {kind: "unavailable"}, [VOICE]: {kind: "unavailable"}},
    });

    assert.deepEqual(texts(notices.music), [AUDIO_NOT_AVAILABLE_MESSAGE]);
    assert.deepEqual(texts(notices.scenes.get(0).sfx), [AUDIO_NOT_AVAILABLE_MESSAGE]);
    assert.deepEqual(notices.scenes.get(0).voice, []);
    assert.deepEqual(texts(notices.scenes.get(1).voice), [AUDIO_NOT_AVAILABLE_MESSAGE]);
    assert.deepEqual(notices.scenes.get(1).sfx, []);
  });

  it("a sound that is only loading has no warning text; a bundled one that could not be fetched gets the text and a Retry", () => {
    const story = {title: "T", scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}}), scene({sfx: {src: "pop"}})]};
    const sources = {ding: {kind: "pending"}, pop: {kind: "pending"}};
    const loading = noticesFor(story, {
      sources,
      bundled: new Map([["ding", {kind: "loading"}], ["pop", {kind: "retrying", attempt: 2}]]),
    });

    assert.deepEqual(loading, NO_AUDIO_NOTICES);

    const failed = noticesFor(story, {
      sources,
      bundled: new Map([["ding", {kind: "fetch-failed", message: "x"}], ["pop", {kind: "retrying", attempt: 3}]]),
    });

    assert.deepEqual(failed.scenes.get(0).sfx, [
      {text: "Audio could not be loaded.", retry: {id: "ding", label: "Ding"}},
    ]);
    assert.equal(failed.scenes.has(1), false);
  });

  it("a pending local sound never gets a warning or a Retry", () => {
    const notices = noticesFor(
      {title: "T", scenes: [scene({voice: {src: VOICE}})]},
      {sources: {[VOICE]: {kind: "pending"}}},
    );

    assert.deepEqual(notices, NO_AUDIO_NOTICES);
  });

  it("warns that a voice is cut, with the plan's seconds, on that scene's voice", () => {
    const story = {title: "T", scenes: [scene({duration: 1}), scene({duration: 1, voice: {src: VOICE}})]};
    const notices = noticesFor(story, {local: {[VOICE]: 2.2}, sources: {[VOICE]: url("voice")}});

    assert.deepEqual(texts(notices.scenes.get(1).voice), ["Voice is cut by 1.2 s."]);
    assert.equal(notices.scenes.has(0), false);
    assert.deepEqual(notices.music, []);
  });

  it("a voice only a frame longer than its scene says 0.1 s, not 0.0 s", () => {
    const story = {title: "T", scenes: [scene({duration: 2.2, voice: {src: VOICE}})]};
    const notices = noticesFor(story, {local: {[VOICE]: 2.2 + 1 / 30}, sources: {[VOICE]: url("voice")}});

    assert.deepEqual(texts(notices.scenes.get(0).voice), ["Voice is cut by 0.1 s."]);
  });

  it("an auto scene is never cut", () => {
    const story = {title: "T", scenes: [scene({duration: "auto", voice: {src: VOICE}})]};

    assert.equal(noticesFor(story, {local: {[VOICE]: 2.2}, sources: {[VOICE]: url("voice")}}), NO_AUDIO_NOTICES);
  });

  it("warns on the music panel when the fades do not fit, when the music is too short to loop and when the mix may clip", () => {
    const fades = noticesFor(
      {title: "T", music: {src: LOCAL, fadeIn: 2, fadeOut: 2}, scenes: [scene({duration: 3})]},
      {local: {[LOCAL]: 10}, sources: {[LOCAL]: url("m")}},
    );

    assert.deepEqual(texts(fades.music), [FADES_SCALED_MESSAGE]);

    const short = noticesFor(
      {title: "T", music: {src: "pop"}, scenes: [scene()]},
      {sources: {pop: url("pop")}},
    );

    assert.deepEqual(texts(short.music), [MUSIC_TOO_SHORT_MESSAGE]);

    // Full-volume looping music crosses over at sqrt(2); a sound effect on top is not needed.
    const loop = noticesFor(
      {title: "T", music: {src: LOCAL}, scenes: [scene({duration: 20})]},
      {local: {[LOCAL]: 10}, sources: {[LOCAL]: url("m")}},
    );

    assert.deepEqual(texts(loop.music), ["The mix may clip (peak gain 1.5)."]);

    const quiet = noticesFor(
      {title: "T", music: {src: LOCAL, volume: 0.5}, scenes: [scene({duration: 20})]},
      {local: {[LOCAL]: 10}, sources: {[LOCAL]: url("m")}},
    );

    assert.equal(quiet, NO_AUDIO_NOTICES);
  });

  it("a Story with music and an effect at full volume that sum above 1 clips", () => {
    const story = {
      title: "T",
      music: {src: LOCAL, duck: 1},
      scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}})],
    };
    const notices = noticesFor(story, {local: {[LOCAL]: 10}, sources: {[LOCAL]: url("m"), ding: url("ding")}});

    assert.deepEqual(texts(notices.music), ["The mix may clip (peak gain 2.0)."]);
  });
});
```

Append to `tests/browser/audio-editor.spec.mjs`:

```js

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Plan warnings in the editor (AUDIO-004 task 7)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const notice = (page, text) =>
  page.getByRole("status").filter({hasText: text});
// The warning blocks are always mounted (a live region that appears already full is not announced reliably):
// tests read them by name and "no warning" means an empty block.
const noticeFor = (page, name) => page.locator(`[data-audio-notices="${name}"]`);
const sceneNotices = (page) => page.locator("[data-scene-audio] [data-audio-notices]");
const sceneYaml = ({music, tail = [], sfx, voice, durations = [2, 2]}) =>
  [
    "title: Warnings",
    ...(music === undefined ? [] : ["music:", `  src: ${music}`, ...tail]),
    "scenes:",
    ...durations.flatMap((duration, index) => [
      "  - type: dialogue",
      "    pose: formal",
      "    background: office",
      `    text: Scene ${index + 1}`,
      ...(index === 0 && sfx !== undefined ? ["    sfx:", `      src: ${sfx}`] : []),
      ...(index === 0 && voice !== undefined ? ["    voice:", `      src: ${voice}`] : []),
      `    duration: ${duration}`,
    ]),
    "",
  ].join("\n");
const importLibrary = async (page, names) => {
  const refs = [];

  for (const name of names) {
    await importInput(page).setInputFiles(upload(name));

    const label = name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/u, "");

    await expect(card(page, label)).toHaveCount(1);
    refs.push(await card(page, label).getAttribute("data-local-asset-card"));
  }

  return refs;
};

test("Voice is cut by X s appears on the scene's voice, and goes when the scene is long enough", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);

  const [voiceRef] = await importLibrary(page, ["tone-440-2.2s.wav"]);

  await applyYaml(page, sceneYaml({voice: voiceRef, durations: [1, 2]}));
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await expect(notice(page, "Voice is cut by 1.2 s.")).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(noticeFor(page, "voice")).toHaveText("Voice is cut by 1.2 s.");
  await expect(noticeFor(page, "sfx")).toHaveText("");

  await page.getByLabel("Duration (seconds)").fill("3");
  await expect(notice(page, "Voice is cut")).toHaveCount(0);
});

test("the music panel warns when fades do not fit, when the music is too short to loop and when the mix may clip", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);

  const [musicRef] = await importLibrary(page, ["tone-220-12s.wav"]);

  await applyYaml(page, sceneYaml({music: musicRef, tail: ["  fadeIn: 2", "  fadeOut: 2"], durations: [1, 2]}));
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await expect(noticeFor(page, "music")).toHaveText("Fades shortened to fit the Story.");
  await expect(music(page)).toHaveAttribute("aria-describedby", await noticeFor(page, "music").getAttribute("id"));

  // 12 s of full-volume music in a 20 s Story loops and crosses over at sqrt(2).
  await applyYaml(page, sceneYaml({music: musicRef, durations: [10, 10]}));
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await expect(noticeFor(page, "music")).toHaveText("The mix may clip (peak gain 1.5).");
  await page.getByLabel("Music volume").fill("0.5");
  await expect(noticeFor(page, "music")).toHaveText("");

  await applyYaml(page, sceneYaml({music: "pop"}));
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await expect(noticeFor(page, "music")).toHaveText("This music is too short to loop (under 1 s); it plays once.");
});

test("deleting the voice from My audio shows Audio not available on the voice field and removes its track while ding keeps playing", async ({page}) => {
  await page.addInitScript(installStartCounter);

test("an undecodable ding shows Audio not available on the sound effect field with no other interaction", async ({page}) => {

  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();

});

test("while ding is loading the field shows no text, and when every request fails it shows the load error with a Retry that works once the route is fixed", async ({page}) => {
  test.setTimeout(45_000);

  const requests = dingRequests(page);
  let broken = true;

  await page.route(DING, (route) => (broken ? route.abort("failed") : route.continue()));
  await openApp(page);
  await applyYaml(page, sceneYaml({sfx: "ding"}));
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await expect.poll(() => requests.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
  // Retrying: a plain block, no warning.
  await expect(page.locator("[data-timeline-placeholder]")).toHaveAttribute("data-timeline-placeholder", "plain");
  await expect(sceneNotices(page)).toHaveText(["", ""]);

  const retry = page.getByRole("button", {name: "Retry loading Ding"});

  await expect(retry).toBeVisible({timeout: 15_000});
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(4);
  await expect(noticeFor(page, "sfx")).toContainText("Audio could not be loaded.");
  // The select is described by its warning, so a screen reader reads both together.
  await expect(sfx(page)).toHaveAttribute("aria-describedby", await noticeFor(page, "sfx").getAttribute("id"));
  await expect(page.locator("[data-timeline-placeholder]")).toHaveAttribute("data-timeline-placeholder", "plain");

  broken = false;
  await retry.click();
  await expect(retry).toHaveCount(0);
  // The button is gone: focus went back to the field instead of falling to the page.
  await expect(sfx(page)).toBeFocused();
  await expect(sceneNotices(page)).toHaveText(["", ""]);
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-audio-tracks", "sfx-0");
  await expect.poll(() => paintedPixels(lane(page, "effects"))).toBeGreaterThan(20);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(5);
});

test("warnings are not shown while a visual draft is pending, because the plan belongs to the Active Story", async ({page}) => {
  await openApp(page);

  const [voiceRef] = await importLibrary(page, ["tone-440-2.2s.wav"]);

  await applyYaml(page, sceneYaml({voice: voiceRef, durations: [1, 2]}));
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await expect(notice(page, "Voice is cut by 1.2 s.")).toHaveCount(1);
  await music(page).selectOption("ding");
  await page.getByLabel("Fade in (seconds)").fill("11");
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "true");
  await expect(notice(page, "Voice is cut")).toHaveCount(0);
  await page.getByLabel("Fade in (seconds)").fill("1");
  await expect(appShell(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-visual-pending", "false");
  await expect(notice(page, "Voice is cut by 1.2 s.")).toHaveCount(1);
});

test("a local voice and ding both play after unmute, each from a blob: URL, with no network request for ding beyond the store's one", async ({page}) => {
  const requests = dingRequests(page);

  await page.addInitScript(installStartCounter);
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    window.__blobFetches = [];

    const original = window.fetch;

    window.fetch = function fetchSpy(input, ...rest) {
      const target = typeof input === "string" ? input : input.url;

      if (typeof target === "string" && target.startsWith("blob:")) {
        window.__blobFetches.push(target);
      }

      return original.call(this, input, ...rest);
    };
  });
  await openApp(page);

  const [voiceRef] = await importLibrary(page, ["tone-440-2.2s.wav"]);

  await applyYaml(page, sceneYaml({sfx: "ding", voice: voiceRef, durations: [6, 2]}));
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();
  await expect(timeline(page)).toHaveAttribute("data-audio-tracks", "sfx-0 voice-0");
  await unmuteAndPlay(page);
  await expect.poll(() => starts(page), {timeout: 10_000}).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  expect(requests).toHaveLength(1);
  // Each of the two tracks is read from its own blob: URL (the library's verified payload and the store's checked ding).
  expect(new Set(await page.evaluate(() => window.__blobFetches)).size).toBe(2);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The pure module and the component**

`src/web/audio/audioNotices.ts`:

```ts
import type {AudioRef, BundledAudio} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import {bundledAudioCatalog, isBundledAudio} from "../../audio/catalog.ts";
import type {AudioRole} from "../../audio/plan.ts";
import type {EditorAudioPlanView} from "./audioPlanView.ts";
import {
  BUNDLED_AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE,
  type BundledAudioState,
} from "./bundledAudio.ts";

export const AUDIO_NOT_AVAILABLE_MESSAGE = "Audio not available: render blocked.";
export const AUDIO_NOT_LOADED_MESSAGE = BUNDLED_AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE;
export const FADES_SCALED_MESSAGE = "Fades shortened to fit the Story.";
export const MUSIC_TOO_SHORT_MESSAGE =
  "This music is too short to loop (under 1 s); it plays once.";

/** The mix is only said to clip above full scale (with room for float noise). */
const CLIP_THRESHOLD = 1 + 1e-9;

/** Rounded UP to one decimal, so a voice that is cut by a frame never reads `0.0 s`. */
export const voiceCutMessage = (seconds: number): string =>
  `Voice is cut by ${(Math.ceil(seconds * 10 - 1e-9) / 10).toFixed(1)} s.`;

/** Rounded UP to one decimal, so a mix that clips never reads `1.0`. */
export const clipMessage = (peakGain: number): string =>
  `The mix may clip (peak gain ${(Math.ceil(peakGain * 10 - 1e-9) / 10).toFixed(1)}).`;

/** One line shown next to a control; `retry` adds the Retry button of a bundled sound that could not be fetched. */
export type AudioNotice = {
  text: string;
  retry?: {id: BundledAudio; label: string};
};

export type SceneAudioNotices = {sfx: AudioNotice[]; voice: AudioNotice[]};

export type AudioFieldNotices = {
  music: AudioNotice[];
  scenes: ReadonlyMap<number, SceneAudioNotices>;
};

/** No warnings at all (also what the editor shows while a visual draft is pending). */
export const NO_AUDIO_NOTICES: AudioFieldNotices = {music: [], scenes: new Map()};

/**
 * The warnings of the plan, placed by field: `Audio not available: render
 * blocked.` on the field whose sound has no usable source, `Audio could not be
 * loaded.` (with Retry) on a bundled sound whose store state is `fetch-failed`,
 * `Voice is cut by X.X s.` on the scene's voice, and the Story-level ones
 * (fades, clipping, music too short to loop) on the music panel. A sound that is
 * only loading has no text (its timeline block says so). Text comes straight
 * from `plan.issues` and `plan.maxSimultaneousGain`; nothing is recomputed.
 */
export const audioFieldNotices = (
  view: EditorAudioPlanView,
  bundled: ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioState>,
): AudioFieldNotices => {
  const {issues, maxSimultaneousGain} = view.plan;

  if (issues.length === 0 && maxSimultaneousGain <= CLIP_THRESHOLD) {
    return NO_AUDIO_NOTICES;
  }

  const music: AudioNotice[] = [];
  const scenes = new Map<number, SceneAudioNotices>();
  const place = (
    role: AudioRole,
    sceneIndex: number | null,
    notice: AudioNotice,
  ) => {
    if (role === "music" || sceneIndex === null) {
      music.push(notice);

      return;
    }

    const entry = scenes.get(sceneIndex) ?? {sfx: [], voice: []};

    entry[role].push(notice);
    scenes.set(sceneIndex, entry);
  };
  const failedBundled = (ref: AudioRef): BundledAudio | null =>
    isBundledAudio(ref) && bundled.get(ref)?.kind === "fetch-failed" ? ref : null;

  for (const issue of issues) {
    switch (issue.kind) {
      case "missing-audio":
        place(issue.role, issue.sceneIndex, {text: AUDIO_NOT_AVAILABLE_MESSAGE});
        break;
      case "pending-audio": {
        const id = failedBundled(issue.ref);

        if (id !== null) {
          place(issue.role, issue.sceneIndex, {
            text: AUDIO_NOT_LOADED_MESSAGE,
            retry: {id, label: bundledAudioCatalog[id].label},
          });
        }

        break;
      }
      case "voice-cut":
        place("voice", issue.sceneIndex, {text: voiceCutMessage(issue.seconds)});
        break;
      case "fades-scaled":
        music.push({text: FADES_SCALED_MESSAGE});
        break;
      case "music-too-short-to-loop":
        music.push({text: MUSIC_TOO_SHORT_MESSAGE});
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
  }

  if (maxSimultaneousGain > CLIP_THRESHOLD) {
    music.push({text: clipMessage(maxSimultaneousGain)});
  }

  return {music, scenes};
};

export const NO_SCENE_NOTICES: SceneAudioNotices = {sfx: [], voice: []};
```

`src/web/components/AudioNotices.tsx`:

```tsx
import type {AudioNotice} from "../audio/audioNotices.ts";
import {retryBundledAudio} from "../audio/bundledAudio.ts";

type AudioNoticesProps = {
  notices: readonly AudioNotice[];
  /** The DOM id of this block: the field's select names it in `aria-describedby`. */
  id: string;
  /** Which warnings these are, for tests: `music`, `sfx` or `voice`. */
  name: string;
  /** The DOM id of the field's select, which gets the focus back after Retry. */
  focusId: string;
};

/**
 * The warnings next to one control. The `role="status"` block is always
 * mounted and only its text changes (a live region that appears already full
 * is not reliably announced), and the field's select is described by it. A
 * `Retry` button disappears as soon as the sound is loading again, so focus
 * moves back to the select instead of falling to the page.
 */
export const AudioNotices = ({notices, id, name, focusId}: AudioNoticesProps) => (
  <div className="audio-notices" id={id} role="status" data-audio-notices={name}>
    {notices.map((notice) => (
      <p key={notice.text}>
        <span>{notice.text}</span>
        {notice.retry === undefined ? null : (
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label={`Retry loading ${notice.retry.label}`}
            onClick={() => {
              retryBundledAudio(notice.retry!.id);
              document.getElementById(focusId)?.focus();
            }}
          >
            Retry
          </button>
        )}
      </p>
    ))}
  </div>
);
```

Item 5 and item 6 add lines or sentences next to the quoted text.

1. Music panel, the `Music` select bullet. Replace:

```text
a `My audio` group (library rows by label).
```

with:

```text
a `My audio` group (every library row, no cap, read page by page from the library and sorted by label ignoring case; two rows with the same label are told apart by their duration, `label (0:02)`, and if still equal by a short id). A value the Story holds that no group offers stays selectable as one extra option (`Calm loop (not available)`, `Missing audio abcd…1234`, and `Loading audio…` while the library is opening or its first read is pending).
```

2. `Who requests` bullet. Replace:

```text
an open sound select adds the id the user previews.
```

with:

```text
no bundled-sound preview exists in this spec, so `App` is the only requester (a future bundled preview must add its id to App's set, because `requestBundledAudio` takes the whole wanted set).
```

3. `Consumers` bullet. Replace:

```text
the Player's source map and the Render button together.
```

with:

```text
the Player's source map together; AUDIO-005 adds the Render button's readiness from the same store.
```

4. the network-errors bullet, after `A retry that succeeds publishes `ready` like a first success.`. Replace:

```text
A retry that succeeds publishes `ready` like a first success.
```

with:

```text
A retry that succeeds publishes `ready` like a first success. An id that stops being requested loses its in-flight fetch, its pending retry and any non-terminal state (`ready` and `unusable` stay for the page session), so requesting it again later starts afresh.
```

5. My audio cards, the delete-wording bullet (add a bullet after it). Replace:

```text
A file used nowhere gets the plain question without a usage sentence.
```

with:

```text
A file used nowhere gets the plain question without a usage sentence.
- A missing card counts the Active Story's usage (it exists because the Active Story uses the ref); a regular card's delete warning counts the visual draft, so a pending draft is never under-counted. Starting a preview primes the one shared audio element synchronously inside the click (Safari plays only inside the gesture); an authoring lock (import, render, asset dialog) stops a running preview, because its Stop button is an authoring control.
```

6. Warnings paragraph. Replace:

```text
Budget failures come from AUDIO-005's readiness message.
```

with:

```text
Budget failures come from AUDIO-005's readiness message. The voice cut and the peak gain are rounded up to one decimal, so they never read `0.0` or `1.0`. The warnings come from the Active Story's plan and are not shown while a visual draft is pending. Each warning block is a `role="status"` region that stays mounted (only its text changes) and is named by its select's `aria-describedby`; after `Retry` focus returns to the select. A sound that is only loading has no text.
```

```markdown
## Implementation notes

- The spec body above was rewritten to match the implementation; the points a reader may trip over are the render-readiness assertions (AUDIO-005's), the missing bundled-preview request, and the Node-only "requested again" coverage.
- Pure editor helpers are in `src/web/audio/audioFields.ts`; `lastDuration` (draft memory for `Auto (from voice)`) is never part of the candidate Story and is lost when the YAML editor is opened and closed, after which unchecking uses the resolved seconds rounded to 0.1 s.
- The bundled-audio store is `src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts`: an HTTP error or a failing body read is transient like a network error; a catalog entry with `file: null` is `unusable` at once; `ready.peaks` is `{peaksPerSecond, peaks}`.
- The Player gets `audioSources` from App with `bundledAudioPolicy: "explicit"`; Remotion Studio keeps `"static-fallback"`.
- The audio timeline is inside `.preview-frame`, one `role="slider"` element; each lane is one canvas redrawn on Story/plan/waveform/width change, never per frame.
- No audio replacement dialog: "Import matching file" with a different audio file is still refused (AUDIO-003).
- **WebKit/Safari playback is not exercised by any gate** (the repository's WebKit project runs one unrelated spec): check the My audio preview by hand once, and add it to the AUDIO-006 checklist.
- Known limits, not fixed here: (1) the My audio page controller loads a card's stored peaks only together with its metadata, so a transient `getWaveform` failure keeps that card on its note glyph until the page is reloaded; (2) the timeline's canvases read `devicePixelRatio` when they render, so a browser zoom change that does not change the element's width leaves them blurry until the next resize; (3) the select's option groups are memoized but `AudioSelect` re-renders with its parent, because its change handlers are new functions on every render.
```

In `docs/audio/AUDIO-002-audio-plan-and-composition.md` make three edits: (1) replace the status line's interim wording with `Status: implemented (the Player resolves bundled sounds through AUDIO-004's bundled-audio store with the explicit policy; Studio keeps static-fallback)`; (2) in the `Who uses which policy` list, delete the last sentence of the **Player** bullet (about line 84): `Until AUDIO-004 lands, the AUDIO-002 Player uses "static-fallback" (an interim, unreleased state; AUDIO-004 task 3 switches it and removes the fallback from the Player).`; (3) in its implementation notes, replace the two bullets about `noBundledAudio` and the pinned `"static-fallback"` with: `Preview receives audioSources from App (AUDIO-004) and passes bundledAudioPolicy: "explicit"; the source test now pins "explicit".` Then run `git grep -n "Until AUDIO-004\|until AUDIO-004" docs/audio` and expect no stale sentence of that kind in AUDIO-002.
